# Stelander scripted effects.
# Sections: core; army restrictions; preparation; regions; civil war; settlements; imperial union.

# Core initialization, suspicion and leader health (COUNTRY)

# STP, after successor creation but before district transfer and focus replacement.
STP_cw_preserve_council_guarantees = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = STP_sided_with_Maksim_flag
			has_completed_focus = STP_cw_autonomy_guarantees
			45 = { is_owned_by = STP is_controlled_by = STP has_state_flag = STP_local_deal_asset }
			STS = { exists = yes }
			SRP = { exists = yes }
		}
		STS = { add_ideas = STP_cw_council_guarantees }
		SRP = { add_ideas = STP_cw_council_guarantees }
	}
}

# SRP: an actual external war grants emergency authority, never its announcement.
STP_cw_republics_war_command = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = SRP has_war_with = VAL NOT = { has_idea = SRP_emergency_command } }
		add_ideas = SRP_emergency_command
		if = {
			limit = { 43 = { is_owned_by = SRP is_controlled_by = SRP } }
			43 = { add_building_construction = { type = bunker level = 1 province = 16450 instant_build = yes } }
		}
	}
}

# STS pays private suppliers; no equipment or money is taken from the recipient.
STP_cw_fund_republican_supplies = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { STP_cw_republics_aid_available = yes check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 300 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } }
		hidden_effect = {
			subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 300 }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 300 }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		SRP = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 12000 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 30 }
			add_timed_idea = { idea = SRP_council_supply days = 42 }
		}
	}
}

# STS: consuming both promises makes late or repeated event choices harmless.
STP_cw_ratify_republican_charter = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { STP_cw_can_settle_council_guarantees = yes } }
		remove_ideas = STP_cw_council_guarantees
		add_ideas = STP_cw_republican_charter
		SRP = { remove_ideas = STP_cw_council_guarantees add_ideas = STP_cw_republican_charter }
	}
}

STP_cw_reject_republican_charter = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { STP_cw_can_settle_council_guarantees = yes } }
		remove_ideas = STP_cw_council_guarantees
		add_ideas = STP_cw_central_authority
		SRP = { remove_ideas = STP_cw_council_guarantees add_ideas = SRP_distrust_stelander }
	}
}

STP_initialize_core_mechanics = {
	STP_cw_initialize_preparation = yes
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_party_suspicion } }
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_party_suspicion
			value = 5
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_leader_health_stage } }
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_leader_health_stage
			value = 1
		}
	}

	STP_refresh_party_suspicion = yes
	STP_refresh_leader_health = yes

	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = STP_hedonism_with_no_bondaries }
		ADISCORD_STP_lock_regular_army_templates = yes
	}
	else = {
		ADISCORD_STP_unlock_regular_army_templates = yes
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_dynamic_modifier = {
					modifier = STP_party_suspicion_dynamic_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = {
			modifier = STP_party_suspicion_dynamic_modifier
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_dynamic_modifier = {
					modifier = STP_fading_father
				}
			}
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = {
			modifier = STP_fading_father
		}
	}
}

STP_refresh_party_suspicion = {
	clamp_variable = {
		var = STP_party_suspicion
		min = 0
		max = 100
	}

	# 0 suspicion -> +35% PP gain; 50 -> 0%; 100 -> -35%.
	set_variable = {
		var = STP_sus_political_power_factor
		value = STP_party_suspicion
	}
	multiply_variable = {
		var = STP_sus_political_power_factor
		value = -0.007
	}
	add_to_variable = {
		var = STP_sus_political_power_factor
		value = 0.35
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = STP_sus_political_power_factor
		min = -0.35
		max = 0.35
	}
	force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
}

# Call with temporary variable STP_party_suspicion_change.
STP_change_party_suspicion = {
	add_to_variable = {
		var = STP_party_suspicion
		value = STP_party_suspicion_change
	}

	STP_refresh_party_suspicion = yes
	STP_pc_update_suspicion_crisis = yes

	set_temp_variable = {
		var = STP_party_suspicion_change
		value = 0
	}
	if = {
		limit = { STP_cw_can_schedule_party_inspections = yes }
		STP_schedule_next_party_inspection = yes
	}
}

# 0 loyalty -> -20% PP and -5% stability; 50 -> 0; 100 -> +20% PP and +5% stability.
STP_refresh_apparatus_loyalty = {
	# After the congress the faction balance is frozen at its last state.
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_pf_initialized NOT = { STP_pf_settled = yes } } STP_pf_calculate = yes }
	clamp_variable = {
		var = STP_apparatus_loyalty
		min = 0
		max = 100
	}
	set_variable = {
		var = STP_loy_political_power_factor
		value = STP_apparatus_loyalty
	}
	multiply_variable = {
		var = STP_loy_political_power_factor
		value = 0.004
	}
	add_to_variable = {
		var = STP_loy_political_power_factor
		value = -0.20
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = STP_loy_political_power_factor
		min = -0.20
		max = 0.20
	}
	set_variable = {
		var = STP_loy_stability_factor
		value = STP_apparatus_loyalty
	}
	multiply_variable = {
		var = STP_loy_stability_factor
		value = 0.001
	}
	add_to_variable = {
		var = STP_loy_stability_factor
		value = -0.05
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = STP_loy_stability_factor
		min = -0.05
		max = 0.05
	}
	force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
}

# Call with temporary variable STP_apparatus_loyalty_change.
STP_change_apparatus_loyalty = {
	# General administrative work changes every faction's support equally.
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = STP_pf_initialized }
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_support value = STP_apparatus_loyalty_change }
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_borons_support value = STP_apparatus_loyalty_change }
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_security_support value = STP_apparatus_loyalty_change }
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_support value = STP_apparatus_loyalty_change }
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_advisers_support value = STP_apparatus_loyalty_change }
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_merchants_support value = STP_apparatus_loyalty_change }
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_radicals_support value = STP_apparatus_loyalty_change }
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_apparatus_loyalty value = STP_apparatus_loyalty_change }
	}
	STP_refresh_apparatus_loyalty = yes
	set_temp_variable = {
		var = STP_apparatus_loyalty_change
		value = 0
	}
}

# Party briefing replaces suspicion. Reopening keeps the accumulated value.
STP_cw_init_apparatus_loyalty = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = STP_sided_with_the_party_flag }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_apparatus_loyalty } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = STP_cw_loyalty_start_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				set_variable = {
					var = STP_apparatus_loyalty
					value = 40
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_party_suspicion_dynamic_modifier } }
				remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_party_suspicion_dynamic_modifier }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_apparatus_loyalty_dynamic_modifier } }
				}
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_apparatus_loyalty_dynamic_modifier }
			}
			STP_pf_initialize = yes
			STP_refresh_apparatus_loyalty = yes
		}
	}
}

STP_refresh_leader_health = {
	clamp_variable = {
		var = STP_leader_health_stage
		min = 1
		max = 5
	}

	set_variable = {
		var = STP_fading_father_stability_factor
		value = 0
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = STP_leader_health_stage
				value = 5
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = STP_ivanov_dead
		if = {
			limit = { has_character = STP_Petr_Ivanov }
			set_portraits = {
				character = STP_Petr_Ivanov
				civilian = { large = GFX_portrait_STP_Petr_Ivanov_animated }
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		clr_country_flag = STP_ivanov_dead
		if = {
			limit = { has_character = STP_Petr_Ivanov }
			set_portraits = {
				character = STP_Petr_Ivanov
				civilian = { large = GFX_portrait_STP_Petr_Ivanov }
			}
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = STP_leader_health_stage
				value = 2
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_fading_father_stability_factor
			value = -0.05
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = STP_leader_health_stage
				value = 3
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_fading_father_stability_factor
			value = -0.10
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = STP_leader_health_stage
				value = 4
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_fading_father_stability_factor
			value = -0.20
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = STP_leader_health_stage
				value = 5
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_fading_father_stability_factor
			value = -0.30
		}
	}
	force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
}

# Call with temporary variable STP_requested_health_stage.
STP_set_leader_health_stage = {
	set_variable = {
		var = STP_leader_health_stage
		value = STP_requested_health_stage
	}

	STP_refresh_leader_health = yes
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_ivanov_dead } STP_cw_begin_elections = yes }

	set_temp_variable = {
		var = STP_requested_health_stage
		value = 0
	}
}

# Starting army template restrictions (COUNTRY)

ADISCORD_STP_lock_regular_army_templates = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_template = "Police division" }
		set_division_template_lock = {
			division_template = "Police division"
			is_locked = yes
		}
		set_division_force_allow_recruiting = {
			division_template = "Police division"
			force_allow_recruiting = no
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = { has_template = "Regular army" }
		set_division_template_lock = {
			division_template = "Regular army"
			is_locked = yes
		}
		set_division_force_allow_recruiting = {
			division_template = "Regular army"
			force_allow_recruiting = no
		}
	}

	# Capital Guard remains unique.
	if = {
		limit = { has_template = "Capital Guard" }
		set_division_template_lock = {
			division_template = "Capital Guard"
			is_locked = yes
		}
		set_division_force_allow_recruiting = {
			division_template = "Capital Guard"
			force_allow_recruiting = no
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_STP_unlock_regular_army_templates = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_template = "Police division" }
		set_division_template_lock = {
			division_template = "Police division"
			is_locked = no
		}
		set_division_force_allow_recruiting = {
			division_template = "Police division"
			force_allow_recruiting = yes
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = { has_template = "Regular army" }
		set_division_template_lock = {
			division_template = "Regular army"
			is_locked = no
		}
		set_division_force_allow_recruiting = {
			division_template = "Regular army"
			force_allow_recruiting = yes
		}
	}
}

# After load_oob. Fixed-price STP assault orders retain their original composition.
STP_cw_apply_wartime_template_locks = {
	if = { limit = { tag = STP } ADISCORD_STP_unlock_regular_army_templates = yes }
	if = {
		limit = { has_template = "Capital Guard" }
		set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Capital Guard" is_locked = no }
	}
	set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Stelander Territorial Brigade" is_locked = no }
	set_division_force_allow_recruiting = { division_template = "Stelander Territorial Brigade" force_allow_recruiting = yes }
	# STP has a repeatable fixed-price order; ordinary recruitment remains available.
	if = {
		limit = { tag = STP }
		set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Stelander Assault Division" is_locked = yes }
	}
	else = { set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Stelander Assault Division" is_locked = no } }
	set_division_force_allow_recruiting = { division_template = "Stelander Assault Division" force_allow_recruiting = yes }
	if = {
		limit = { has_template = "Kefreyt Volunteer Division" }
		set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Kefreyt Volunteer Division" is_locked = yes }
		set_division_force_allow_recruiting = { division_template = "Kefreyt Volunteer Division" force_allow_recruiting = no }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_template = "Kefreyt Contract Infantry" }
		set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Kefreyt Contract Infantry" is_locked = yes }
		set_division_force_allow_recruiting = { division_template = "Kefreyt Contract Infantry" force_allow_recruiting = no }
	}
}

# Preparation calendar, cumulative modifiers and focus support (COUNTRY)

# Prewar country scope. Death opens elections; a prepared uprising starts war.
STP_cw_initialize_preparation = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_calendar_initialized } }
		set_country_flag = STP_cw_calendar_initialized
		activate_mission = ivanov_health_is_getting_worse
	}
}

STP_cw_first_health_decline = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_first_health_decline } }
		set_country_flag = STP_cw_first_health_decline
		remove_mission = ivanov_health_is_getting_worse
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_requested_health_stage value = 2 }
		STP_set_leader_health_stage = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_preparation.1 }
	}
}

STP_cw_open_preparation = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = STP_cw_started } NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_elections_finished } }
		STP_cw_initialize_focus_modifiers = yes
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_legitimacy_initialized } }
			set_country_flag = STP_cw_legitimacy_initialized
			set_power_balance = { id = STP_shabrat_election_legitimacy set_value = -0.20 }
		}
		set_country_flag = STP_party_crisis_unlocked
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_battle_for_stelander_active } }
			STP_initialize_battle_for_stelander = yes
		}
		STP_cw_init_apparatus_loyalty = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY: initialize accumulated modifiers once, before focus rewards or successor transfers.
STP_cw_initialize_focus_modifiers = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_focus_modifiers_initialized } }
		set_country_flag = STP_cw_focus_modifiers_initialized
		set_variable = { var = STP_cw_network_political_power_gain value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_cw_party_political_power_gain value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_cw_party_stability_factor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_cw_party_command_power_gain_mult value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_cw_army_org_factor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_cw_army_planning_speed value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_cw_army_org_regain value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_cw_army_supply_consumption_factor value = 0 }
		# Reserved for Shabrat's army after the split.
		set_variable = { var = STP_cw_pending_army_org_factor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_cw_pending_army_planning_speed value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_cw_pending_army_org_regain value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_cw_pending_army_supply_consumption_factor value = 0 }
	}
}

STP_cw_refresh_network_modifier = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					OR = { tag = STP tag = STS }
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = STP_cw_postwar
						has_global_flag = STP_cw_union_wars_finished
					}
				}
			}
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_cw_resistance_network_dynamic } }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_cw_resistance_network_dynamic }
	}
	force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
}

STP_cw_refresh_party_modifier = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					OR = { tag = STP tag = STS }
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = STP_cw_postwar
						has_global_flag = STP_cw_union_wars_finished
					}
				}
			}
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_cw_party_administration_dynamic } }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_cw_party_administration_dynamic }
	}
	force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
}

STP_cw_refresh_army_modifier = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					OR = { tag = STP tag = STS }
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = STP_cw_postwar
						has_global_flag = STP_cw_union_wars_finished
					}
				}
			}
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_cw_military_staff_dynamic } }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_cw_military_staff_dynamic }
	}
	force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
}

# COUNTRY: clear the resistance network after transfer or restoration of civil authority.
STP_cw_clear_resistance_modifiers = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_cw_resistance_network_dynamic } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_cw_resistance_network_dynamic }
	}
	clear_variable = STP_cw_network_political_power_gain
	clear_variable = STP_cw_pending_army_org_factor
	clear_variable = STP_cw_pending_army_planning_speed
	clear_variable = STP_cw_pending_army_org_regain
	clear_variable = STP_cw_pending_army_supply_consumption_factor
	force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
}

STP_cw_clear_political_modifiers = {
	STP_cw_clear_resistance_modifiers = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_cw_party_administration_dynamic } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_cw_party_administration_dynamic }
	}
	clear_variable = STP_cw_party_political_power_gain
	clear_variable = STP_cw_party_stability_factor
	clear_variable = STP_cw_party_command_power_gain_mult
	# Civil authority ends the succession crisis; retain Ivanov's death flag.
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_fading_father } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_fading_father }
	}
	set_variable = { var = STP_fading_father_stability_factor value = 0 }
	force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
}

# COUNTRY: close the claimants' civil-war bonuses on settlement, including restored STP.
# SRP has a separate war and keeps its own military administration.
STP_cw_clear_wartime_modifiers = {
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { tag = STP tag = STS } }
		STP_cw_clear_political_modifiers = yes
		if = {
			limit = { tag = STS }
			STP_hw_stall_clear = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_cw_military_staff_dynamic } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_cw_military_staff_dynamic }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_cw_election_mandate_dynamic } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_cw_election_mandate_dynamic }
		}
		clear_variable = STP_cw_army_org_factor
		clear_variable = STP_cw_army_planning_speed
		clear_variable = STP_cw_army_org_regain
		clear_variable = STP_cw_army_supply_consumption_factor
		clear_variable = STP_cw_mandate_org_regain
		clear_variable = STP_cw_mandate_core_defence
		clear_variable = STP_cw_mandate_training_time
		clear_variable = STP_cw_mandate_mobilization
		remove_ideas = {
			STP_cw_party_battle_spirit
			STP_cw_resistance_battle_spirit
			STP_cw_mountain_training
			STP_cw_mountain_assault_training
		}
		force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
	}
}

STP_cw_advance_health = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_first_health_decline } NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_participant } }
		STP_cw_first_health_decline = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = STP_cw_started } check_variable = { var = STP_leader_health_stage value = 5 compare = less_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_requested_health_stage value = STP_leader_health_stage }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_requested_health_stage value = 1 }
		STP_set_leader_health_stage = yes
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_leader_health_stage value = 3 compare = equals } } country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_preparation.20 days = 7 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_leader_health_stage value = 4 compare = equals } } country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_preparation.21 days = 10 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_leader_health_stage value = 3 compare = equals } has_idea = volunteer_only }
			add_ideas = limited_conscription
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_preparation.19 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = STP_ivanov_dead }
			STP_cw_begin_elections = yes
		}
		else = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_preparation.2 days = 1 } }
	}
}

# The opening deadline runs independently of focus completion.
# Each later health stage lasts seven weeks; elections have their own deadline.
STP_cw_schedule_health = {
	activate_mission = ivanov_health_is_getting_worse
	add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = ivanov_health_is_getting_worse days = -21 }
}

STP_cw_begin_elections = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = STP_ivanov_dead
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_elections_started }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = STP_cw_started }
		}
		STP_cw_open_preparation = yes
		set_country_flag = STP_cw_elections_started
		# Ivanov remains the public face until the election result replaces the presidency.
		remove_mission = ivanov_health_is_getting_worse
		set_variable = { var = STP_cw_completed_public_campaigns value = 0 }
		activate_mission = STP_cw_election_window
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_sided_with_Maksim_flag } activate_mission = STP_cw_credentials_review }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_preparation.3 }
		STP_cw_launch_northern_crisis = yes
		NOD = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.42 days = 112 } }
		log = "[STP CW] Elections opened: 140 days to the result"
	}
}

STP_cw_finish_elections = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_elections_started NOT = { has_global_flag = STP_cw_started } NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_elections_finished } }
		set_country_flag = STP_cw_elections_finished
		# Freeze the mandate before closing the BOP or distributing army reserves.
		# A disputed result favours the incumbent apparatus. Exceptional support
		# requires surviving civil and military commitments, not only the BOP threshold.
		if = {
			limit = { power_balance_value = { id = STP_shabrat_election_legitimacy value > 0.1 } }
			set_country_flag = STP_cw_shabrat_election_victory
			if = { limit = { STP_cw_limited_party_revolt_ready = yes } set_country_flag = STP_cw_limited_party_revolt }
			set_variable = { var = STP_cw_mandate_org_regain value = 0.20 }
			set_variable = { var = STP_cw_mandate_core_defence value = 0.10 }
			set_variable = { var = STP_cw_mandate_training_time value = -0.15 }
			set_variable = { var = STP_cw_mandate_mobilization value = 0.20 }
			if = {
				limit = { power_balance_value = { id = STP_shabrat_election_legitimacy value > 0.55 } }
				set_variable = { var = STP_cw_mandate_org_regain value = 0.30 }
				set_variable = { var = STP_cw_mandate_core_defence value = 0.15 }
				set_variable = { var = STP_cw_mandate_training_time value = -0.20 }
				set_variable = { var = STP_cw_mandate_mobilization value = 0.30 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_public_command_agreement STP_cw_public_command_available = yes }
				add_to_variable = { var = STP_cw_mandate_org_regain value = 0.10 }
				add_to_variable = { var = STP_cw_mandate_core_defence value = 0.05 }
			}
		}
		else = { set_country_flag = STP_cw_party_election_victory }
		clr_country_flag = STP_cw_public_command_agreement
		# The split removes missions and replaces trees; run it after the expiring
		# election mission has left the native timed-decision update.
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.10 hours = 1 }
		if = { limit = { STP_cw_can_start = no } country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_preparation.4 } }
		log = "[STP CW] Election mandate recorded; civil-war split requested"
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope. The first target allows 35 focus days + a 21-day response;
# subsequent operations use the normal 28-day clock.
# The fixed five-state pool avoids the separatist districts and daily scans.
STP_cw_schedule_opposition = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = STP
			STP_cw_preparation_open = yes
			has_country_flag = STP_battle_for_stelander_active
			has_country_flag = STP_sided_with_the_party_flag
			NOT = { has_active_mission = STP_cw_opposition_preparation }
		}
		2 = { clr_state_flag = STP_cw_opposition_target }
		3 = { clr_state_flag = STP_cw_opposition_target }
		29 = { clr_state_flag = STP_cw_opposition_target }
		46 = { clr_state_flag = STP_cw_opposition_target }
		53 = { clr_state_flag = STP_cw_opposition_target }
		random_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = { state = 2 state = 3 state = 29 state = 46 state = 53 }
				STP_region_is_operable = yes
				NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_ps_opposition_target_second }
				is_controlled_by = STP
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_administration_asset }
					NOT = { STP_region_resistance_entrenched = yes }
					AND = { OR = { state = 2 state = 29 } NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_garrison_asset } }
					AND = { OR = { state = 3 state = 46 } NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_supply_asset } }
					AND = { state = 53 NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_sabotage_asset } }
				}
			}
			set_state_flag = STP_cw_opposition_target
		}
		if = { limit = { any_owned_state = { has_state_flag = STP_cw_opposition_target } } activate_mission = STP_cw_opposition_preparation }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_first_opposition_announced } }
			set_country_flag = STP_cw_first_opposition_announced
			add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_cw_opposition_preparation days = 32 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = STP_apparatus_loyalty
				check_variable = { var = STP_apparatus_loyalty value = 70 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_cw_opposition_preparation days = 10 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = STP_apparatus_loyalty
				check_variable = { var = STP_apparatus_loyalty value = 25 compare = less_than }
			}
			add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_cw_opposition_preparation days = -7 }
		}
	}

	STP_cw_schedule_opposition_second = yes

}

# COUNTRY scope, from the announced mission. A raid clears its target; ownership,
# operation locks and the preparation phase are checked again at the callback.
STP_cw_prepare_opposition = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_cw_preparation_open = yes has_country_flag = STP_battle_for_stelander_active has_country_flag = STP_sided_with_the_party_flag }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = STP_cw_opposition_target STP_region_is_operable = yes is_controlled_by = STP }
			clr_state_flag = STP_cw_opposition_target
			# Administration must have existed before this operation to prepare arms.
			if = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_administration_asset }
				if = {
					limit = { OR = { state = 2 state = 29 } NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_garrison_asset } }
					set_state_flag = STP_resistance_garrison_asset
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { OR = { state = 3 state = 46 } NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_supply_asset } STP = { STP_cw_can_spend_1200 = yes } }
					STP = { STP_cw_spend_1200 = yes }
					set_state_flag = STP_resistance_supply_asset
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { state = 53 NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_sabotage_asset } STP = { NOT = { has_equipment = { infantry_equipment < 2400 } } } }
					STP = {
						set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_rifle_cost value = 2400 }
						STP_cw_pay_rifles = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = { STP = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_rifles_paid } }
						set_variable = { var = STP_cw_sabotage_rifles value = 2400 }
						set_state_flag = STP_resistance_sabotage_asset
					}
				}
			}
			STP = { add_power_balance_value = { id = STP_shabrat_election_legitimacy value = 0.04 } }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_region_influence_change value = 20 }
			STP_add_resistance_influence = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = STP_cw_loyalty_minus_6_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				STP = {
					set_temp_variable = { var = STP_apparatus_loyalty_change value = -6 }
					STP_change_apparatus_loyalty = yes
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { STP_region_resistance_entrenched = yes }
				if = { limit = { NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_administration_asset } } set_state_flag = STP_resistance_administration_asset }
			}
			log = "[STP preparation] Announced district operation completed in [THIS.GetName]"
		}
		# The election deadline alone triggers the split. A disrupted operation
		# still spends its announced time before the next target is selected.
		hidden_effect = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_preparation.5 days = 1 } }
		STP_cw_refresh_army_report = yes
	}

	set_country_flag = STP_ps_first_opposition_finished
	STP_cw_schedule_opposition_second = yes

}

# STATE scope. Focus and decision consumers use the same visible police action.
STP_cw_secure_party_district = {
	STP_cw_assess_search_evidence = yes
	clr_state_flag = STP_cw_opposition_target
	clr_state_flag = STP_ps_opposition_target_second
	STP_cw_return_preparation_reserves = yes
	clr_state_flag = STP_niansas_transfer_secured
	clr_state_flag = STP_resistance_administration_asset
	clr_state_flag = STP_resistance_garrison_asset
	clr_state_flag = STP_resistance_supply_asset
	clr_state_flag = STP_resistance_sabotage_asset
	clr_state_flag = STP_local_deal_asset
	set_state_flag = { flag = STP_party_counterintelligence_asset value = 1 days = 56 }
	if = { limit = { OR = { state = 2 state = 29 } } set_state_flag = STP_party_reinforced_garrison_asset }
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_region_influence_change value = 15 }
	STP_add_party_influence = yes
}

# STATE scope, before confiscation. Finding an actual network helps the party;
# an empty search discredits it. Reuse for timed inspections and player orders.
STP_cw_assess_search_evidence = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP = { STP_cw_preparation_open = yes } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_state_flag = STP_cw_opposition_target
					STP_region_has_detectable_assets = yes
					check_variable = { var = STP_cw_cached_rifles value = 0 compare = greater_than }
					check_variable = { var = STP_cw_pending_rifles value = 0 compare = greater_than }
				}
			}
			STP = {
				add_power_balance_value = { id = STP_shabrat_election_legitimacy value = -0.04 }
				set_variable = { var = STP_last_party_response value = 10 }
			}
		}
		else = {
			STP = {
				add_power_balance_value = { id = STP_shabrat_election_legitimacy value = 0.02 }
				set_variable = { var = STP_last_party_response value = 11 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY: forecast formations using the mobilisation conditions in STP_cw_start.
STP_cw_refresh_army_report = {
	set_variable = { var = STP_cw_report_party_brigades value = 16 }
	set_variable = { var = STP_cw_report_resistance_brigades value = 14 }
	# Early-uprising baseline; paid preparations are added before the mandate allocation.
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_party_reserve_ready } add_to_variable = { var = STP_cw_report_party_brigades value = 2 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_party_mobile_reserve_ready } add_to_variable = { var = STP_cw_report_party_brigades value = 2 } }
	if = {
		limit = {
			2 = {
				is_owned_by = STP
				has_state_flag = STP_party_reinforced_garrison_asset
				NOT = {
					STP_cw_region_goes_to_resistance = yes
				}
				NOT = {
					STP_cw_region_goes_to_republics = yes
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = STP_cw_report_party_brigades
			value = 2
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			29 = {
				is_owned_by = STP
				has_state_flag = STP_party_reinforced_garrison_asset
				NOT = {
					STP_cw_region_goes_to_resistance = yes
				}
				NOT = {
					STP_cw_region_goes_to_republics = yes
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = STP_cw_report_party_brigades
			value = 2
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			45 = {
				is_owned_by = STP
				has_state_flag = STP_party_reinforced_garrison_asset
				NOT = {
					STP_cw_region_goes_to_resistance = yes
				}
				NOT = {
					STP_cw_region_goes_to_republics = yes
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = STP_cw_report_party_brigades
			value = 2
		}
	}
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_abila_reserve_ready } add_to_variable = { var = STP_cw_report_resistance_brigades value = 2 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_local_recruits_ready } add_to_variable = { var = STP_cw_report_resistance_brigades value = 2 } }
	if = {
		limit = {
			2 = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						STP_cw_region_goes_to_resistance = yes
						has_state_flag = STP_resistance_garrison_asset
					}
					has_state_flag = STP_cw_garrison_evacuated
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = STP_cw_report_resistance_brigades
			value = 2
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			29 = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						STP_cw_region_goes_to_resistance = yes
						has_state_flag = STP_resistance_garrison_asset
					}
					has_state_flag = STP_cw_garrison_evacuated
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = STP_cw_report_resistance_brigades
			value = 2
		}
	}
}

# State scope. The ledger is cleared before credits, so late callbacks cannot duplicate it.
STP_cw_return_preparation_reserves = {
	STP_cw_refund_kefreyt_volunteers = yes
	# Abandoned weapons remain with the territorial owner, as local stockpiles.
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_returned_rifles value = STP_cw_cached_rifles }
	add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_returned_rifles value = STP_cw_pending_rifles }
	add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_returned_rifles value = STP_cw_sabotage_rifles }
	clear_variable = STP_cw_cached_rifles
	clear_variable = STP_cw_pending_rifles
	clear_variable = STP_cw_sabotage_rifles
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_returned_rifles value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		owner = { add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = PREV.STP_cw_returned_rifles } }
	}
}

STP_cw_launch_northern_crisis = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_active_mission = STP_cw_election_window
			NOD = {
				exists = yes has_capitulated = no is_subject = no
				NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_northern_crisis_pending }
				check_variable = { var = STP_cw_northern_campaign_status value = 1 compare = less_than }
			}
		}
		NOD = {
			set_country_flag = STP_cw_northern_crisis_pending
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_preparation.10 days = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# NOD owns the mobilization; a Stelander succession cannot cancel its orders.
# COUNTRY NOD. If Kefreyt is already mobilising against NOD, TFF remains
# inside the northern alliance but does not enter the war before Kefreyt does.
STP_cw_release_tff_to_northern_war = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = STP_cw_northern_campaign_status value = 1 compare = equals }
			has_war_with = YPR
			YPR = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no is_in_faction_with = TFF }
			TFF = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no NOT = { has_war_with = NOD } }
			OR = {
				VAL = { has_war_with = NOD }
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						AND = {
							STS = {
								exists = yes
								has_capitulated = no
								has_war_with = NOD
								has_country_leader = { character = STP_maksim_shabrat ruling_only = yes }
							}
						}
						AND = {
							VAL = { exists = yes has_country_flag = VAL_campaign_mobilizing }
							has_country_flag = VAL_campaign_target
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		TFF = {
			add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = YPR enemy = NOD hostility_reason = asked_to_join single_target_only = yes }
		}
	}
}

NOD_cw_apply_northern_exhaustion = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_capitulated = no
			OR = { has_war_with = YPR has_war_with = COF has_war_with = TFF }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = NOD_cw_northern_exhaustion_applied }
		}
		set_country_flag = NOD_cw_northern_exhaustion_applied
		# The coalition rallies either way; NOD stalls only if the push took no ground.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { NOD_cw_northern_push_succeeded = yes } }
			add_ideas = NOD_cw_stalled_army
			log = "[STP_NORTH] NOD push failed: stalled army"
		}
		else = { log = "[STP_NORTH] NOD push held ground: no stall" }
		YPR = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no has_war_with = NOD }
				add_ideas = YPR_cw_northern_resolve
			}
		}
		COF = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no has_war_with = NOD }
				add_ideas = COF_cw_northern_resolve
			}
		}
		TFF = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no has_war_with = NOD }
				add_ideas = TFF_cw_northern_resolve
			}
		}
	}
}

NOD_cw_exhaust_northern_push = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = NOD_cw_northern_offensive }
		remove_ideas = NOD_cw_northern_offensive
	}
	else = { NOD_cw_apply_northern_exhaustion = yes }
}

STP_cw_start_northern_war = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = STP_cw_northern_crisis_pending
			STP_cw_northern_targets_available = yes
			check_variable = { var = STP_cw_northern_campaign_status value = 1 compare = less_than }
		}
		clr_country_flag = STP_cw_northern_crisis_pending
		# Entry requires all three members to be factionless. TFF may remain a
		# non-belligerent member while Kefreyt's own NOD mobilisation is pending.
		YPR = {
			create_faction_from_template = { template = faction_template_ADISCORD_standard name = STP_northern_defence_alliance }
			add_to_faction = COF
			add_to_faction = TFF
			set_major = yes
			clear_variable = STP_cw_capitulation_occupier
		}
		COF = { set_major = yes clear_variable = STP_cw_capitulation_occupier }
		TFF = { set_major = yes clear_variable = STP_cw_capitulation_occupier }
		if = {
			limit = { YPR = { is_in_faction_with = COF is_in_faction_with = TFF } }
			declare_war_on = { target = YPR type = take_state_focus generator = { 20 } }
			if = {
				limit = { has_war_with = YPR }
				COF = { add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = YPR enemy = NOD hostility_reason = asked_to_join single_target_only = yes } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_war_with = YPR has_war_with = COF }
			set_variable = { var = STP_cw_northern_campaign_status value = 1 }
			set_country_flag = NOD_cw_northern_war_started
			STP_cw_release_tff_to_northern_war = yes
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = NOD_cw_northern_offensive } } add_timed_idea = { idea = NOD_cw_northern_offensive days = 85 } }
			activate_mission = NOD_cw_northern_push
			# Northern deployment can permit an uprising before the election deadline.
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.42 days = 7 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_preparation.12 days = 1 }
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.82 }
			log = "[STP_NORTH] Northern alliance opened the NOD war; TFF entry follows the Kefreyt gate"
		}
		else = {
			# Cancel the wars opened by this incomplete coalition entry.
			if = { limit = { has_war_with = YPR } white_peace = YPR }
			if = { limit = { has_war_with = COF } white_peace = COF }
			if = { limit = { has_war_with = TFF } white_peace = TFF }
			STP_cw_dismantle_northern_alliance = yes
			YPR = { set_major = no }
			COF = { set_major = no }
			TFF = { set_major = no }
			set_variable = { var = STP_cw_northern_campaign_status value = 4 }
			STP = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_preparation.14 } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_northern_crisis_pending }
		clr_country_flag = STP_cw_northern_crisis_pending
		set_variable = { var = STP_cw_northern_campaign_status value = 4 }
		STP = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_preparation.14 } }
	}
}

# Regional influence, assets and operations (STATE unless noted)

# Country-scope political-action slot accounting.
STP_political_action_slot_grant = {
	add_to_variable = { var = STP_political_action_slots_total value = 1 }
	add_to_variable = { var = STP_political_action_slots_available value = 1 }
}

STP_political_action_slot_consume = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_has_political_action_slot = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_political_action_slots_available value = -1 }
	}
}

STP_political_action_slot_release = {
	add_to_variable = { var = STP_political_action_slots_available value = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = STP_political_action_slots_available
				value = STP_political_action_slots_total
				compare = greater_than
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_political_action_slots_available
			value = STP_political_action_slots_total
		}
	}
	if = { limit = { tag = STP STP_cw_preparation_open = yes has_country_flag = STP_battle_for_stelander_active } STP_cw_refresh_army_report = yes }
}

# Battle for Stelander regional influence. All mutators execute in state scope.
# Call a mutator after setting temporary variable STP_region_influence_change.

STP_normalize_region_influence = {
	clamp_variable = { var = STP_resistance_influence min = 0 max = 100 }
	clamp_variable = { var = STP_party_influence min = 0 max = 100 }

	# Local forces are the balancing term, so the three values always total 100.
	set_variable = { var = STP_local_forces_influence value = 100 }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_local_forces_influence value = STP_resistance_influence }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_local_forces_influence value = STP_party_influence }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_local_forces_influence value = 0 compare = less_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_region_overflow value = STP_local_forces_influence }
		multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_region_overflow value = -1 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_local_forces_influence value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_party_influence value = STP_region_overflow compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_party_influence value = STP_region_overflow }
		}
		else = {
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_region_overflow value = STP_party_influence }
			set_variable = { var = STP_party_influence value = 0 }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_resistance_influence value = STP_region_overflow }
		}
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = STP_local_forces_influence min = 0 max = 100 }
	STP_calculate_region_alignment = yes
	STP = { if = { limit = { STP_cw_preparation_open = yes has_country_flag = STP_battle_for_stelander_active } STP_cw_refresh_army_report = yes } }
}

STP_add_resistance_influence = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_region_remaining_shift value = STP_region_influence_change }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = STP_region_remaining_shift min = 0 max = 100 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_party_influence value = STP_region_remaining_shift compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_party_influence value = STP_region_remaining_shift }
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_resistance_influence value = STP_region_remaining_shift }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_region_remaining_shift value = 0 }
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_resistance_influence value = STP_party_influence }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_region_remaining_shift value = STP_party_influence }
		set_variable = { var = STP_party_influence value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_local_forces_influence value = STP_region_remaining_shift compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_local_forces_influence value = STP_region_remaining_shift }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_resistance_influence value = STP_region_remaining_shift }
		}
		else = {
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_resistance_influence value = STP_local_forces_influence }
			set_variable = { var = STP_local_forces_influence value = 0 }
		}
	}
	STP_normalize_region_influence = yes
}

STP_add_party_influence = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_region_remaining_shift value = STP_region_influence_change }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = STP_region_remaining_shift min = 0 max = 100 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_resistance_influence value = STP_region_remaining_shift compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_resistance_influence value = STP_region_remaining_shift }
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_party_influence value = STP_region_remaining_shift }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_region_remaining_shift value = 0 }
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_party_influence value = STP_resistance_influence }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_region_remaining_shift value = STP_resistance_influence }
		set_variable = { var = STP_resistance_influence value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_local_forces_influence value = STP_region_remaining_shift compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_local_forces_influence value = STP_region_remaining_shift }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_party_influence value = STP_region_remaining_shift }
		}
		else = {
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_party_influence value = STP_local_forces_influence }
			set_variable = { var = STP_local_forces_influence value = 0 }
		}
	}
	STP_normalize_region_influence = yes
}

STP_add_local_forces_influence = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_region_remaining_shift value = STP_region_influence_change }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = STP_region_remaining_shift min = 0 max = 100 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_party_influence value = STP_region_remaining_shift compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_party_influence value = STP_region_remaining_shift }
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_local_forces_influence value = STP_region_remaining_shift }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_region_remaining_shift value = 0 }
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_local_forces_influence value = STP_party_influence }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_region_remaining_shift value = STP_party_influence }
		set_variable = { var = STP_party_influence value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_resistance_influence value = STP_region_remaining_shift compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_resistance_influence value = STP_region_remaining_shift }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_local_forces_influence value = STP_region_remaining_shift }
		}
		else = {
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_local_forces_influence value = STP_resistance_influence }
			set_variable = { var = STP_resistance_influence value = 0 }
		}
	}
	STP_normalize_region_influence = yes
}

STP_calculate_region_alignment = {
	set_variable = { var = STP_region_lead value = STP_resistance_influence }
	set_variable = { var = STP_region_runner_up value = STP_party_influence }
	set_variable = { var = STP_region_alignment value = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_party_influence value = STP_region_lead compare = greater_than } }
		set_variable = { var = STP_region_runner_up value = STP_region_lead }
		set_variable = { var = STP_region_lead value = STP_party_influence }
		set_variable = { var = STP_region_alignment value = 2 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_party_influence value = STP_region_runner_up compare = greater_than } }
		set_variable = { var = STP_region_runner_up value = STP_party_influence }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_local_forces_influence value = STP_region_lead compare = greater_than } }
		set_variable = { var = STP_region_runner_up value = STP_region_lead }
		set_variable = { var = STP_region_lead value = STP_local_forces_influence }
		set_variable = { var = STP_region_alignment value = 3 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_local_forces_influence value = STP_region_runner_up compare = greater_than } }
		set_variable = { var = STP_region_runner_up value = STP_local_forces_influence }
	}
	set_variable = { var = STP_region_margin value = STP_region_lead }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_region_margin value = STP_region_runner_up }
	set_variable = { var = STP_region_third value = 100 }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_region_third value = STP_region_lead }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_region_third value = STP_region_runner_up }
	set_variable = { var = STP_region_status value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = STP_region_lead value = 40 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = STP_region_margin value = 10 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_region_alignment value = 1 compare = equals } } set_variable = { var = STP_region_status value = 1 } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_region_alignment value = 2 compare = equals } } set_variable = { var = STP_region_status value = 3 } }
		else = { set_variable = { var = STP_region_status value = 5 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					check_variable = { var = STP_region_lead value = 60 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					check_variable = { var = STP_region_margin value = 20 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { var = STP_region_lead value = 55 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					check_variable = { var = STP_region_runner_up value = 40 compare = less_than_or_equals }
					check_variable = { var = STP_region_margin value = 15 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					check_variable = { var = STP_region_third value = 10 compare = less_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_region_alignment value = 1 compare = equals } } set_variable = { var = STP_region_status value = 2 } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_region_alignment value = 2 compare = equals } } set_variable = { var = STP_region_status value = 4 } }
		else = { set_variable = { var = STP_region_status value = 6 } }
	}
}

STP_mark_region_player_action = {
	set_state_flag = { flag = STP_region_recent_player_action value = 1 days = 80 }
}

STP_cw_count_live_party_inspections = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_live_inspection_count value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { 2 = { has_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active } has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_2 } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_live_inspection_count value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { 3 = { has_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active } has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_3 } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_live_inspection_count value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { 29 = { has_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active } has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_29 } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_live_inspection_count value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { 45 = { has_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active } has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_45 } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_live_inspection_count value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { 46 = { has_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active } has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_46 } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_live_inspection_count value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { 53 = { has_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active } has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_53 } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_live_inspection_count value = 1 }
	}
}

STP_cw_repair_party_inspection_markers = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_2 NOT = { 2 = { has_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active } } }
		2 = { set_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { 2 = { has_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active } NOT = { has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_2 } }
		2 = { clr_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_3 NOT = { 3 = { has_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active } } }
		3 = { set_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { 3 = { has_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active } NOT = { has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_3 } }
		3 = { clr_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_29 NOT = { 29 = { has_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active } } }
		29 = { set_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { 29 = { has_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active } NOT = { has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_29 } }
		29 = { clr_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_45 NOT = { 45 = { has_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active } } }
		45 = { set_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { 45 = { has_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active } NOT = { has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_45 } }
		45 = { clr_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_46 NOT = { 46 = { has_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active } } }
		46 = { set_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { 46 = { has_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active } NOT = { has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_46 } }
		46 = { clr_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_53 NOT = { 53 = { has_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active } } }
		53 = { set_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { 53 = { has_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active } NOT = { has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_53 } }
		53 = { clr_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active }
	}
}

# STATE: drop a surplus commission without applying a completed inspection.
STP_cw_abort_party_inspection = {
	clr_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active
	if = { limit = { state = 2 } ROOT = { remove_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_2 } }
	else_if = { limit = { state = 3 } ROOT = { remove_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_3 } }
	else_if = { limit = { state = 29 } ROOT = { remove_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_29 } }
	else_if = { limit = { state = 45 } ROOT = { remove_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_45 } }
	else_if = { limit = { state = 46 } ROOT = { remove_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_46 } }
	else_if = { limit = { state = 53 } ROOT = { remove_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_53 } }
}

# Caller sets STP_cw_inspection_candidate_state and STP_cw_inspection_candidate_days.
STP_cw_consider_inspection_keeper = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = STP_cw_keep_inspection_a value = 0 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = STP_cw_inspection_candidate_days value = STP_cw_keep_inspection_days_a compare = less_than }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_keep_inspection_b value = STP_cw_keep_inspection_a }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_keep_inspection_days_b value = STP_cw_keep_inspection_days_a }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_keep_inspection_a value = STP_cw_inspection_candidate_state }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_keep_inspection_days_a value = STP_cw_inspection_candidate_days }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = STP_cw_keep_inspection_b value = 0 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = STP_cw_inspection_candidate_days value = STP_cw_keep_inspection_days_b compare = less_than }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_keep_inspection_b value = STP_cw_inspection_candidate_state }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_keep_inspection_days_b value = STP_cw_inspection_candidate_days }
	}
}

STP_cw_trim_excess_party_inspections = {
	STP_cw_count_live_party_inspections = yes
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_inspection_target value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_party_suspicion value = 60 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_inspection_target value = 2 }
	}
	else = { set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_inspection_target value = 1 } }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_live_inspection_count value = STP_cw_inspection_target compare = greater_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_keep_inspection_a value = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_keep_inspection_days_a value = 999 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_keep_inspection_b value = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_keep_inspection_days_b value = 999 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_inspection_candidate_state value = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_inspection_candidate_days value = days_mission_timeout@STP_party_inspection_state_2 }
			STP_cw_consider_inspection_keeper = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_3 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_inspection_candidate_state value = 3 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_inspection_candidate_days value = days_mission_timeout@STP_party_inspection_state_3 }
			STP_cw_consider_inspection_keeper = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_29 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_inspection_candidate_state value = 29 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_inspection_candidate_days value = days_mission_timeout@STP_party_inspection_state_29 }
			STP_cw_consider_inspection_keeper = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_45 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_inspection_candidate_state value = 45 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_inspection_candidate_days value = days_mission_timeout@STP_party_inspection_state_45 }
			STP_cw_consider_inspection_keeper = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_46 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_inspection_candidate_state value = 46 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_inspection_candidate_days value = days_mission_timeout@STP_party_inspection_state_46 }
			STP_cw_consider_inspection_keeper = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_53 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_inspection_candidate_state value = 53 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_inspection_candidate_days value = days_mission_timeout@STP_party_inspection_state_53 }
			STP_cw_consider_inspection_keeper = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_inspection_target value = 1 compare = less_than_or_equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_keep_inspection_b value = 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_2
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_keep_inspection_a value = 2 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_keep_inspection_b value = 2 compare = equals } }
			}
			2 = { STP_cw_abort_party_inspection = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_3
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_keep_inspection_a value = 3 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_keep_inspection_b value = 3 compare = equals } }
			}
			3 = { STP_cw_abort_party_inspection = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_29
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_keep_inspection_a value = 29 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_keep_inspection_b value = 29 compare = equals } }
			}
			29 = { STP_cw_abort_party_inspection = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_45
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_keep_inspection_a value = 45 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_keep_inspection_b value = 45 compare = equals } }
			}
			45 = { STP_cw_abort_party_inspection = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_46
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_keep_inspection_a value = 46 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_keep_inspection_b value = 46 compare = equals } }
			}
			46 = { STP_cw_abort_party_inspection = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_53
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_keep_inspection_a value = 53 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_keep_inspection_b value = 53 compare = equals } }
			}
			53 = { STP_cw_abort_party_inspection = yes }
		}
	}
}

STP_schedule_next_party_inspection = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_cw_can_schedule_party_inspections = yes }
		STP_cw_repair_party_inspection_markers = yes
		STP_cw_trim_excess_party_inspections = yes
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { STP_cw_any_inspection_active = yes } }
			STP_cw_open_one_party_inspection = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { STP_cw_second_inspection_unlocked = yes }
			STP_cw_open_one_party_inspection = yes
		}
	}
}

# Pace applies only to the mission opened here. A running second commission keeps its own deadline.
STP_cw_open_one_party_inspection = {
	STP_cw_count_live_party_inspections = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_cw_can_schedule_party_inspections = yes
			STP_cw_has_openable_inspection_region = yes
			OR = {
				AND = {
					check_variable = { var = STP_party_suspicion value = 60 compare = less_than }
					check_variable = { var = STP_cw_live_inspection_count value = 1 compare = less_than }
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { var = STP_party_suspicion value = 60 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					check_variable = { var = STP_cw_live_inspection_count value = 2 compare = less_than }
				}
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { var = STP_last_inspection_state value = 2 compare = equals } STP_cw_has_other_openable_inspection = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 0 2 = { NOT = { STP_cw_inspection_region_openable = yes } } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 2 = { has_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_2 }
				modifier = { factor = 3 2 = { STP_region_resistance_strongest = yes } }
				modifier = { factor = 2 2 = { has_state_flag = STP_region_recent_player_action } }
				modifier = { factor = 2 2 = { STP_region_has_detectable_assets = yes } }
				2 = { set_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active }
				activate_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_2
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_last_inspection_state value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
					if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_party_suspicion value = 50 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_party_inspection_state_2 days = -16 } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_party_suspicion value = 25 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_party_inspection_state_2 days = -8 } }
				}
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { var = STP_last_inspection_state value = 3 compare = equals } STP_cw_has_other_openable_inspection = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 0 3 = { NOT = { STP_cw_inspection_region_openable = yes } } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 3 = { has_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_3 }
				modifier = { factor = 3 3 = { STP_region_resistance_strongest = yes } }
				modifier = { factor = 2 3 = { has_state_flag = STP_region_recent_player_action } }
				modifier = { factor = 2 3 = { STP_region_has_detectable_assets = yes } }
				3 = { set_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active }
				activate_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_3
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_last_inspection_state value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
					if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_party_suspicion value = 50 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_party_inspection_state_3 days = -16 } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_party_suspicion value = 25 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_party_inspection_state_3 days = -8 } }
				}
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { var = STP_last_inspection_state value = 29 compare = equals } STP_cw_has_other_openable_inspection = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 0 29 = { NOT = { STP_cw_inspection_region_openable = yes } } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 29 = { has_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_29 }
				modifier = { factor = 3 29 = { STP_region_resistance_strongest = yes } }
				modifier = { factor = 2 29 = { has_state_flag = STP_region_recent_player_action } }
				modifier = { factor = 2 29 = { STP_region_has_detectable_assets = yes } }
				29 = { set_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active }
				activate_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_29
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_last_inspection_state value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
					if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_party_suspicion value = 50 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_party_inspection_state_29 days = -16 } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_party_suspicion value = 25 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_party_inspection_state_29 days = -8 } }
				}
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { var = STP_last_inspection_state value = 45 compare = equals } STP_cw_has_other_openable_inspection = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 0 45 = { NOT = { STP_cw_inspection_region_openable = yes } } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 45 = { has_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_45 }
				modifier = { factor = 4 45 = { STP_region_resistance_strongest = yes } }
				modifier = { factor = 2 45 = { has_state_flag = STP_region_recent_player_action } }
				modifier = { factor = 2 45 = { STP_region_has_detectable_assets = yes } }
				45 = { set_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active }
				activate_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_45
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_last_inspection_state value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
					if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_party_suspicion value = 50 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_party_inspection_state_45 days = -16 } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_party_suspicion value = 25 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_party_inspection_state_45 days = -8 } }
				}
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { var = STP_last_inspection_state value = 46 compare = equals } STP_cw_has_other_openable_inspection = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 0 46 = { NOT = { STP_cw_inspection_region_openable = yes } } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 46 = { has_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_46 }
				modifier = { factor = 3 46 = { STP_region_resistance_strongest = yes } }
				modifier = { factor = 2 46 = { has_state_flag = STP_region_recent_player_action } }
				modifier = { factor = 2 46 = { STP_region_has_detectable_assets = yes } }
				46 = { set_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active }
				activate_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_46
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_last_inspection_state value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
					if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_party_suspicion value = 50 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_party_inspection_state_46 days = -16 } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_party_suspicion value = 25 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_party_inspection_state_46 days = -8 } }
				}
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { var = STP_last_inspection_state value = 53 compare = equals } STP_cw_has_other_openable_inspection = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 0 53 = { NOT = { STP_cw_inspection_region_openable = yes } } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 53 = { has_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_active_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_53 }
				modifier = { factor = 3 53 = { STP_region_resistance_strongest = yes } }
				modifier = { factor = 2 53 = { has_state_flag = STP_region_recent_player_action } }
				modifier = { factor = 2 53 = { STP_region_has_detectable_assets = yes } }
				53 = { set_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active }
				activate_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_53
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_last_inspection_state value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
					if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_party_suspicion value = 50 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_party_inspection_state_53 days = -16 } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_party_suspicion value = 25 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_party_inspection_state_53 days = -8 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

STP_false_trail_clean_redirect = {
	if = { limit = { STP = { STP_cw_preparation_open = yes } } STP = { add_power_balance_value = { id = STP_shabrat_election_legitimacy value = 0.04 } } }
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_region_influence_change value = 4 }
	STP_add_local_forces_influence = yes
	ROOT = {
		set_variable = { var = STP_last_party_response value = 1 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_party_suspicion_change value = -4 }
		STP_change_party_suspicion = yes
	}
}

STP_false_trail_noisy_redirect = {
	if = { limit = { STP = { STP_cw_preparation_open = yes } } STP = { add_power_balance_value = { id = STP_shabrat_election_legitimacy value = 0.01 } } }
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_region_influence_change value = 2 }
	STP_add_party_influence = yes
	ROOT = {
		set_variable = { var = STP_last_party_response value = 2 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_party_suspicion_change value = 2 }
		STP_change_party_suspicion = yes
	}
}

STP_false_trail_courier_seized = {
	if = { limit = { STP = { STP_cw_preparation_open = yes } } STP = { add_power_balance_value = { id = STP_shabrat_election_legitimacy value = -0.03 } } }
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_region_influence_change value = 6 }
	STP_add_party_influence = yes
	clr_state_flag = STP_niansas_transfer_secured
	clr_state_flag = STP_resistance_administration_asset
	ROOT = {
		set_variable = { var = STP_last_party_response value = 3 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_party_suspicion_change value = 8 }
		STP_change_party_suspicion = yes
	}
}

STP_false_trail_counterintelligence_breakthrough = {
	if = { limit = { STP = { STP_cw_preparation_open = yes } } STP = { add_power_balance_value = { id = STP_shabrat_election_legitimacy value = -0.06 } } }
	STP_cw_return_preparation_reserves = yes
	clr_state_flag = STP_resistance_sabotage_asset
	clr_state_flag = STP_resistance_supply_asset
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { AND = { state = 45 STP = { has_completed_focus = STP_cw_autonomy_guarantees } } } }
		clr_state_flag = STP_local_deal_asset
	}
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_region_influence_change value = 10 }
	STP_add_party_influence = yes
	set_state_flag = { flag = STP_party_counterintelligence_asset value = 1 days = 56 }
	clr_state_flag = STP_niansas_transfer_secured
	clr_state_flag = STP_resistance_administration_asset
	clr_state_flag = STP_resistance_garrison_asset
	ROOT = {
		set_variable = { var = STP_last_party_response value = 4 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_party_suspicion_change value = 12 }
		STP_change_party_suspicion = yes
	}
}

STP_resolve_false_trail = {
	# Save-compatibility fallback for trails prepared before immediate diversion.
	STP_false_trail_clean_redirect = yes
	set_state_flag = { flag = STP_false_trail_redirected days = 28 }
}

STP_resolve_party_inspection = {
	ROOT = { set_country_flag = STP_cw_inspection_resolving }
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_inspection_found_assets value = 0 }
	clr_state_flag = STP_cw_garrison_evacuation_in_progress
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_false_trail_prepared } NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_inspection_concession_prepared } }
		STP_cw_assess_search_evidence = yes
		if = {
			limit = { STP_region_has_detectable_assets = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_inspection_found_assets value = 1 }
			set_state_flag = { flag = STP_party_counterintelligence_asset value = 1 days = 56 }
		}
		STP_cw_return_preparation_reserves = yes
		clr_state_flag = STP_resistance_sabotage_asset
		clr_state_flag = STP_resistance_garrison_asset
		clr_state_flag = STP_resistance_supply_asset
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = STP_inspection_concession_prepared }
		# The chosen concession has already surrendered either civil or military assets.
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_administration_asset has_state_flag = STP_niansas_transfer_secured } }
			ROOT = { set_variable = { var = STP_last_party_response value = 12 } }
		}
		else = { ROOT = { set_variable = { var = STP_last_party_response value = 9 } } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = STP_false_trail_prepared }
		STP_resolve_false_trail = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { OR = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_administration_asset has_state_flag = STP_niansas_transfer_secured } }
		clr_state_flag = STP_niansas_transfer_secured
		clr_state_flag = STP_resistance_administration_asset
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_region_influence_change value = 8 }
		STP_add_party_influence = yes
		ROOT = {
			set_variable = { var = STP_last_party_response value = 5 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_party_suspicion_change value = 6 }
			STP_change_party_suspicion = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = STP_local_deal_asset NOT = { AND = { state = 45 STP = { has_completed_focus = STP_cw_autonomy_guarantees } } } }
		clr_state_flag = STP_local_deal_asset
		set_state_flag = STP_party_reinforced_garrison_asset
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_region_influence_change value = 8 }
		STP_add_party_influence = yes
		ROOT = {
			set_variable = { var = STP_last_party_response value = 6 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_party_suspicion_change value = 4 }
			STP_change_party_suspicion = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { STP_region_resistance_strongest = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_region_influence_change value = 10 }
		STP_add_party_influence = yes
		ROOT = {
			set_variable = { var = STP_last_party_response value = 7 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_party_suspicion_change value = 5 }
			STP_change_party_suspicion = yes
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_region_influence_change value = 6 }
		STP_add_party_influence = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_inspection_found_assets value = 1 compare = equals } }
			ROOT = { set_variable = { var = STP_last_party_response value = 10 } }
		}
		else = { ROOT = { set_variable = { var = STP_last_party_response value = 8 } } }
		ROOT = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_party_suspicion_change value = 3 }
			STP_change_party_suspicion = yes
		}
	}
	clr_state_flag = STP_false_trail_prepared
	clr_state_flag = STP_inspection_concession_prepared
	clr_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active
	ROOT = { clr_country_flag = STP_cw_inspection_resolving }
	STP = { if = { limit = { STP_cw_preparation_open = yes has_country_flag = STP_battle_for_stelander_active } STP_cw_refresh_army_report = yes } }
}

STP_initialize_battle_for_stelander = {

	set_variable = { var = STP_last_party_response value = 0 }

	1 = {

		set_variable = { var = STP_party_influence value = 35 }

		STP_calculate_region_alignment = yes
