STP_clear_region_runtime = {
	clr_state_flag = STP_cw_district_operation_in_progress
	clr_state_flag = STP_cw_opposition_target
	clr_state_flag = STP_ps_opposition_target_second
	clr_state_flag = STP_cw_garrison_evacuated
	clr_state_flag = STP_cw_garrison_evacuation_in_progress
	clr_state_flag = STP_false_trail_in_progress
	clr_state_flag = STP_false_trail_redirected
	clr_state_flag = STP_inspection_concession_prepared
	STP_cw_return_preparation_reserves = yes
	clr_state_flag = STP_region_participates
	clr_state_flag = STP_region_player_operations_allowed
	clr_state_flag = STP_region_recent_player_action
	clr_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active
	clr_state_flag = STP_cw_inspection_delay_escrow
	clr_state_flag = STP_false_trail_prepared
	clr_state_flag = STP_niansas_transfer_secured
	clr_state_flag = STP_resistance_administration_asset
	clr_state_flag = STP_resistance_garrison_asset
	clr_state_flag = STP_resistance_supply_asset
	clr_state_flag = STP_resistance_sabotage_asset
	clr_state_flag = STP_local_deal_asset
	clr_state_flag = STP_party_reinforced_garrison_asset
	clr_state_flag = STP_party_counterintelligence_asset
	clear_variable = STP_resistance_influence
	clear_variable = STP_party_influence
	clear_variable = STP_local_forces_influence
	clear_variable = STP_region_lead
	clear_variable = STP_region_runner_up
	clear_variable = STP_region_margin
	clear_variable = STP_region_third
	clear_variable = STP_region_alignment
	clear_variable = STP_region_status
}

STP_end_battle_for_stelander = {
	clr_country_flag = STP_battle_for_stelander_active
	clr_country_flag = STP_cw_inspection_chain_open
	clr_country_flag = STP_cw_inspection_resolving
	# Keep the side briefing and BOP while elections and preparation are still active.
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { has_global_flag = STP_cw_started has_country_flag = STP_cw_elections_finished } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_party_suspicion_dynamic_modifier } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_party_suspicion_dynamic_modifier }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { STP_pf_active = yes } has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_apparatus_loyalty_dynamic_modifier } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_apparatus_loyalty_dynamic_modifier }
		}
		remove_power_balance = { id = STP_shabrat_election_legitimacy }
		clear_variable = STP_sus_political_power_factor
		if = { limit = { NOT = { STP_pf_active = yes } } clear_variable = STP_loy_political_power_factor clear_variable = STP_loy_stability_factor }
		force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
	}
	remove_mission = STP_cw_opposition_preparation
	remove_mission = STP_ps_opposition_preparation_second
	clr_country_flag = STP_regional_operation_active
	remove_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_2
	remove_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_3
	remove_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_29
	remove_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_45
	remove_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_46
	remove_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_53
	1 = { STP_clear_region_runtime = yes }
	2 = { STP_clear_region_runtime = yes }
	3 = { STP_clear_region_runtime = yes }
	28 = { STP_clear_region_runtime = yes }
	29 = { STP_clear_region_runtime = yes }
	43 = { STP_clear_region_runtime = yes }
	44 = { STP_clear_region_runtime = yes }
	45 = { STP_clear_region_runtime = yes }
	46 = { STP_clear_region_runtime = yes }
	53 = { STP_clear_region_runtime = yes }
	88 = { STP_clear_region_runtime = yes }
	clear_variable = STP_last_party_response
	clear_variable = STP_last_inspection_state
	clear_variable = STP_political_action_slots_total
	clear_variable = STP_political_action_slots_available
}

# Civil-war split, paid reserves, intervention and settlements (COUNTRY unless noted)

# COUNTRY: caller sets positive temp STP_cw_rifle_cost.
# Debit each producer separately; an unspecified producer repeats the debit across mixed stocks.
STP_cw_pay_rifles = {
	clr_country_flag = STP_cw_rifles_paid
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_rifles_remaining value = STP_cw_rifle_cost }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = STP_cw_rifle_cost value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@infantry_equipment value = STP_cw_rifle_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		every_possible_country = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_rifles_remaining value = 0 compare = greater_than } } }
			# PREV enters the payer; inside it PREV is this iteration's producer.
			PREV = {
				# Recheck the unpaid balance after each producer debit.
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_rifles_remaining value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
					set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_rifles_removed value = num_equipment@infantry_equipment }
					set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_rifle_debit value = STP_cw_rifles_remaining }
					multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_rifle_debit value = -1 }
					add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = STP_cw_rifle_debit producer = PREV }
					subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_rifles_removed value = num_equipment@infantry_equipment }
					subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_rifles_remaining value = STP_cw_rifles_removed }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_rifles_remaining value = 0 compare = equals } }
			set_country_flag = STP_cw_rifles_paid
			log = "[STP_CW] [THIS.GetTag] exact producer-aware rifle payment=[?STP_cw_rifle_cost]"
		}
		else = { log = "[STP_CW] [THIS.GetTag] rifle payment incomplete, no asset created; remaining=[?STP_cw_rifles_remaining]" }
	}
}

# COUNTRY: return any unfinished NOD-rear rifle escrow to the caller.
STP_cw_refund_nod_sabotage_rifles = {

			limit = {

# COUNTRY scope. Six ADISCORD_territorial: 6 * (1000 manpower + 900 rifles).
# create_unit supplies its own contents: debit the real pools first.
STP_cw_ensure_rear_cell_template = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_template = "Stelander Underground Cell" } }
		division_template = {
			name = "Stelander Underground Cell"
			template_counter = 12
			is_locked = yes
			force_allow_recruiting = no
			regiments = {
				ADISCORD_territorial = { x = 0 y = 0 }
				ADISCORD_territorial = { x = 0 y = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
}

STP_cw_raise_rear_cell = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = STS has_war_with = STP has_capitulated = no
			has_completed_focus = STP_cw_organize_underground
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_postwar }
			NOT = { has_manpower < 2000 }
			NOT = { has_equipment = { infantry_equipment < 1800 } }
			NOT = { has_political_power < 35 }
			FROM = { is_owned_by = STP is_controlled_by = STP NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_cw_rear_cell_raised } }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_rifle_cost value = 1800 }
		STP_cw_pay_rifles = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_rifles_paid }
			add_manpower = -2000
			add_political_power = -35
			STP_cw_ensure_rear_cell_template = yes
			FROM = {
				set_state_flag = STP_cw_rear_cell_raised
				create_unit = {
					division = "division_template = \"Stelander Underground Cell\" start_experience_factor = 0.2 start_equipment_factor = 1.0 start_manpower_factor = 1.0"
					owner = STS
					allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# STS volunteers use local arms networks but still consume real recruits.
STP_cw_mobilize_volunteer_brigade = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = STS
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_postwar }
			STP_cw_can_rearm = yes
			NOT = { has_manpower < 6000 }
			any_owned_state = { is_controlled_by = PREV }
		}
		add_manpower = -6000
		if = {
			limit = { any_owned_state = { is_controlled_by = PREV STP_cw_state_faces_rival = yes } }
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = { STP_cw_state_faces_rival = yes }
				STP_cw_create_territorial_brigade = yes
			}
		}
		else = { random_owned_controlled_state = { STP_cw_create_territorial_brigade = yes } }
	}
}

STP_cw_mobilize_brigade = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_owned_state = { is_controlled_by = PREV }
			NOT = { has_manpower < 6000 }
			NOT = { has_equipment = { infantry_equipment < 5400 } }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_rifle_cost value = 5400 }
		STP_cw_pay_rifles = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_rifles_paid }
			add_manpower = -6000
			# Keep a capital garrison; later brigades go to the actual front.
			# The fallback also permits wartime mobilisation after losing the capital.
			if = {
				limit = { num_divisions < 3 capital_scope = { is_owned_by = PREV is_controlled_by = PREV } }
				capital_scope = { STP_cw_create_territorial_brigade = yes }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { any_owned_state = { is_controlled_by = PREV STP_cw_state_faces_rival = yes } }
				random_owned_controlled_state = {
					limit = { STP_cw_state_faces_rival = yes }
					STP_cw_create_territorial_brigade = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				random_owned_controlled_state = { STP_cw_create_territorial_brigade = yes }
			}
			log = "[STP_CW] [THIS.GetTag] brigade paid: 6000 manpower, 5400 rifles; remaining manpower=[?manpower] rifles=[?num_equipment@infantry_equipment]"
		}
	}
}

# STATE: owner is PREV. Used only after the brigade has been paid.
STP_cw_create_territorial_brigade = {
	create_unit = {
		division = "division_template = \"Stelander Territorial Brigade\" start_experience_factor = 0.1 start_equipment_factor = 1.0 start_manpower_factor = 1.0"
		owner = PREV
		allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
	}
}


# COUNTRY: equipment is debited across producers; a partial package is refunded
# before returning. The escrow is a paid division, not an extra recruitment pool.
STP_cw_pay_assault_division = {
	clr_country_flag = STP_cw_assault_division_paid
	if = {
		limit = { STP_cw_can_pay_assault_division = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_infantry_equipment value = 7110 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment value = 48 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_support_equipment value = 30 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_artillery_equipment value = 60 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_anti_air_equipment value = 20 }
		every_possible_country = {
			limit = { PREV = { OR = {
						check_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_infantry_equipment value = 0 compare = greater_than }
						check_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment value = 0 compare = greater_than }
						check_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_support_equipment value = 0 compare = greater_than }
						check_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_artillery_equipment value = 0 compare = greater_than }
						check_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_anti_air_equipment value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			} } }
			PREV = {
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_infantry_equipment value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
					set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_before value = num_equipment@infantry_equipment }
					set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_debit value = STP_cw_payment_infantry_equipment }
					multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_debit value = -1 }
					add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = STP_cw_payment_debit producer = PREV }
					subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_before value = num_equipment@infantry_equipment }
					subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_infantry_equipment value = STP_cw_payment_before }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
					set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_before value = num_equipment@ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment }
					set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_debit value = STP_cw_payment_ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment }
					multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_debit value = -1 }
					add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment amount = STP_cw_payment_debit producer = PREV }
					subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_before value = num_equipment@ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment }
					subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment value = STP_cw_payment_before }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_support_equipment value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
					set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_before value = num_equipment@support_equipment }
					set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_debit value = STP_cw_payment_support_equipment }
					multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_debit value = -1 }
					add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = STP_cw_payment_debit producer = PREV }
					subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_before value = num_equipment@support_equipment }
					subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_support_equipment value = STP_cw_payment_before }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_artillery_equipment value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
					set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_before value = num_equipment@artillery_equipment }
					set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_debit value = STP_cw_payment_artillery_equipment }
					multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_debit value = -1 }
					add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment amount = STP_cw_payment_debit producer = PREV }
					subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_before value = num_equipment@artillery_equipment }
					subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_artillery_equipment value = STP_cw_payment_before }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_anti_air_equipment value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
					set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_before value = num_equipment@anti_air_equipment }
					set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_debit value = STP_cw_payment_anti_air_equipment }
					multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_debit value = -1 }
					add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = anti_air_equipment amount = STP_cw_payment_debit producer = PREV }
					subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_before value = num_equipment@anti_air_equipment }
					subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_anti_air_equipment value = STP_cw_payment_before }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_infantry_equipment value = 0 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment value = 0 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_support_equipment value = 0 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_artillery_equipment value = 0 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_anti_air_equipment value = 0 compare = equals }
			}
			add_manpower = -7900
			set_country_flag = STP_cw_assault_division_paid
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_refund value = 7110 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_refund value = STP_cw_payment_infantry_equipment }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = STP_cw_payment_refund }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_refund value = 48 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_refund value = STP_cw_payment_ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment amount = STP_cw_payment_refund }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_refund value = 30 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_refund value = STP_cw_payment_support_equipment }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = STP_cw_payment_refund }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_refund value = 60 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_refund value = STP_cw_payment_artillery_equipment }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment amount = STP_cw_payment_refund }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_refund value = 20 }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_payment_refund value = STP_cw_payment_anti_air_equipment }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = anti_air_equipment amount = STP_cw_payment_refund }
		}
	}
}

# Cancel only an outstanding training receipt, including during the split.
STP_cw_cancel_assault_training = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_assault_training_reserved }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_cash_refund value = 500 }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_cw_assault_training_deposit } set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_cash_refund value = STP_cw_assault_training_deposit } }
		clear_variable = STP_cw_assault_training_deposit
		clr_country_flag = STP_cw_assault_training_reserved
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = STP_cw_cash_refund }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = STP_cw_cash_refund }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		STP_political_action_slot_release = yes
	}
}

STP_cw_refund_assault_division = {
	add_manpower = 7900
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 7110 }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment amount = 48 }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 30 }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment amount = 60 }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = anti_air_equipment amount = 20 }
}

# COUNTRY (VAL): state 1 holds paid formation counts outside shared STP reserves.
STP_cw_reserve_kefreyt_volunteers = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_cw_kefreyt_aid_open = yes
			has_country_flag = VAL_cw_volunteers_pending
			STP_cw_can_fund_kefreyt_volunteers = yes
			OR = {
				AND = { NOT = { has_global_flag = STP_cw_started } 1 = { is_owned_by = STP is_controlled_by = STP } }
				AND = { has_global_flag = STP_cw_started STS = { has_war_with = STP has_country_flag = STP_cw_templates_loaded } 1 = { is_owned_by = STS is_controlled_by = STS } }
			}
		}
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = 3
			value = STP_cw_formation_index
			STP_cw_pay_assault_division = yes
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_assault_division_paid }
				clr_country_flag = STP_cw_assault_division_paid
				1 = { add_to_variable = { var = STP_cw_kefreyt_volunteers value = 1 } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { 1 = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_kefreyt_volunteers value = 3 compare = equals } } }
			clr_country_flag = VAL_cw_volunteers_pending
			set_country_flag = VAL_cw_supported_resistance
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = STP_cw_started }
				STS = { set_country_flag = STP_cw_kefreyt_shipment_received country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_preparation.8 } }
				1 = { STP_cw_deploy_kefreyt_volunteers = yes }
			}
			else = {
				STP = { set_country_flag = STP_cw_kefreyt_shipment_received country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_preparation.8 } }
			}
		}
		else = {
			1 = { STP_cw_refund_kefreyt_volunteers = yes }
		}
	}
}

# STATE: unissued formations return to their donor, never to the local owner.
STP_cw_refund_kefreyt_volunteers = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_kefreyt_refund_count value = STP_cw_kefreyt_volunteers }
	clear_variable = STP_cw_kefreyt_volunteers
	for_loop_effect = {
		end = 3
		value = STP_cw_formation_index
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_kefreyt_refund_count value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_kefreyt_refund_count value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { VAL = { exists = yes } } VAL = { STP_cw_refund_assault_division = yes } }
		}
	}
}

# STATE: the successor and its templates exist before the split distributes assets.
STP_cw_deploy_kefreyt_volunteers = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			state = 1
			is_owned_by = STS
			is_controlled_by = STS
			check_variable = { var = STP_cw_kefreyt_volunteers value = 3 compare = equals }
			STS = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_templates_loaded }
		}
		clear_variable = STP_cw_kefreyt_volunteers
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = 3
			value = STP_cw_formation_index
			create_unit = {
				division = "division_template = \"Kefreyt Volunteer Division\" start_experience_factor = 0.5 start_equipment_factor = 1.0 start_manpower_factor = 1.0"
				owner = STS
				allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
			}
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY: unlock first. A locked Kefreyt template can ignore template deletion.
STP_cw_purge_kefreyt_host_templates = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_template = "Kefreyt Volunteer Division" }
		set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Kefreyt Volunteer Division" is_locked = no }
		delete_unit = { division_template = "Kefreyt Volunteer Division" disband = no }
		delete_unit_template_and_units = { division_template = "Kefreyt Volunteer Division" disband = no }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_template = "Kefreyt Contract Infantry" }
		set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Kefreyt Contract Infantry" is_locked = no }
		delete_unit = { division_template = "Kefreyt Contract Infantry" disband = no }
		delete_unit_template_and_units = { division_template = "Kefreyt Contract Infantry" disband = no }
	}
}

# COUNTRY: drop the union peacetime roster. Field templates stay with the owner.
STP_cw_strip_peacetime_union_templates = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_template = "Police division" }
		set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Police division" is_locked = no }
		delete_unit = { division_template = "Police division" disband = no }
		delete_unit_template_and_units = { division_template = "Police division" disband = no }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_template = "Regular army" }
		set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Regular army" is_locked = no }
		delete_unit = { division_template = "Regular army" disband = no }
		delete_unit_template_and_units = { division_template = "Regular army" disband = no }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_template = "Capital Guard" }
		set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Capital Guard" is_locked = no }
		delete_unit = { division_template = "Capital Guard" disband = no }
		delete_unit_template_and_units = { division_template = "Capital Guard" disband = no }
	}
}

# COUNTRY: the shared wartime OOB also carries the STS-only volunteer template.
STP_cw_strip_kefreyt_volunteer_template = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_template = "Kefreyt Volunteer Division" }
		set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Kefreyt Volunteer Division" is_locked = no }
		delete_unit_template_and_units = { division_template = "Kefreyt Volunteer Division" disband = no }
	}
}

# Volunteers belong to STS. Strip leftover peacetime union templates there too.
STP_cw_remove_kefreyt_volunteers = {
	STP = { STP_cw_purge_kefreyt_host_templates = yes }
	STS = {
		STP_cw_purge_kefreyt_host_templates = yes
		STP_cw_strip_peacetime_union_templates = yes
	}
	SRP = {
		STP_cw_purge_kefreyt_host_templates = yes
		STP_cw_strip_peacetime_union_templates = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY: call only after payment or consumption of one prepared escrow entry.
STP_cw_deploy_assault_division = {
	if = {
		limit = { any_owned_state = { is_controlled_by = PREV STP_cw_state_faces_rival = yes } }
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = { STP_cw_state_faces_rival = yes }
			STP_cw_create_assault_division = yes
		}
	}
	else = {
		random_owned_controlled_state = { STP_cw_create_assault_division = yes }
	}
}

# STATE: owner is PREV.
STP_cw_create_assault_division = {
	create_unit = {
		division = "division_template = \"Stelander Assault Division\" start_experience_factor = 0.3 start_equipment_factor = 1.0 start_manpower_factor = 1.0"
		owner = PREV
		allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
	}
}

STP_cw_mobilize_assault_division = {
	if = {
		limit = { any_owned_state = { is_controlled_by = PREV } STP_cw_can_pay_assault_division = yes }
		STP_cw_pay_assault_division = yes
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_assault_division_paid } clr_country_flag = STP_cw_assault_division_paid STP_cw_deploy_assault_division = yes }
	}
}

# COUNTRY: paid cohorts are kept outside the available reserve until settlement.
STP_cw_refund_reserve_training = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_training_cohorts value = 2 compare = equals } } }
		hidden_effect = { clear_variable = STP_cw_training_cohorts }
		add_manpower = 12000
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_training_political_payment }
			clr_country_flag = STP_cw_training_political_payment
		}
		else = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 10800 }
		}
	}
}

STP_cw_finish_reserve_training = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_training_cohorts value = 2 compare = equals } NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_postwar } STP_cw_can_rearm = yes any_owned_state = { is_controlled_by = PREV } }
		clear_variable = STP_cw_training_cohorts
		clr_country_flag = STP_cw_training_political_payment
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = 2
			value = STP_cw_formation_index
			if = {
				limit = { any_owned_state = { is_controlled_by = PREV STP_cw_state_faces_rival = yes } }
				random_owned_controlled_state = {
					limit = { STP_cw_state_faces_rival = yes }
					create_unit = {
						division = "division_template = \"Stelander Territorial Brigade\" start_experience_factor = 0.3 start_equipment_factor = 1.0 start_manpower_factor = 1.0"
						owner = PREV
						allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				random_owned_controlled_state = {
					create_unit = {
						division = "division_template = \"Stelander Territorial Brigade\" start_experience_factor = 0.3 start_equipment_factor = 1.0 start_manpower_factor = 1.0"
						owner = PREV
						allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else = { STP_cw_refund_reserve_training = yes }
}

# Close a wartime laboratory slot exactly once. The idea on_remove and the
# postwar cleanup both call this; the ledger blocks a second subtraction.
STP_cw_revoke_emergency_research_slot = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_emergency_research_slots value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		add_research_slot = -1
		subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_cw_emergency_research_slots value = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_emergency_research_slots value = 0 compare = less_than_or_equals } }
			clear_variable = STP_cw_emergency_research_slots
		}
	}
}

# Close fixed-price orders before restoring unrestricted template editing.
STP_cw_finish_mobilization = {
	clr_country_flag = STP_party_mobile_reserve_ready
	STP_cw_refund_disruption = yes
	clr_country_flag = STP_cw_disruption_unlocked
	clr_country_flag = STP_cw_disruption_cooldown
	clear_variable = STP_cw_disruption_completed
	set_country_flag = STP_cw_postwar
	STP_cw_clear_wartime_modifiers = yes
	if = { limit = { tag = STS } VAL_operations_map_refresh_cache = yes }
	STP_pw_reconcile_postwar_interactivity = yes
	STP_qol_initialize_staff = yes
	mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
	if = { limit = { OR = { tag = STP tag = STS } } STS = { STP_cw_stop_operations = yes } }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = STP_cw_emergency_laboratories } remove_ideas = STP_cw_emergency_laboratories }
	STP_cw_revoke_emergency_research_slot = yes
	if = { limit = { tag = SRP NOT = { has_war_with = VAL } } remove_ideas = { SRP_emergency_command SRP_council_supply } }
	STP_cw_refund_reserve_training = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_templates_loaded }
		set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Stelander Territorial Brigade" is_locked = no }
		set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Stelander Assault Division" is_locked = no }
	}
}

STP_cw_refund_arsenal_project = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = STP_cw_arsenal_deposit }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_cash_refund value = STP_cw_arsenal_deposit }
		multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_cash_refund value = 0.5 }
		clear_variable = STP_cw_arsenal_deposit
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = STP_cw_cash_refund } add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = STP_cw_cash_refund } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

# STATE: release reserved resources after transfer and before cleanup.
STP_cw_materialize_region_assets = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_cw_assets_consumed } }
		set_state_flag = STP_cw_assets_consumed
		STP_cw_deploy_kefreyt_volunteers = yes
		# Unfinished orders already returned before the proportional allocation.
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_cached_rifles value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
			owner = { add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = PREV.STP_cw_cached_rifles } }
			log = "[STP_CW] state [THIS.GetID] cached rifles paid=[?STP_cw_cached_rifles]"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_sabotage_rifles value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
			owner = { add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = PREV.STP_cw_sabotage_rifles } }
			log = "[STP_CW] state [THIS.GetID] sabotage escrow paid=[?STP_cw_sabotage_rifles]"
		}
		# Each captured port or inland depot transfers up to 500 fuel from government stock.
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_state_flag = STP_resistance_supply_asset
				OR = { state = 3 state = 46 }
				is_owned_by = STS
				STP = { check_variable = { var = fuel_k value = 0.5 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			}
			STP = { add_fuel = -500 }
			owner = { add_fuel = 500 }
			log = "[STP_CW] state [THIS.GetID] supply asset transferred 500 existing fuel"
		}
		# Prepared sabotage matters only if the party keeps the district.
		# A captured district keeps the buildings; damage is repaired normally.
		# State 28 never opens ordinary player operations; only this paid charge fires.
		if = {
			limit = { state = 53 is_owned_by = STP is_controlled_by = STP has_state_flag = STP_resistance_sabotage_asset infrastructure > 0 }
			damage_building = { type = infrastructure damage = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { state = 28 is_owned_by = STP is_controlled_by = STP has_state_flag = STP_resistance_sabotage_asset infrastructure > 0 }
			damage_building = { type = infrastructure damage = 2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { state = 28 is_owned_by = STP is_controlled_by = STP has_state_flag = STP_resistance_sabotage_asset arms_factory > 0 }
			damage_building = { type = arms_factory damage = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { state = 29 is_owned_by = STP is_controlled_by = STP has_state_flag = STP_resistance_sabotage_asset industrial_complex > 0 }
			damage_building = { type = industrial_complex damage = 2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				state = 29
				is_owned_by = STP
				is_controlled_by = STP
				has_state_flag = STP_resistance_sabotage_asset
			}
			STP = {
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_idea = STP_cw_party_sabotage_aftermath } }
					add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_cw_party_sabotage_aftermath days = 70 }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { state = 28 is_owned_by = STP is_controlled_by = STP has_state_flag = STP_resistance_sabotage_asset }
			STP = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = STP_cw_party_command_sabotage } } add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_cw_party_command_sabotage days = 70 } } }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = STP_cw_defensive_line_prepared }
			add_building_construction = { type = bunker level = 2 instant_build = yes province = { all_provinces = yes limit_to_border = yes } }
			clr_state_flag = STP_cw_defensive_line_prepared
		}
		clear_variable = STP_cw_cached_rifles
		clear_variable = STP_cw_sabotage_rifles
		clr_state_flag = STP_resistance_sabotage_asset
		clr_state_flag = STP_niansas_transfer_secured
		clr_state_flag = STP_resistance_administration_asset
		clr_state_flag = STP_local_deal_asset
		clr_state_flag = STP_party_inspection_active
		# Garrison assets are consumed separately after paid mobilisation.
	}
}

STP_cw_prepare_successor = {
	set_country_flag = STP_cw_participant
	set_rule = { can_join_factions = no can_create_factions = no }
	# inherit_technology copies later union research when the engine applies it.
	# A newly activated tag can keep an empty tree; the same starting modules
	# unlock the union's equipment even if that copy does nothing.
	inherit_technology = STP
	ADISCORD_grant_technology_profile_common = yes
	ADISCORD_grant_technology_profile_industrial = yes
	ADISCORD_grant_technology_profile_energy = yes
	ADISCORD_grant_technology_profile_institutional = yes
	ADISCORD_grant_technology_profile_land = yes
	ADISCORD_grant_technology_profile_air = yes
	ADISCORD_grant_technology_profile_naval = yes
	ADISCORD_grant_technology_profile_recon_platform = yes
	ADISCORD_grant_technology_profile_field_air_defense = yes
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_emergency_research_slots value = 0 compare = greater_than } } }
		set_research_slots = 1
	}
	# Recruits keep a higher union ceiling. Volunteer-only and disarmed
	# mobilisation are raised to limited conscription so a civil-war
	# successor can recruit immediately.
	if = { limit = { STP = { has_idea = disarmed_nation } } add_ideas = limited_conscription }
	else_if = { limit = { STP = { has_idea = volunteer_only } } add_ideas = limited_conscription }
	else_if = { limit = { STP = { has_idea = limited_conscription } } add_ideas = limited_conscription }
	else_if = { limit = { STP = { has_idea = extensive_conscription } } add_ideas = extensive_conscription }
	else_if = { limit = { STP = { has_idea = service_by_requirement } } add_ideas = service_by_requirement }
	else_if = { limit = { STP = { has_idea = all_adults_serve } } add_ideas = all_adults_serve }
	else_if = { limit = { STP = { has_idea = scraping_the_barrel } } add_ideas = scraping_the_barrel }
	load_oob = ADISCORD_STP_civil_war_templates
	STP_cw_strip_peacetime_union_templates = yes
	if = { limit = { NOT = { tag = STS } } STP_cw_strip_kefreyt_volunteer_template = yes }
	set_country_flag = STP_cw_templates_loaded
	STP_cw_apply_wartime_template_locks = yes
	if = {
		limit = { tag = STS }
		add_ideas = {
			ADISCORD_military_organization_militia_autonomy
			ADISCORD_officer_corps_field_commissions
			ADISCORD_training_basic_drills
			ADISCORD_logistics_local_foraging
			ADISCORD_justice_field_courts
		}
	}
	set_country_flag = ADISCORD_economy_important_country
	ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
	ADISCORD_economy_set_simulation_tier = yes
	STP_cw_initialize_focus_modifiers = yes
	# Record and clear the successor's manpower pool before allocating shared reserves.
	log = "[STP_CW] newborn [THIS.GetTag] engine manpower=[?manpower]"
	# transfer_state already moved this recruitable pool out of STP. Return it,
	# then apply the mandate-dependent allocation from the original reserve.
	set_variable = { var = STP_cw_newborn_manpower value = manpower }
	STP = { add_manpower = var:PREV.STP_cw_newborn_manpower }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_cw_newborn_manpower value = -1 }
	add_manpower = var:STP_cw_newborn_manpower
	clear_variable = STP_cw_newborn_manpower
}

# STP: transfer accumulated values after successors initialize their modifier variables.
STP_cw_transfer_preparation_modifiers = {
	# Shabrat inherits the actual prewar sovereign debt. The successor economy
	# must not erase liabilities created while the player was still controlling STP.
	STS = {
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_debt value = STP.ADISCORD_economy_debt }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_disruption_unlocked }
		STS = { set_country_flag = STP_cw_disruption_unlocked }
		clr_country_flag = STP_cw_disruption_unlocked
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_cw_resistance_network_dynamic } }
		STS = {
			set_variable = { var = STP_cw_network_political_power_gain value = STP.STP_cw_network_political_power_gain }
			STP_cw_refresh_network_modifier = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_pending_army_org_factor value = 0 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_pending_army_planning_speed value = 0 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_pending_army_org_regain value = 0 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_pending_army_supply_consumption_factor value = 0 compare = equals } }
			}
		}
		STS = {
			set_variable = { var = STP_cw_army_org_factor value = STP.STP_cw_pending_army_org_factor }
			set_variable = { var = STP_cw_army_planning_speed value = STP.STP_cw_pending_army_planning_speed }
			set_variable = { var = STP_cw_army_org_regain value = STP.STP_cw_pending_army_org_regain }
			set_variable = { var = STP_cw_army_supply_consumption_factor value = STP.STP_cw_pending_army_supply_consumption_factor }
			STP_cw_refresh_army_modifier = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_shabrat_election_victory }
		STS = {
			set_variable = { var = STP_cw_mandate_org_regain value = STP.STP_cw_mandate_org_regain }
			set_variable = { var = STP_cw_mandate_core_defence value = STP.STP_cw_mandate_core_defence }
			set_variable = { var = STP_cw_mandate_training_time value = STP.STP_cw_mandate_training_time }
			set_variable = { var = STP_cw_mandate_mobilization value = STP.STP_cw_mandate_mobilization }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_cw_election_mandate_dynamic }
		}
	}
	# Read the prewar focus before replacing the tree; the observers follow Shabrat.
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = STP_cw_northern_dossier STS = { exists = yes } NOD = { exists = yes } }
		STS = { add_intel = { target = NOD army_intel = 15 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = STP_cw_northern_desk STS = { exists = yes } NOD = { exists = yes } }
		STS = { add_intel = { target = NOD army_intel = 10 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_northern_strategy_evidence }
		STS = { set_country_flag = STP_cw_northern_strategy_evidence }
		clr_country_flag = STP_cw_northern_strategy_evidence
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_northern_strategy_supply }
		STS = { set_country_flag = STP_cw_northern_strategy_supply }
		clr_country_flag = STP_cw_northern_strategy_supply
	}
	# Unfinished northern treasury belongs to Shabrat after the successor economy exists.
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = STP_cw_northern_fort_deposit }
		STS = {
			add_to_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury
				value = PREV.STP_cw_northern_fort_deposit
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income
				value = PREV.STP_cw_northern_fort_deposit
			}
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		clear_variable = STP_cw_northern_fort_deposit
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = STP_cw_northern_engineer_deposit }
		STS = {
			add_to_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury
				value = PREV.STP_cw_northern_engineer_deposit
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income
				value = PREV.STP_cw_northern_engineer_deposit
			}
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		clear_variable = STP_cw_northern_engineer_deposit
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_northern_aid_open }
		STS = { set_country_flag = STP_cw_northern_aid_open }
		clr_country_flag = STP_cw_northern_aid_open
	}
	STP_cw_clear_resistance_modifiers = yes
	# The former government's payment agreements end when the state splits.
	remove_ideas = STP_cw_private_payments
}

# COUNTRY (STP): markers explain the same flags that govern the actual transfers.
# A resolved allegiance is never reapplied, so repeated refreshes preserve earned traits.
STP_cw_refresh_officer_loyalties = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_character = STP_Edmund_Ravel STP_Edmund_Ravel = { has_trait = STP_ravel_undecided } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_officers_ready }
			STP_Edmund_Ravel = { remove_unit_leader_trait = STP_ravel_undecided add_unit_leader_trait = STP_shabrat_loyalist }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_participant }
			STP_Edmund_Ravel = { remove_unit_leader_trait = STP_ravel_undecided add_unit_leader_trait = STP_party_loyalist }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_character = STP_Severin_Drake STP_Severin_Drake = { has_trait = STP_drake_undecided } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_young_officers_ready }
			STP_Severin_Drake = { remove_unit_leader_trait = STP_drake_undecided add_unit_leader_trait = STP_shabrat_loyalist }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_participant }
			STP_Severin_Drake = { remove_unit_leader_trait = STP_drake_undecided add_unit_leader_trait = STP_party_loyalist }
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY: drop donor country_leader roles before set_nationality. The engine keeps
# a live office on the current tag, so Sotnikov would stay on STP after arrest.
STP_cw_release_resistance_officeholders = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_character = STP_maksim_shabrat }
		STP_maksim_shabrat = { if = { limit = { has_ideology = national_legitimism } remove_country_leader_role = { ideology = national_legitimism } } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_character = STP_grigory_sotnikov }
		STP_grigory_sotnikov = {
			if = { limit = { has_ideology = humanism_ideology } remove_country_leader_role = { ideology = humanism_ideology } }
			if = { limit = { has_ideology = steland_military_directory } remove_country_leader_role = { ideology = steland_military_directory } }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_character = STP_ilya_gornin }
		STP_ilya_gornin = { if = { limit = { has_ideology = steland_liberationism } remove_country_leader_role = { ideology = steland_liberationism } } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_character = STP_vera_tikh }
		STP_vera_tikh = { if = { limit = { has_ideology = utilitarism_ideology } remove_country_leader_role = { ideology = utilitarism_ideology } } }
	}
}

# COUNTRY: Korsh is recruited on STP without a command role. Move him, then
# add the commander role on STS after a human takes Shabrat and he is free.
STP_cw_commission_korsh = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = { STP = { has_character = STP_Gleb_Korsh } STS = { has_character = STP_Gleb_Korsh } }
			is_ai = no
			STS = { exists = yes }
			OR = {
				tag = STS
				has_country_flag = STP_sided_with_Maksim_flag
			}
			OR = {
				STP_cw_shabrat_available = yes
				STS = { STP_cw_shabrat_available = yes }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { STP = { has_character = STP_Gleb_Korsh } }
			STP = { set_nationality = { character = STP_Gleb_Korsh target_country = STS } }
		}
		STS = {
			STP_Gleb_Korsh = {
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { is_unit_leader = yes } }
					add_corps_commander_role = {
						traits = { trickster STP_shabrat_loyalist }
						skill = 3
						attack_skill = 3
						defense_skill = 2
						planning_skill = 2
						logistics_skill = 4
						legacy_id = -1
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY (STS): create only Shabrat's missing role; never recruit an absent character.
# Promote with a registered subtype. set_politics stays in the align helper so the
# ruling party matches the person who actually received the role.
STP_cw_establish_resistance_command = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = STS has_country_flag = STP_cw_participant }
		if = {
			limit = { STP_cw_shabrat_available = yes }
			STP_maksim_shabrat = {
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { is_country_leader = yes } }
					add_country_leader_role = {
						promote_leader = yes
						country_leader = { ideology = national_legitimism expire = "2200.1.1.1" }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { is_unit_leader = yes } }
					add_field_marshal_role = {
						traits = { STP_shabrat_loyalist }
						skill = 2
						attack_skill = 2
						defense_skill = 2
						planning_skill = 2
						logistics_skill = 1
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { is_corps_commander = yes NOT = { is_field_marshal = yes } }
					promote_leader = yes
				}
				if = { limit = { NOT = { has_trait = STP_shabrat_loyalist } } add_unit_leader_trait = STP_shabrat_loyalist }
			}
			promote_character = { character = STP_maksim_shabrat ideology = national_legitimism }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character = STP_grigory_sotnikov STP_grigory_sotnikov = { NOT = { has_character_flag = STP_cw_arrested } } }
			STP_grigory_sotnikov = {
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { is_country_leader = yes } }
					add_country_leader_role = {
						promote_leader = yes
						country_leader = { ideology = steland_military_directory expire = "2200.1.1.1" }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { is_unit_leader = yes } }
					add_field_marshal_role = {
						traits = { STP_shabrat_loyalist }
						skill = 2
						attack_skill = 2
						defense_skill = 2
						planning_skill = 3
						logistics_skill = 2
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { is_corps_commander = yes NOT = { is_field_marshal = yes } }
					promote_leader = yes
				}
			}
			promote_character = { character = STP_grigory_sotnikov ideology = steland_military_directory }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character = STP_Leonid_Barchel STP_Leonid_Barchel = { NOT = { has_character_flag = STP_cw_arrested } } }
			# Barchel leads the provisional government without losing his existing army role.
			STP_Leonid_Barchel = {
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { is_country_leader = yes } }
					add_country_leader_role = {
						promote_leader = yes
						country_leader = { ideology = chauvinism_ideology expire = "2200.1.1.1" }
					}
				}
			}
			promote_character = { character = STP_Leonid_Barchel ideology = chauvinism_ideology }
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY (STP). Predetermined sides call this at the end of setup.
# An unset human side still waits on .30; AI never waits.
STP_cw_begin_hostilities = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = STP has_country_flag = STP_cw_participant
			STS = { exists = yes }
			NOT = { has_war_with = STS }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = STP_cw_union_wars_finished }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = STP_cw_started } }
			set_global_flag = STP_cw_started
			SLI = { SLI_declare_independence = yes }
			# The Party remains STP through the split, so explicitly retire the
			# prewar hedonist-regime spirits when open hostilities begin.
			remove_ideas = STP_hedonism_with_no_bondaries
			remove_ideas = STP_hidden_slaves_trade
			remove_ideas = STP_legalize
			remove_ideas = STP_worldwide_famous_tourist_destination
			if = { limit = { VAL = { exists = yes } } VAL = { VAL_open_refugee_waves = yes } }
			hidden_effect = {
				# Playback waits until change_tag_from has assigned the human to the chosen side.
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.95 hours = 1 }
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.94 hours = 1 }
				STS = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.95 hours = 1 } }
			}
			remove_mission = STP_cw_nod_warning
			clr_country_flag = STP_cw_nod_warning_active
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_early_orders_ready }
				STS = {
					add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_cw_offensive_preparation days = 35 }
					army_experience = 10
					add_command_power = 15
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_party_defence_ready }
				add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_cw_defensive_preparation days = 35 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = STP_party_mobile_reserve_ready }
				add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_cw_offensive_preparation days = 35 }
				clr_country_flag = STP_party_mobile_reserve_ready
			}
			# Either captured depot supports infantry during mobilisation. Two depots
			# transfer their own fuel but never stack or refresh this one army-wide idea.
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						3 = { is_owned_by = STS has_state_flag = STP_resistance_supply_asset }
						46 = { is_owned_by = STS has_state_flag = STP_resistance_supply_asset }
					}
				}
				STS = { add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_cw_prepared_supply_lines days = 21 } }
			}
			STP_end_battle_for_stelander = yes
		}
		declare_war_on = { target = STS type = annex_everything }
		# Fix all province controllers after the engine creates the war relation.
		every_owned_state = { set_state_controller_to = PREV }
		STS = { every_owned_state = { set_state_controller_to = PREV } }
		SRP = { every_owned_state = { set_state_controller_to = PREV } }
		STS = { STP_hw_stall_begin = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { has_war_with = STS NOT = { has_idea = STP_cw_party_battle_spirit } }
			STP_cw_poll_nod_intervention = yes
			STS = { STP_cw_begin_operations = yes }
			add_ideas = STP_cw_party_battle_spirit
			STS = { add_ideas = STP_cw_resistance_battle_spirit }
			# Major news is repeatable so every country receives it; this flag is
			# shared with the war-relation on_action that publishes the same report.
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_news_stelander_civil_war_published } }
				set_global_flag = ADISCORD_news_stelander_civil_war_published
				news_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.70 hours = 1 }
			}
			STS = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.91 days = 5 } }
			if = { limit = { VAL = { exists = yes } } VAL = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.20 days = 1 } } }
			log = "[STP_CW] hostilities begin after side selection and full territorial control setup"
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			tag = STS has_country_flag = STP_cw_participant
			STP = { exists = yes }
			NOT = { has_war_with = STP }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = STP_cw_union_wars_finished }
		}
		STP = { STP_cw_begin_hostilities = yes }
		# After change_tag the player is on STS; STP may no longer be able to declare.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_war_with = STP } }
			declare_war_on = { target = STP type = annex_everything }
			every_owned_state = { set_state_controller_to = PREV }
			STP = { every_owned_state = { set_state_controller_to = PREV } }
			SRP = { every_owned_state = { set_state_controller_to = PREV } }
			if = {
				limit = { has_war_with = STP }
				STP = {
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_idea = STP_cw_party_battle_spirit } }
						add_ideas = STP_cw_party_battle_spirit
						STS = { add_ideas = STP_cw_resistance_battle_spirit }
						if = {
							limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_news_stelander_civil_war_published } }
							set_global_flag = ADISCORD_news_stelander_civil_war_published
							news_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.70 hours = 1 }
						}
						STS = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.91 days = 5 } }
						if = { limit = { VAL = { exists = yes } } VAL = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.20 days = 1 } } }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# STP: reserve prepared formations, then baseline armies; divide only the surplus.
# The caller sets STP_cw_input_pool, STP_cw_input_light and STP_cw_input_heavy.
# The three temporary allocations conserve the free pool, including fractional shortages.
STP_cw_allocate_formation_reserves = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_allocation_pool value = STP_cw_input_pool }
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_party_prepared_reserve value = STP_cw_report_party_brigades }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_party_prepared_reserve value = 16 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_party_prepared_reserve value = STP_cw_input_light }
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_resistance_prepared_reserve value = STP_cw_report_resistance_brigades }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_resistance_prepared_reserve value = 14 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_resistance_prepared_reserve value = STP_cw_input_light }
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_prepared_reserve value = STP_cw_party_prepared_reserve }
	add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_prepared_reserve value = STP_cw_resistance_prepared_reserve }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_prepared_reserve value = STP_cw_allocation_pool compare = greater_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_prepared_share value = STP_cw_allocation_pool }
		divide_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_prepared_share value = STP_cw_prepared_reserve }
		multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_party_prepared_reserve value = STP_cw_prepared_share }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_resistance_prepared_reserve value = STP_cw_allocation_pool }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_resistance_prepared_reserve value = STP_cw_party_prepared_reserve }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_prepared_reserve value = STP_cw_allocation_pool }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_party_base_reserve value = 16 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_resistance_base_reserve value = 14 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_republics_base_reserve value = 10 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_party_heavy_reserve value = 6 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_resistance_remainder_share value = 0.4 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_limited_party_revolt }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_party_base_reserve value = 5 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_resistance_base_reserve value = 16 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_party_heavy_reserve value = 3 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_resistance_remainder_share value = 0.64 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_shabrat_election_victory }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_party_base_reserve value = 14 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_resistance_base_reserve value = 16 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_resistance_remainder_share value = 0.5 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_party_election_victory }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_party_base_reserve value = 16 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_resistance_base_reserve value = 16 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_resistance_remainder_share value = 0.4 }
	}
	multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_party_base_reserve value = STP_cw_input_light }
	multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_resistance_base_reserve value = STP_cw_input_light }
	multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_republics_base_reserve value = STP_cw_input_light }
	multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_party_heavy_reserve value = STP_cw_input_heavy }
	add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_party_base_reserve value = STP_cw_party_heavy_reserve }
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_resistance_heavy_reserve value = 6 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_resistance_heavy_reserve value = STP_cw_input_heavy }
	add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_resistance_base_reserve value = STP_cw_resistance_heavy_reserve }
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_base_reserve value = STP_cw_party_base_reserve }
	add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_base_reserve value = STP_cw_resistance_base_reserve }
	add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_base_reserve value = STP_cw_republics_base_reserve }
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_unassigned_reserve value = STP_cw_allocation_pool }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_unassigned_reserve value = STP_cw_prepared_reserve }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_base_reserve value = STP_cw_unassigned_reserve compare = greater_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_base_share value = STP_cw_unassigned_reserve }
		divide_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_base_share value = STP_cw_base_reserve }
		multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_party_base_reserve value = STP_cw_base_share }
		multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_resistance_base_reserve value = STP_cw_base_share }
		multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_republics_base_reserve value = STP_cw_base_share }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_base_reserve value = STP_cw_unassigned_reserve }
	}
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_unassigned_reserve value = STP_cw_base_reserve }
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_allocation_republics value = STP_cw_unassigned_reserve }
	multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_allocation_republics value = 0.2 }
	add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_allocation_republics value = STP_cw_republics_base_reserve }
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_allocation_resistance value = STP_cw_unassigned_reserve }
	multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_allocation_resistance value = STP_cw_resistance_remainder_share }
	add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_allocation_resistance value = STP_cw_resistance_base_reserve }
	add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_allocation_resistance value = STP_cw_resistance_prepared_reserve }
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_allocation_party value = STP_cw_allocation_pool }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_allocation_party value = STP_cw_allocation_republics }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_allocation_party value = STP_cw_allocation_resistance }
}

# COUNTRY STP/SRP: consolidate only aircraft after the general stockpile split.
# Native transfers retain equipment models and their original producers.
STP_cw_send_air_reserves_to_resistance = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_air_transfer value = num_equipment@ADISCORD_fighter_archetype }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_air_transfer value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		send_equipment = { type = ADISCORD_fighter_archetype amount = STP_cw_air_transfer target = STS }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_air_transfer value = num_equipment@ADISCORD_cas_archetype }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_air_transfer value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		send_equipment = { type = ADISCORD_cas_archetype amount = STP_cw_air_transfer target = STS }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_air_transfer value = num_equipment@ADISCORD_rocket_strike_archetype }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_air_transfer value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		send_equipment = { type = ADISCORD_rocket_strike_archetype amount = STP_cw_air_transfer target = STS }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_air_transfer value = num_equipment@ADISCORD_bomber_archetype }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_air_transfer value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		send_equipment = { type = ADISCORD_bomber_archetype amount = STP_cw_air_transfer target = STS }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_air_transfer value = num_equipment@ADISCORD_naval_aircraft_archetype }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_air_transfer value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		send_equipment = { type = ADISCORD_naval_aircraft_archetype amount = STP_cw_air_transfer target = STS }
	}
}

# COUNTRY STP: read the completed focus before replacing the preparation tree.
STP_cw_divide_air_reserves = {
	STP_cw_send_air_reserves_to_resistance = yes
	SRP = { STP_cw_send_air_reserves_to_resistance = yes }
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = STP_ps_loyal_aircrews }
		STS = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_air_transfer value = num_equipment@ADISCORD_fighter_archetype }
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_air_transfer value = 0.2 }
			round_temp_variable = STP_cw_air_transfer
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_air_transfer value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
				send_equipment = { type = ADISCORD_fighter_archetype amount = STP_cw_air_transfer target = STP }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_air_transfer value = num_equipment@ADISCORD_cas_archetype }
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_air_transfer value = 0.2 }
			round_temp_variable = STP_cw_air_transfer
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_air_transfer value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
				send_equipment = { type = ADISCORD_cas_archetype amount = STP_cw_air_transfer target = STP }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_air_transfer value = num_equipment@ADISCORD_rocket_strike_archetype }
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_air_transfer value = 0.2 }
			round_temp_variable = STP_cw_air_transfer
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_air_transfer value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
				send_equipment = { type = ADISCORD_rocket_strike_archetype amount = STP_cw_air_transfer target = STP }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_air_transfer value = num_equipment@ADISCORD_bomber_archetype }
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_air_transfer value = 0.2 }
			round_temp_variable = STP_cw_air_transfer
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_air_transfer value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
				send_equipment = { type = ADISCORD_bomber_archetype amount = STP_cw_air_transfer target = STP }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_air_transfer value = num_equipment@ADISCORD_naval_aircraft_archetype }
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_air_transfer value = 0.2 }
			round_temp_variable = STP_cw_air_transfer
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_air_transfer value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
				send_equipment = { type = ADISCORD_naval_aircraft_archetype amount = STP_cw_air_transfer target = STP }
			}
		}
	}
}

# When the party is played, the AI resistance opens the war with a ground-attack
# arm: aircraft in the stockpile (the AI forms wings from them) and room at Abilia.
STP_cw_sts_ai_air_arm = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_ai = yes STP = { is_ai = no } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_cas_airframe_2170 amount = 50 producer = STS }
		1 = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_owned_by = STS }
				add_building_construction = { type = air_base level = 1 instant_build = yes }
			}
		}
	}
}

# Shabrat's AI attacks in waves: 42 days of pressure, then 21 days of
# regrouping (STS_shabrat_recover_party_front) unless the wave took a state
# from the party or the resistance army clearly outweighs it.
STP_cw_ai_shabrat_offensive_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STS_ai_assault_wave }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STS_ai_regroup }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = STS_ai_party_states_temp value = 0 }
		STP = { every_controlled_state = { ROOT = { add_to_temp_variable = { var = STS_ai_party_states_temp value = 1 } } } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = STS_ai_wave_start_states
				check_variable = { var = STS_ai_party_states_temp value = STS_ai_wave_start_states compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				NOT = { fighting_army_strength_ratio = { tag = STP ratio > 1.5 } }
			}
			set_country_flag = { flag = STS_ai_regroup value = 1 days = 21 }
			clear_variable = STS_ai_wave_start_states
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { var = STS_ai_wave_start_states value = STS_ai_party_states_temp }
			set_country_flag = { flag = STS_ai_assault_wave value = 1 days = 42 }
		}
	}
}

STP_cw_ai_shabrat_clear_offensive = {
	clr_country_flag = STS_ai_assault_wave
	clr_country_flag = STS_ai_regroup
	clear_variable = STS_ai_wave_start_states
}

STP_cw_start = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_cw_can_start = yes }
		set_country_flag = STP_cw_participant
		clr_country_flag = STP_cw_uprising_prepared
		clear_variable = STP_nectar_story_page
		remove_mission = STP_cw_election_window
		remove_mission = STP_cw_credentials_review
		remove_mission = ivanov_health_is_getting_worse
		remove_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_2
		remove_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_3
		remove_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_29
		remove_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_45
		remove_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_46
		remove_mission = STP_party_inspection_state_53
		if = { limit = { has_character = STP_Petr_Ivanov } retire_character = STP_Petr_Ivanov }
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_fading_father } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_fading_father }
		}
		set_variable = { var = STP_fading_father_stability_factor value = 0 }
		force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
		if = {
			limit = { is_subject = yes }
			overlord = { set_autonomy = { target = STP autonomy_state = autonomy_free end_wars = no end_civil_wars = no } }
		}
		if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } faction_leader = { remove_from_faction = STP } }
		set_rule = { can_join_factions = no can_create_factions = no }
		# Demobilisation refunds the actual surviving contents; Capital Guard is untouched.
		delete_unit = { division_template = "Regular army" disband = yes }
		delete_unit = { division_template = "Police division" disband = yes }
		clear_variable = STP_cw_completed_public_campaigns
		STP_cw_cancel_assault_training = yes
		# An undelivered northern shipment returns to the shared reserve before
		# allocation. Its eventual cancelled decision must not pay it a second time.
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_northern_cargo_reserved }
			clr_country_flag = STP_cw_northern_cargo_reserved
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 24000 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_nod_sabotage_reserved }
			clr_country_flag = STP_cw_nod_sabotage_reserved
		}
		STP_cw_refund_nod_sabotage_rifles = yes
		# An unfinished national delivery rejoins the same common stock as the
		# demobilised army. Clear its receipt before late decision callbacks run.
		if = {
			limit = { 1 = { has_variable = STP_cw_pending_rifles } }
			1 = {
				STP = { add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = PREV.STP_cw_pending_rifles } }
				clear_variable = STP_cw_pending_rifles
			}
			STP_political_action_slot_release = yes
		}
		log = "[STP_CW] after demobilisation, before split: manpower=[?manpower] rifles=[?num_equipment@infantry_equipment]"
		set_variable = { var = STP_cw_initial_manpower value = manpower }
		# Capture preparations before territorial ownership changes.
		STP_cw_refresh_army_report = yes
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_input_pool value = manpower }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_input_light value = 6000 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_input_heavy value = 7900 }
		STP_cw_allocate_formation_reserves = yes
		set_variable = { var = STP_cw_republics_manpower value = STP_cw_allocation_republics }
		round_variable = STP_cw_republics_manpower
		clamp_variable = { var = STP_cw_republics_manpower min = 0 max = STP_cw_initial_manpower }
		set_variable = { var = STP_cw_resistance_manpower value = STP_cw_allocation_resistance }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_union_manpower value = STP_cw_initial_manpower }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_union_manpower value = STP_cw_republics_manpower }
		round_variable = STP_cw_resistance_manpower
		clamp_variable = { var = STP_cw_resistance_manpower min = 0 max = STP_cw_union_manpower }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_manpower_debit value = STP_cw_resistance_manpower }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_manpower_debit value = STP_cw_republics_manpower }
		multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_manpower_debit value = -1 }
		add_manpower = var:STP_cw_manpower_debit
		# transfer_state is the first operation in an absent fixed tag's scope.
		# Contested districts are transferred by id: iterating owned states while
		# removing them can skip a remaining recipient.
		STS = { transfer_state = 1 set_capital = { state = 1 } }
		SRP = { transfer_state = 43 transfer_state = 44 transfer_state = 88 set_capital = { state = 43 } }
		STP_cw_preserve_council_guarantees = yes
		set_capital = { state = 28 }
		if = { limit = { 2 = { STP_cw_region_goes_to_republics = yes } } SRP = { transfer_state = 2 } }
		else_if = { limit = { 2 = { STP_cw_region_goes_to_resistance = yes } } STS = { transfer_state = 2 } }
		if = { limit = { 3 = { STP_cw_region_goes_to_republics = yes } } SRP = { transfer_state = 3 } }
		else_if = { limit = { 3 = { STP_cw_region_goes_to_resistance = yes } } STS = { transfer_state = 3 } }
		if = { limit = { 29 = { STP_cw_region_goes_to_republics = yes } } SRP = { transfer_state = 29 } }
		else_if = { limit = { 29 = { STP_cw_region_goes_to_resistance = yes } } STS = { transfer_state = 29 } }
		if = { limit = { 45 = { STP_cw_region_goes_to_republics = yes } } SRP = { transfer_state = 45 } }
		else_if = { limit = { 45 = { STP_cw_region_goes_to_resistance = yes } } STS = { transfer_state = 45 } }
		if = { limit = { 46 = { STP_cw_region_goes_to_republics = yes } } SRP = { transfer_state = 46 } }
		else_if = { limit = { 46 = { STP_cw_region_goes_to_resistance = yes } } STS = { transfer_state = 46 } }
		if = { limit = { 53 = { STP_cw_region_goes_to_republics = yes } } SRP = { transfer_state = 53 } }
		else_if = { limit = { 53 = { STP_cw_region_goes_to_resistance = yes } } STS = { transfer_state = 53 } }
		every_owned_state = { set_state_controller_to = PREV }
		STS = { every_owned_state = { set_state_controller_to = PREV } }
		SRP = { every_owned_state = { set_state_controller_to = PREV } }
		# transfer_state creates the successor, but every_owned_state on that
		# newborn tag is still empty in this same effect. Walk states by core
		# or owner instead: STS claims the original Stelander cores, including
		# districts that remain with the party.
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_core_of = STP }
			add_core_of = STS
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_owned_by = SRP }
			add_core_of = SRP
		}
		STS = {
			STP_cw_prepare_successor = yes
			STP_pc_copy_split_flags = yes
			add_manpower = var:STP.STP_cw_resistance_manpower
		}
		SRP = {
			STP_cw_prepare_successor = yes
			add_ideas = SRP_council_authority
			add_manpower = var:STP.STP_cw_republics_manpower
		}
		STP_cw_refresh_officer_loyalties = yes
		STP_cw_release_resistance_officeholders = yes
		if = { limit = { STP_cw_shabrat_available = yes } set_nationality = { character = STP_maksim_shabrat target_country = STS } }
		if = { limit = { has_character = STP_grigory_sotnikov } set_nationality = { character = STP_grigory_sotnikov target_country = STS } }
		if = { limit = { has_character = STP_ilya_gornin } set_nationality = { character = STP_ilya_gornin target_country = STS } }
		if = { limit = { has_character = STP_vera_tikh } set_nationality = { character = STP_vera_tikh target_country = STS } }
		if = { limit = { has_character = STP_Leonid_Barchel } set_nationality = { character = STP_Leonid_Barchel target_country = STS } }
		if = { limit = { has_character = STP_Viktor_Marent } set_nationality = { character = STP_Viktor_Marent target_country = SRP } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_officers_ready }
			if = { limit = { has_character = STP_Edmund_Ravel } set_nationality = { character = STP_Edmund_Ravel target_country = STS } }
			STS = { army_experience = 10 add_command_power = 15 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_young_officers_ready }
			if = { limit = { has_character = STP_Severin_Drake } set_nationality = { character = STP_Severin_Drake target_country = STS } }
			STS = { army_experience = 15 }
		}
		STP_cw_transfer_preparation_modifiers = yes
		STS = { STP_cw_establish_resistance_command = yes STP_pc_align_resistance_ruling_party = yes }
		set_politics = { ruling_party = hedonism elections_allowed = no }
		if = { limit = { has_character = STP_rufus_hedersett } promote_character = STP_rufus_hedersett }
		# Apply the paid rifle-package shares to the native stockpile transfer so
		# all equipment models and their producers remain intact. Aircraft split separately.
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_input_pool value = num_equipment@infantry_equipment }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_input_light value = 5400 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_input_heavy value = 7110 }
		STP_cw_allocate_formation_reserves = yes
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_republics_stockpile_ratio value = 0.2 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_resistance_stockpile_ratio value = STP_cw_resistance_remainder_share }
		divide_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_resistance_stockpile_ratio value = 0.8 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_allocation_pool value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_republics_stockpile_ratio value = STP_cw_allocation_republics }
			divide_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_republics_stockpile_ratio value = STP_cw_allocation_pool }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_union_rifles value = STP_cw_allocation_pool }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_union_rifles value = STP_cw_allocation_republics }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_union_rifles value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_resistance_stockpile_ratio value = STP_cw_allocation_resistance }
				divide_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_resistance_stockpile_ratio value = STP_cw_union_rifles }
			}
		}
		transfer_units_fraction = { target = SRP size = 0 stockpile_ratio = var:STP_cw_republics_stockpile_ratio army_ratio = 0 navy_ratio = 0 air_ratio = 0 keep_unit_leaders_trigger = { always = yes } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = STP_ps_loyal_aircrews }
			transfer_units_fraction = { target = STS size = 0 stockpile_ratio = var:STP_cw_resistance_stockpile_ratio army_ratio = 0 navy_ratio = 0 air_ratio = 0.8 keep_unit_leaders_trigger = { always = yes } }
		}
		else = {
			transfer_units_fraction = { target = STS size = 0 stockpile_ratio = var:STP_cw_resistance_stockpile_ratio army_ratio = 0 navy_ratio = 0 air_ratio = 1 keep_unit_leaders_trigger = { always = yes } }
		}
		STP_cw_divide_air_reserves = yes
		log = "[STP_CW] STP proportional allocation: manpower=[?manpower] rifles=[?num_equipment@infantry_equipment]"
		STS = { log = "[STP_CW] STS proportional allocation: manpower=[?manpower] rifles=[?num_equipment@infantry_equipment]" }
		SRP = { log = "[STP_CW] SRP proportional allocation: manpower=[?manpower] rifles=[?num_equipment@infantry_equipment]" }

		1 = { STP_cw_materialize_region_assets = yes }
		2 = { STP_cw_materialize_region_assets = yes }
		3 = { STP_cw_materialize_region_assets = yes }
		28 = { STP_cw_materialize_region_assets = yes }
		29 = { STP_cw_materialize_region_assets = yes }
		43 = { STP_cw_materialize_region_assets = yes }
		44 = { STP_cw_materialize_region_assets = yes }
		45 = { STP_cw_materialize_region_assets = yes }
		46 = { STP_cw_materialize_region_assets = yes }
		53 = { STP_cw_materialize_region_assets = yes }
		88 = { STP_cw_materialize_region_assets = yes }
		load_oob = ADISCORD_STP_civil_war_templates
		STP_cw_strip_kefreyt_volunteer_template = yes
		set_country_flag = STP_cw_templates_loaded
		STP_cw_apply_wartime_template_locks = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_prepared_assault_divisions value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
			STS = { for_loop_effect = { end = STP.STP_cw_prepared_assault_divisions value = STP_cw_formation_index STP_cw_deploy_assault_division = yes } }
			clear_variable = STP_cw_prepared_assault_divisions
		}
		# Prepared cohorts have first claim on their reserved people and rifles.
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_party_reserve_ready }
			STP_cw_mobilize_brigade = yes
			STP_cw_mobilize_brigade = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = STP_party_mobile_reserve_ready }
			STP_cw_mobilize_brigade = yes
			STP_cw_mobilize_brigade = yes
		}
		if = { limit = { 2 = { is_owned_by = STP has_state_flag = STP_party_reinforced_garrison_asset } } for_loop_effect = { end = 2 value = STP_cw_formation_index STP_cw_mobilize_brigade = yes } }
		if = { limit = { 29 = { is_owned_by = STP has_state_flag = STP_party_reinforced_garrison_asset } } for_loop_effect = { end = 2 value = STP_cw_formation_index STP_cw_mobilize_brigade = yes } }
		if = { limit = { 45 = { is_owned_by = STP has_state_flag = STP_party_reinforced_garrison_asset } } for_loop_effect = { end = 2 value = STP_cw_formation_index STP_cw_mobilize_brigade = yes } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_limited_party_revolt }
			for_loop_effect = { end = 3 value = STP_cw_formation_index STP_cw_mobilize_assault_division = yes }
		}
		else = { for_loop_effect = { end = 6 value = STP_cw_formation_index STP_cw_mobilize_assault_division = yes } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_limited_party_revolt } for_loop_effect = { end = 5 value = STP_cw_formation_index STP_cw_mobilize_brigade = yes } }
		else = { for_loop_effect = { end = 14 value = STP_cw_formation_index STP_cw_mobilize_brigade = yes } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_shabrat_election_victory } } for_loop_effect = { end = 2 value = STP_cw_formation_index STP_cw_mobilize_brigade = yes } }
		STS = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					2 = {
						OR = {
							AND = {
								is_owned_by = STS
								has_state_flag = STP_resistance_garrison_asset
							}
							has_state_flag = STP_cw_garrison_evacuated
						}
					}
				}
				for_loop_effect = {
					end = 2
					value = STP_cw_formation_index
					STP_cw_mobilize_brigade = yes
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					29 = {
						OR = {
							AND = {
								is_owned_by = STS
								has_state_flag = STP_resistance_garrison_asset
							}
							has_state_flag = STP_cw_garrison_evacuated
						}
					}
				}
				for_loop_effect = {
					end = 2
					value = STP_cw_formation_index
					STP_cw_mobilize_brigade = yes
				}
			}
			if = { limit = { STP = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_abila_reserve_ready } } for_loop_effect = { end = 2 value = STP_cw_formation_index STP_cw_mobilize_brigade = yes } }
			if = { limit = { STP = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_local_recruits_ready } } for_loop_effect = { end = 2 value = STP_cw_formation_index STP_cw_mobilize_brigade = yes } }
			for_loop_effect = { end = 6 value = STP_cw_formation_index STP_cw_mobilize_assault_division = yes }
			for_loop_effect = { end = 12 value = STP_cw_formation_index STP_cw_mobilize_brigade = yes }
			if = { limit = { STP = { NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_party_election_victory } } } for_loop_effect = { end = 2 value = STP_cw_formation_index STP_cw_mobilize_brigade = yes } }
			if = { limit = { STP = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_shabrat_election_victory } } for_loop_effect = { end = 2 value = STP_cw_formation_index STP_cw_mobilize_brigade = yes } }
			if = { limit = { STP = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_party_election_victory } } for_loop_effect = { end = 4 value = STP_cw_formation_index STP_cw_mobilize_brigade = yes } }
		}
		SRP = {
			for_loop_effect = { end = 10 value = STP_cw_formation_index STP_cw_mobilize_brigade = yes }
		}
		2 = { clr_state_flag = STP_resistance_garrison_asset }
		29 = { clr_state_flag = STP_resistance_garrison_asset }
		load_focus_tree = { tree = STP_cw_focus keep_completed = no }
		STS = { load_focus_tree = { tree = STP_cw_focus keep_completed = no } }
		SRP = { load_focus_tree = { tree = STP_cw_focus keep_completed = no } }
		# Only explicit neutrality closes the newborn republics' preparation.
		if = {
			limit = { VAL = { OR = { exists = no has_country_flag = VAL_cw_refused } } }
			SRP = { STP_cw_finish_mobilization = yes }
		}
		# Late preparation callbacks must cancel once armies are distributed.
		# Keep the state assets until begin_hostilities reads the timed bonuses.
		clr_country_flag = STP_battle_for_stelander_active
		clr_country_flag = STP_cw_inspection_chain_open
		clr_country_flag = STP_cw_inspection_resolving
		log = "[STP_CW] territorial and army split complete; awaiting side selection"
		# Predetermined Shabrat control switches immediately. An unset side still waits on .30.
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = no has_country_flag = STP_sided_with_Maksim_flag STS = { exists = yes } }
			STP_cw_commission_korsh = yes
			STP_cw_begin_hostilities = yes
			STS = { change_tag_from = STP }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { is_ai = no has_country_flag = STP_sided_with_the_party_flag }
			STP_cw_begin_hostilities = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { is_ai = no }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.30 }
		}
		else = { STP_cw_begin_hostilities = yes }
	}
}

VAL_cw_start_intervention = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAL_cw_can_intervene = yes OR = { has_country_flag = VAL_cw_military_course has_country_flag = VAL_cw_trade_course } }
		set_country_flag = VAL_cw_military_course
		clr_country_flag = VAL_cw_mobilization_cancelled
		clr_country_flag = VAL_cw_offer_pending
		set_country_flag = VAL_cw_offer_shown
		set_country_flag = VAL_cw_mobilizing
		set_country_flag = VAL_foreign_operation_active
		activate_mission = VAL_cw_intervention_preparation
		SRP = { activate_mission = STP_cw_val_ultimatum }
