STP_ps_clear_nod_receipt = {
	clear_variable = STP_ps_nod_receipt_type
	clear_variable = STP_ps_nod_receipt_money
	clear_variable = STP_ps_nod_receipt_stage
	remove_mission = STP_ps_nod_dispatch
	remove_mission = STP_ps_nod_route_wait
	remove_mission = STP_ps_nod_delivery
}
STP_ps_refund_nod = {
	if = { limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = STP_ps_nod_receipt_stage value = 1 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = STP_ps_nod_receipt_stage value = 2 compare = equals }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_refund value = STP_ps_nod_receipt_money }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_receipt_type value = STP_ps_nod_receipt_type }
		STP_ps_clear_nod_receipt = yes
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = STP_ps_refund }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = STP_ps_refund }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_receipt_type value = 2 compare = equals } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 12000 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment amount = 100 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 60 }
		}
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}
STP_ps_dispatch_nod = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_nod_receipt_stage value = 1 compare = equals } }
		if = { limit = { STP_ps_nod_order_current = yes STP_ps_nod_supply_route = yes }
			set_variable = { var = STP_ps_nod_receipt_stage value = 2 }
			activate_mission = STP_ps_nod_delivery
		}
		else_if = { limit = { STP_ps_nod_order_current = yes } activate_mission = STP_ps_nod_route_wait }
		else = { STP_ps_refund_nod = yes }
	}
}
STP_ps_deliver_nod = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_nod_receipt_stage value = 2 compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = { STP_ps_nod_order_current = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_receipt_type value = STP_ps_nod_receipt_type }
			STP_ps_clear_nod_receipt = yes
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_receipt_type value = 1 compare = equals } } set_country_flag = { flag = STP_ps_fund_nod_cooldown value = 1 days = 42 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_receipt_type value = 2 compare = equals } } set_country_flag = { flag = STP_ps_arm_nod_cooldown value = 1 days = 28 } }
			if = { limit = { NOD = { STP_ps_nod_can_host_exiles = yes } }
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_receipt_type value = 1 compare = equals } }
					NOD = {
						ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
						add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 1500 }
						add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 1500 }
						set_country_flag = STP_ps_nod_procurement_credit
						ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_receipt_type value = 2 compare = equals } }
					NOD = {
						add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 12000 }
						add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment amount = 100 }
						add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 60 }
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_receipt_type value = 3 compare = equals } NOD = { STP_ps_northern_front_active = yes } }
					NOD = { add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_ps_engineers_idea days = 60 } }
				}
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_receipt_type value = 4 compare = equals } NOD = { OR = { NOD_cw_intervention_possible = yes AND = { has_country_flag = NOD_cw_entered has_war_with = STS } } } }
					NOD = { set_country_flag = STP_ps_expedition_ready STP_ps_apply_expedition = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		else = { STP_ps_refund_nod = yes }
	}
}
STP_ps_apply_expedition = {
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_expedition_ready has_country_flag = NOD_cw_entered has_war_with = STS }
		clr_country_flag = STP_ps_expedition_ready
		set_country_flag = STP_ps_expedition_used
		add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_ps_expedition_idea days = 42 }
	}
}

STP_ps_request_northern_agreement = {
	if = { limit = { NOD = { STP_ps_nod_can_host_exiles = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_agreement_requested } } }
		NOD = { set_country_flag = STP_ps_agreement_requested country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.200 } }
	}
}

STP_ps_nod_rifle_purchase_refund = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_nod_rifle_purchase_deposit }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_refund value = STP_ps_nod_rifle_purchase_deposit }
		clear_variable = STP_ps_nod_rifle_purchase_deposit
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = STP_ps_refund }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = STP_ps_refund }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}
STP_ps_nod_rifle_purchase_finish = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_nod_rifle_purchase_deposit }
		if = { limit = { tag = NOD STP_ps_northern_front_active = yes has_country_flag = STP_ps_nod_procurement_credit }
			clear_variable = STP_ps_nod_rifle_purchase_deposit


			clr_country_flag = STP_ps_nod_procurement_credit add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 20000 }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		else = { STP_ps_nod_rifle_purchase_refund = yes }
	}
}

# Accepted STP defeat only. Settle outgoing resources before annexation.
STP_ps_prepare_exile = {
	if = { limit = { tag = STP NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_exile_settled } }
		set_country_flag = STP_ps_exile_settled
		STP_ps_close_defence = yes
		STP_ps_refund_cabinet = yes
		STP_ps_build_radio_refund = yes
		STP_ps_build_hq_refund = yes
		STP_ps_prepare_evacuation_refund = yes
		STP_ps_transport_refund = yes
		STP_ps_ammunition_refund = yes
		STP_ps_secure_routes_refund = yes
		STP_ps_reserve_training_refund = yes
		STP_ps_val_intelligence_refund = yes
		STP_ps_val_intercept_refund = yes
		STP_ps_val_pressure_refund = yes

		STP_ps_refund_nod = yes
		# An outgoing convoy is settled once before the donor ceases to exist.
		STP_ps_deliver_nod = yes
		if = { limit = { NOD = { STP_ps_nod_can_host_exiles = yes OR = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_asylum_agreement AND = { is_ai = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_asylum_requested } } } } }
			STP_ps_settle_exile = yes
		}
		else_if = { limit = { NOD = { STP_ps_nod_can_host_exiles = yes is_ai = no NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_asylum_requested } } }
			STP_ps_snapshot_exile = yes
			NOD = { set_country_flag = STP_ps_exile_pending country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.201 } }
		}
	}
}
STP_ps_snapshot_exile = {
	NOD = {
		set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_money value = STP.STP_ps_evacuated_money }
		set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_loyalty value = STP.STP_apparatus_loyalty }
	}
	clear_variable = STP_ps_evacuated_money

	NOD = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_conservatives_support value = STP.STP_pf_conservatives_support } }
	NOD = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_conservatives_influence value = STP.STP_pf_conservatives_influence } }
	NOD = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_borons_support value = STP.STP_pf_borons_support } }
	NOD = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_borons_influence value = STP.STP_pf_borons_influence } }
	NOD = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_security_support value = STP.STP_pf_security_support } }
	NOD = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_security_influence value = STP.STP_pf_security_influence } }
	NOD = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_army_support value = STP.STP_pf_army_support } }
	NOD = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_army_influence value = STP.STP_pf_army_influence } }
	NOD = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_advisers_support value = STP.STP_pf_advisers_support } }
	NOD = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_advisers_influence value = STP.STP_pf_advisers_influence } }
	NOD = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_merchants_support value = STP.STP_pf_merchants_support } }
	NOD = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_merchants_influence value = STP.STP_pf_merchants_influence } }
	NOD = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_radicals_support value = STP.STP_pf_radicals_support } }
	NOD = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_radicals_influence value = STP.STP_pf_radicals_influence } }
}
STP_ps_settle_exile = {
	STP_ps_snapshot_exile = yes
	NOD = { STP_ps_accept_exile = yes }
}
STP_ps_accept_exile = {
	if = { limit = { STP_ps_nod_can_host_exiles = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_exile_received } }
		set_country_flag = STP_ps_exile_received
		clr_country_flag = STP_ps_exile_pending
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_credit value = STP_ps_exile_money }
		clear_variable = STP_ps_exile_money
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = STP_ps_credit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = STP_ps_credit }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		# Only an existing character can move; no recruitment of dead officeholders.
		STP = { if = { limit = { STP_ps_hedersett_available = yes }
				STP_rufus_hedersett = { remove_country_leader_role = { ideology = aristocratic_hedonism } }
				set_nationality = { character = STP_rufus_hedersett target_country = NOD }
		} }
		mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.202 }
	}
}


STP_ps_begin_return = {
	if = { limit = { STP_ps_can_launch_return = yes }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_war_with = STS } } declare_war_on = { target = STS type = annex_everything } }
		# A rejected declaration must leave the decision available for another attempt.
		if = {
			limit = { has_war_with = STS }
			set_country_flag = STP_ps_return_campaign
			STS = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pc.49 hours = 1 } }
		}
	}
}
STP_ps_settle_return = {
	# Actual NOD victory, never a paid project's completion.
	if = { limit = { tag = NOD has_country_flag = STP_ps_return_campaign STP = { exists = no } STS = { has_capitulated = yes } }
		STP_ps_close_exile = yes
		STS = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = NOD } white_peace = NOD }
			every_owned_state = { limit = { is_core_of = STP } STP = { transfer_state = PREV } }
		}
		STP = {
			if = { limit = { exists = yes }
				set_country_flag = STP_ps_restored_dependency
				set_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_support value = NOD.STP_ps_exile_conservatives_support }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_influence value = NOD.STP_ps_exile_conservatives_influence }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pf_borons_support value = NOD.STP_ps_exile_borons_support }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pf_borons_influence value = NOD.STP_ps_exile_borons_influence }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pf_security_support value = NOD.STP_ps_exile_security_support }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pf_security_influence value = NOD.STP_ps_exile_security_influence }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_support value = NOD.STP_ps_exile_army_support }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_influence value = NOD.STP_ps_exile_army_influence }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pf_advisers_support value = NOD.STP_ps_exile_advisers_support }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pf_advisers_influence value = NOD.STP_ps_exile_advisers_influence }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pf_merchants_support value = NOD.STP_ps_exile_merchants_support }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pf_merchants_influence value = NOD.STP_ps_exile_merchants_influence }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pf_radicals_support value = NOD.STP_ps_exile_radicals_support }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pf_radicals_influence value = NOD.STP_ps_exile_radicals_influence }
				set_country_flag = STP_pf_initialized
				STP_refresh_apparatus_loyalty = yes
				set_country_flag = STP_sided_with_the_party_flag
				clr_country_flag = STP_sided_with_Maksim_flag
				STP_cw_finish_mobilization = yes
				every_owned_state = { set_state_controller_to = STP }
				load_focus_tree = { tree = STP_cw_focus keep_completed = yes }
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { STP = { exists = yes } }
			set_autonomy = { target = STP autonomy_state = autonomy_puppet }
			if = { limit = { STP_ps_hedersett_available = yes }
				set_nationality = { character = STP_rufus_hedersett target_country = STP }
				STP = { STP_rufus_hedersett = { add_country_leader_role = { country_leader = { ideology = aristocratic_hedonism expire = "2300.1.1" } } } promote_character = STP_rufus_hedersett }
			}
			else = { STP = { create_country_leader = { name = STP_ps_return_council_name desc = STP_ps_return_council_desc ideology = aristocratic_hedonism expire = "2300.1.1" } } }
			if = { limit = { is_ai = no } country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.203 } }
		}
	}
}

STP_ps_exile_training_refund = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_exile_training_deposit }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_refund value = STP_ps_exile_training_deposit }
		clear_variable = STP_ps_exile_training_deposit
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = STP_ps_refund }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = STP_ps_refund }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}
STP_ps_exile_training_finish = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_exile_training_deposit }
		if = { limit = { tag = NOD STP_ps_nod_can_host_exiles = yes has_country_flag = STP_ps_exile_received }
			clear_variable = STP_ps_exile_training_deposit
			set_country_flag = STP_ps_exile_training_completed

			army_experience = 15
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		else = { STP_ps_exile_training_refund = yes }
	}
}

STP_ps_institution_payment_refund = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_institution_payment_deposit }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_refund value = STP_ps_institution_payment_deposit }
		clear_variable = STP_ps_institution_payment_deposit
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = STP_ps_refund }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = STP_ps_refund }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}
STP_ps_institution_payment_finish = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_institution_payment_deposit }
		if = {
			limit = {
				tag = STP
				STP_pw_can_reconstruct = yes
				OR = {
					has_completed_focus = STP_ps_permanent_security
					has_completed_focus = STP_ps_district_fund
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ps_institution_stage
					value = 3
					compare = less_than
				}
			}
			clear_variable = STP_ps_institution_payment_deposit


			add_to_variable = { var = STP_ps_institution_stage value = 1 }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		else = { STP_ps_institution_payment_refund = yes }
	}
}

STP_ps_finalize_centre = {
	add_to_variable = { var = STP_pw_stability_factor value = 0.05 }
	add_to_variable = { var = STP_pw_political_power_gain value = 0.10 }
	add_to_variable = { var = STP_pw_ADISCORD_economy_admin_expense_factor value = 0.08 }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_district_concessions }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pw_ADISCORD_economy_admin_expense_factor value = 0.10 }
		clr_country_flag = STP_ps_district_concessions
	}
	STP_pw_refresh_modifier = yes
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}
STP_ps_finalize_districts = {
	add_to_variable = { var = STP_pw_production_speed_buildings_factor value = 0.08 }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pw_political_power_gain value = 0.05 }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pw_ADISCORD_economy_admin_expense_factor value = 0.05 }
	STP_pw_refresh_modifier = yes
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

STP_ps_begin_val_supply = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_ps_val_supply_open = yes
			has_country_flag = STP_ps_val_authorized
			NOT = {
				has_variable = STP_ps_val_receipt_rifles
			}
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = STP_ps_val_supply_cooldown
			}
			NOT = {
				has_equipment = {
					infantry_equipment < 12000
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_rifle_cost value = 12000 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_partial_refund value = num_equipment@infantry_equipment }
		STP_cw_pay_rifles = yes
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_rifles_paid } }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_partial_refund value = num_equipment@infantry_equipment }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = STP_ps_partial_refund producer = VAL }
		}
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_rifles_paid }
			clr_country_flag = STP_cw_rifles_paid
			set_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_receipt_rifles value = 12000 }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_sequence value = 1 }
			set_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_receipt_stage value = 1 }
			activate_mission = STP_ps_val_departure
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_val_first_dispatch } }
				set_country_flag = STP_ps_val_first_dispatch
				add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_ps_val_departure days = 7 }
			}
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_val_pressure_active } add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_ps_val_departure days = 14 } }
		}
	}
}
STP_ps_clear_val_receipt = {
	clear_variable = STP_ps_val_receipt_rifles
	clear_variable = STP_ps_val_receipt_stage
	clear_variable = STP_ps_val_receipt_contract
	clear_variable = STP_ps_val_receipt_money
	clear_variable = STP_ps_val_known_sequence
	clear_variable = STP_ps_val_intercepted_sequence
	set_country_flag = { flag = STP_ps_val_supply_cooldown value = 1 days = 7 }
	remove_mission = STP_ps_val_departure
	remove_mission = STP_ps_val_route_wait
}
STP_ps_refund_val_supply = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_val_receipt_rifles }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_refund_rifles value = STP_ps_val_receipt_rifles }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_refund_money value = STP_ps_val_receipt_money }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_contract_type value = STP_ps_val_receipt_contract }
		# Compatibility for saves from before fulfilment moved to convoy resolution.
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = STP_ps_contract_type value = 0 compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = STP_ps_contract_type value = 3 compare = less_than }
			}
			clr_country_flag = VAL_cw_arms_contract_fulfilled
		}
		STP_ps_clear_val_receipt = yes
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = STP_ps_refund_rifles producer = VAL }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_contract_type value = 2 compare = equals } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment amount = 96 producer = VAL }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 60 producer = VAL }
			add_manpower = 12600
		}
		if = { limit = { STS = { exists = yes } }
			STS = {
				add_to_variable = {
					var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury
					value = PREV.STP_ps_refund_money
				}
				add_to_variable = {
					var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income
					value = PREV.STP_ps_refund_money
				}
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			}
		}
	}
}
STP_ps_dispatch_val_supply = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_val_receipt_rifles }
		if = { limit = { STP_ps_val_supply_open = no } STP_ps_refund_val_supply = yes }
		else_if = { limit = { STP_ps_val_land_route = yes }
			set_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_receipt_stage value = 2 }
			STP_ps_resolve_val_supply = yes
		}
		else = { activate_mission = STP_ps_val_route_wait }
	}
}
STP_ps_resolve_val_supply = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_val_receipt_rifles }
		if = { limit = { STP_ps_val_supply_open = no } STP_ps_refund_val_supply = yes }
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_cargo_rifles value = STP_ps_val_receipt_rifles }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_cargo_payment value = STP_ps_val_receipt_money }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_contract_type value = STP_ps_val_receipt_contract }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_cargo_intercepted value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_intercepted_sequence value = STP_ps_val_sequence compare = equals } STP = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_cargo_intercepted value = 1 }
			}
			STP_ps_clear_val_receipt = yes
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_cargo_intercepted value = 1 compare = equals } }
				STP = { add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = PREV.STP_ps_cargo_rifles producer = VAL } }
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_contract_type value = 2 compare = equals } }
					STP = { add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment amount = 96 producer = VAL } add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 60 producer = VAL } }
				}
			}
			else_if = { limit = { STS = { exists = yes has_war_with = STP } }
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_contract_type value = 0 compare = greater_than } check_variable = { var = STP_ps_contract_type value = 3 compare = less_than } }
					STP_ps_deliver_val_contract = yes
				}
				else = { STS = { add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = PREV.STP_ps_cargo_rifles producer = VAL } } }
			}
			else = {
				# Existing state cache follows the actual split and inspection accounting.
				1 = { add_to_variable = { var = STP_cw_cached_rifles value = PREV.STP_ps_cargo_rifles } }
				STP = { STP_cw_refresh_army_report = yes }
			}
			# Pay the stored receipt so in-flight agreements retain their signed price.
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = STP_ps_cargo_payment }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = STP_ps_cargo_payment }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			# Paid Shabrat contracts become fulfilled only after the convoy resolves.
			# Ordinary preparation shipments (type 0) and export cargo (type 3) do not.
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = STP_ps_contract_type value = 0 compare = greater_than }
					check_variable = { var = STP_ps_contract_type value = 3 compare = less_than }
				}
				# Old saves may already carry the premature fulfilled flag and rewards.
				# In that case resolve the escrow without granting them a second time.
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_cw_arms_contract_fulfilled } }
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_contract_type value = 2 compare = equals } }
						set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_cw_arms_contract_fulfilled value = 2 }
					}
					else = { set_country_flag = VAL_cw_arms_contract_fulfilled }
					army_experience = 5
					set_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_effect_value value = 5 }
					VAL_change_contract_authority = yes
					country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.51 }
				}
			}
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_contract_type value = 3 compare = equals } }
				if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = VAL_export_income_1 } } add_timed_idea = { idea = VAL_export_income_1 days = 70 } }
				else = { add_timed_idea = { idea = VAL_export_income_2 days = 70 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

STP_ps_val_intelligence_refund = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_val_intelligence_deposit }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_refund value = STP_ps_val_intelligence_deposit }
		clear_variable = STP_ps_val_intelligence_deposit
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = STP_ps_refund }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = STP_ps_refund }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}
STP_ps_val_intelligence_finish = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_val_intelligence_deposit }
		if = {
			limit = {
				STP_ps_val_intelligence_available = yes
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ps_val_intelligence_sequence
					value = VAL.STP_ps_val_sequence
					compare = equals
				}
				OR = {
					has_completed_focus = STP_ps_foreign_supply_desk
					has_completed_focus = STP_ps_counter_supply
				}
			}
			clear_variable = STP_ps_val_intelligence_deposit


			VAL = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_known_sequence value = STP_ps_val_sequence } }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		else = { STP_ps_val_intelligence_refund = yes }
	}
}

		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = STP_ps_refund }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}
STP_ps_val_intercept_finish = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_val_intercept_deposit }

			limit = {
				STP_ps_val_intelligence_available = yes
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ps_val_intercept_sequence
					value = VAL.STP_ps_val_sequence
					compare = equals
				}
				OR = {
					has_completed_focus = STP_ps_foreign_supply_desk
					has_completed_focus = STP_ps_counter_supply
				}
				VAL = {

						var = STP_ps_val_known_sequence
						value = STP_ps_val_sequence
						compare = equals
					}
					STP_ps_val_land_route = yes
				}
			}

			VAL = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_intercepted_sequence value = STP_ps_val_sequence } }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		else = { STP_ps_val_intercept_refund = yes }
	}
}

STP_ps_val_pressure_refund = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_val_pressure_deposit }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_refund value = STP_ps_val_pressure_deposit }
		clear_variable = STP_ps_val_pressure_deposit
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = STP_ps_refund }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = STP_ps_refund }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}
STP_ps_val_pressure_finish = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_val_pressure_deposit }
		if = { limit = { tag = STP OR = { STP_cw_preparation_open = yes STP_ps_war_active = yes } OR = { has_completed_focus = STP_ps_foreign_supply_desk has_completed_focus = STP_ps_counter_supply } VAL = { exists = yes } }
			clear_variable = STP_ps_val_pressure_deposit

			set_country_flag = { flag = STP_ps_val_pressure_cooldown value = 1 days = 60 }
			VAL = {
				set_country_flag = { flag = STP_ps_val_pressure_active value = 1 days = 60 }
				if = {
					limit = { has_active_mission = STP_ps_val_departure }
					add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_ps_val_departure days = 14 }
				}
			}
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		else = { STP_ps_val_pressure_refund = yes }
	}
}

STP_ps_deliver_val_contract = {
	STS = {
		# Deliver free reserves here; the full package also pays for the units below.
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 32000 producer = VAL }
		if = {
			limit = { VAL = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_contract_type value = 2 compare = equals } } }
			division_template = {
				name = "Kefreyt Contract Infantry"
				template_counter = 3
				is_locked = yes
				force_allow_recruiting = no
				override_model = ADISCORD_VAL_regular_entity
				division_names_group = VAL_INF_01
				regiments = {
					infantry = { x = 0 y = 0 }
					infantry = { x = 0 y = 1 }
					infantry = { x = 0 y = 2 }
					infantry = { x = 1 y = 0 }
					infantry = { x = 1 y = 1 }
					infantry = { x = 1 y = 2 }
				}
				support = { engineer = { x = 0 y = 0 } }
			}
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				create_unit = {
					division = "division_template = \"Kefreyt Contract Infantry\" start_experience_factor = 0.5 start_equipment_factor = 1.0 start_manpower_factor = 1.0"
					owner = PREV
					count = 2
					allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

STP_cw_schedule_opposition_second = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = STP
			has_country_flag = STP_ps_first_opposition_finished
			# Both networks operate before the election; stop opening the second late in the campaign.
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_active_mission = STP_cw_election_window }
				check_variable = { var = days_mission_timeout@STP_cw_election_window value = 56 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			STP_cw_preparation_open = yes
			has_country_flag = STP_battle_for_stelander_active
			has_country_flag = STP_sided_with_the_party_flag
			NOT = { has_active_mission = STP_ps_opposition_preparation_second }
		}
		2 = { clr_state_flag = STP_ps_opposition_target_second }
		3 = { clr_state_flag = STP_ps_opposition_target_second }
		29 = { clr_state_flag = STP_ps_opposition_target_second }
		46 = { clr_state_flag = STP_ps_opposition_target_second }
		53 = { clr_state_flag = STP_ps_opposition_target_second }
		random_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = { state = 2 state = 3 state = 29 state = 46 state = 53 }
				STP_region_is_operable = yes
				NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_cw_opposition_target }
				is_controlled_by = STP
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_administration_asset }
					NOT = { STP_region_resistance_entrenched = yes }
					AND = { OR = { state = 2 state = 29 } NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_garrison_asset } }
					AND = { OR = { state = 3 state = 46 } NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_supply_asset } }
					AND = { state = 53 NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_sabotage_asset } }
				}
			}
			set_state_flag = STP_ps_opposition_target_second
		}
		if = { limit = { any_owned_state = { has_state_flag = STP_ps_opposition_target_second } } activate_mission = STP_ps_opposition_preparation_second }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_first_opposition_announced } }
			set_country_flag = STP_cw_first_opposition_announced
			add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_ps_opposition_preparation_second days = 32 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = STP_apparatus_loyalty
				check_variable = { var = STP_apparatus_loyalty value = 70 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_ps_opposition_preparation_second days = 10 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = STP_apparatus_loyalty
				check_variable = { var = STP_apparatus_loyalty value = 25 compare = less_than }
			}
			add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_ps_opposition_preparation_second days = -7 }
		}
	}
}

STP_cw_prepare_opposition_second = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_cw_preparation_open = yes has_country_flag = STP_battle_for_stelander_active has_country_flag = STP_sided_with_the_party_flag }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = STP_ps_opposition_target_second STP_region_is_operable = yes is_controlled_by = STP }
			clr_state_flag = STP_ps_opposition_target_second
			# Administration must have existed before this operation to prepare arms.
			if = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_administration_asset }
				if = {
					limit = { OR = { state = 2 state = 29 } NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_garrison_asset } }
					set_state_flag = STP_resistance_garrison_asset
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { OR = { state = 3 state = 46 } NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_supply_asset } STP = { STP_cw_can_spend_1200 = yes } }
					STP = { STP_cw_spend_1200 = yes }
					set_state_flag = STP_resistance_supply_asset
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { state = 53 NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_sabotage_asset } STP = { NOT = { has_equipment = { infantry_equipment < 2400 } } } }
					STP = {
						set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_rifle_cost value = 2400 }
						STP_cw_pay_rifles = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = { STP = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_rifles_paid } }
						set_variable = { var = STP_cw_sabotage_rifles value = 2400 }
						set_state_flag = STP_resistance_sabotage_asset
					}
				}
			}
			STP = { add_power_balance_value = { id = STP_shabrat_election_legitimacy value = 0.04 } }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_region_influence_change value = 20 }
			STP_add_resistance_influence = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = STP_cw_loyalty_minus_6_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				STP = {
					set_temp_variable = { var = STP_apparatus_loyalty_change value = -6 }
					STP_change_apparatus_loyalty = yes
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { STP_region_resistance_entrenched = yes }
				if = { limit = { NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_administration_asset } } set_state_flag = STP_resistance_administration_asset }
			}
			log = "[STP preparation] Announced district operation completed in [THIS.GetName]"
		}
		# The election deadline alone triggers the split. A disrupted operation
		# still spends its announced time before the next target is selected.
		hidden_effect = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_preparation.5 days = 1 } }
		STP_cw_refresh_army_report = yes
	}
}

# STATE: only the state that holds the receipt can release the shared deposit.
STP_ps_refund_district = {
	if = { limit = { has_state_flag = STP_ps_district_paid }
		clr_state_flag = STP_ps_district_paid
		STP = { if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_district_deposit }
				set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_refund value = STP_ps_district_deposit }
				clear_variable = STP_ps_district_deposit
				add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = STP_ps_refund }
				add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = STP_ps_refund }
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		} }
	}
}

STP_ps_exile_formation_refund = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_exile_formation_deposit }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_refund value = STP_ps_exile_formation_deposit }
		clear_variable = STP_ps_exile_formation_deposit
		add_manpower = 6000
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 5400 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = STP_ps_refund }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = STP_ps_refund }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}
STP_ps_exile_formation_finish = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_exile_formation_deposit }
		if = { limit = { tag = NOD STP_ps_exile_formation_context = yes has_country_flag = STP_ps_exile_received }
			clear_variable = STP_ps_exile_formation_deposit

			set_country_flag = { flag = STP_ps_exile_formation_cooldown value = 1 days = 28 }
			STP_ps_deliver_exile_formation = yes
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		else = { STP_ps_exile_formation_refund = yes }
	}
}

STP_ps_deliver_exile_formation = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_template = "Party Exile Brigade" } }
		division_template = {
			name = "Party Exile Brigade"
			is_locked = yes
			force_allow_recruiting = no
			regiments = {
				ADISCORD_territorial = { x = 0 y = 0 }
				ADISCORD_territorial = { x = 0 y = 1 }
				ADISCORD_territorial = { x = 0 y = 2 }
				ADISCORD_territorial = { x = 1 y = 0 }
				ADISCORD_territorial = { x = 1 y = 1 }
				ADISCORD_territorial = { x = 1 y = 2 }
			}
		}
	}
	random_owned_controlled_state = {
		create_unit = {
			division = "division_template = \"Party Exile Brigade\" start_experience_factor = 0.1 start_equipment_factor = 1.0 start_manpower_factor = 1.0"
			owner = PREV
			allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
		}
	}
}

STP_ps_pay_nod_arms = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_rifle_cost value = 12000 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_rifle_before value = num_equipment@infantry_equipment }
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_artillery_before value = num_equipment@artillery_equipment }
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_support_before value = num_equipment@support_equipment }
	STP_cw_pay_rifles = yes
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_artillery_remaining value = 100 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_support_remaining value = 60 }
	every_possible_country = {
		limit = { PREV = { OR = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_artillery_remaining value = 0 compare = greater_than } check_variable = { var = STP_ps_support_remaining value = 0 compare = greater_than } } } }
		PREV = {
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_artillery_remaining value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_before value = num_equipment@artillery_equipment }
				set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_debit value = STP_ps_artillery_remaining }
				multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_debit value = -1 }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment amount = STP_ps_debit producer = PREV }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_before value = num_equipment@artillery_equipment }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_artillery_remaining value = STP_ps_before }
			}
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_support_remaining value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_before value = num_equipment@support_equipment }
				set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_debit value = STP_ps_support_remaining }
				multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_debit value = -1 }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = STP_ps_debit producer = PREV }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_before value = num_equipment@support_equipment }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_support_remaining value = STP_ps_before }
			}
		}
	}
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_rifles_paid check_variable = { var = STP_ps_artillery_remaining value = 0 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = STP_ps_support_remaining value = 0 compare = equals } }
		clr_country_flag = STP_cw_rifles_paid
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 300 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 300 }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		set_variable = { var = STP_ps_nod_receipt_type value = 2 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_ps_nod_receipt_money value = 300 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_ps_nod_receipt_stage value = 1 }
		activate_mission = STP_ps_nod_dispatch
		add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_ps_nod_dispatch days = 13 }
	}
	else = {
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_rifle_before value = num_equipment@infantry_equipment }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_artillery_before value = num_equipment@artillery_equipment }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_support_before value = num_equipment@support_equipment }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = STP_ps_rifle_before }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment amount = STP_ps_artillery_before }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = STP_ps_support_before }
	}
}

STP_ps_authorize_val_supply = {
	set_country_flag = STP_ps_val_authorized
}

STP_ps_request_asylum = {
	if = { limit = { NOD = { STP_ps_nod_can_host_exiles = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_asylum_requested } } }
		NOD = { set_country_flag = STP_ps_asylum_requested country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.204 } }
	}
}

# STS buyer; the existing acceptance gate has checked both parties and the price.
STP_ps_reserve_export_contract = {
	if = { limit = { VAL = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_ps_val_receipt_rifles } } }
		VAL = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_rifle_cost value = 25000 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_partial_refund value = num_equipment@infantry_equipment }
			STP_cw_pay_rifles = yes
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_rifles_paid } }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_partial_refund value = num_equipment@infantry_equipment }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = STP_ps_partial_refund producer = VAL }
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { VAL = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_rifles_paid } }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_before value = ADISCORD_economy_treasury }
			ADISCORD_economy_spend_100 = yes
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_before value = ADISCORD_economy_treasury }
			VAL = {
				clr_country_flag = STP_cw_rifles_paid
				set_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_receipt_money value = PREV.STP_ps_before }
				set_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_receipt_rifles value = 25000 }
				set_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_receipt_contract value = 3 }
				set_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_receipt_stage value = 1 }
				add_to_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_sequence value = 1 }
				activate_mission = STP_ps_val_departure
			}
		}
	}
}

# NOD: settle fixed-price exile orders before closing or restoring the party.
STP_ps_close_exile = {
	STP_ps_exile_formation_refund = yes
	STP_ps_exile_training_refund = yes
	remove_mission = STP_ps_exile_formation_work
	remove_mission = STP_ps_exile_training_work
	clr_country_flag = STP_ps_exile_received
	clr_country_flag = STP_ps_return_campaign
	set_country_flag = STP_ps_return_closed
	if = { limit = { has_template = "Party Exile Brigade" }
		set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Party Exile Brigade" is_locked = no }
	}
	mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
}

# STP: both northern preparations share a single refundable treasury receipt.
STP_pw_party_refund_northern_work = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = STP_pw_party_north_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = STP_pw_party_north_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = STP_pw_party_north_deposit }
		clear_variable = STP_pw_party_north_deposit
		clear_variable = STP_pw_party_north_work_kind
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

STP_pw_party_finish_northern_work = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = STP_pw_party_north_deposit }
		if = {
			limit = { STP_pw_party_northern_work_current = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pw_party_north_work_kind value = 1 compare = equals } }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pw_party_northern_network value = 1 }
				add_intel = { target = NOD civilian_intel = 20 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pw_party_north_work_kind value = 2 compare = equals } }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pw_party_northern_supply value = 1 }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 6000 producer = ROOT }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 60 producer = ROOT }
				army_experience = 20
			}
			clear_variable = STP_pw_party_north_deposit
			clear_variable = STP_pw_party_north_work_kind
		}
		else = { STP_pw_party_refund_northern_work = yes }
	}
}

STP_pw_party_close_northern_operations = {
	STP_pw_party_refund_northern_work = yes
	clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_congress_pending
	clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_congress_refused
	clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_campaign_active
	clear_variable = STP_pw_party_northern_supply
	clear_variable = STP_pw_party_northern_network
}

# NOD: an open window cannot override a later settlement or a new overlord.
STP_pw_party_accept_nod_congress = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_pw_party_nod_congress_current = yes }
		STP = { STP_pw_party_begin_nod_subject = yes }
	}
}

# STP: refusal is also the unanswered-offer outcome; it never declares war.
STP_pw_party_refuse_nod_congress = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_congress_pending }
		clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_congress_pending
		if = {
			limit = { STP_pw_party_northern_work_current = yes }
			set_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_congress_refused
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pc.42 hours = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# STP: clear the invasion before white peace triggers reconciliation. Subject
# creation waits a day so the former faction is absent from the diplomatic cache.
STP_pw_party_begin_nod_subject = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_pw_party_revolution_current = yes
			NOD = { exists = yes is_subject = no }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_subject_pending }
		}
		set_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_subject_pending
		STP_ch_close_pressure = yes
		STP_pw_party_close_northern_operations = yes
		clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_threat_active
		clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_compact_pending
		clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_invasion_active
		set_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_invasion_defeated
		remove_decision = STP_pw_party_nod_invasion_countdown
		if = { limit = { has_war_with = NOD } white_peace = NOD }
		NOD = {
			remove_ideas = STP_pw_nod_invasion_mandate
			if = {
				limit = { is_in_faction = yes }
				if = { limit = { is_faction_leader = yes } dismantle_faction = yes }
				else = { faction_leader = { remove_from_faction = NOD } }
			}
		}
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pc.43 days = 1 }
	}
}

STP_pw_party_finalize_nod_subject = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_subject_pending }
		clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_subject_pending
		if = {
			limit = { STP_pw_party_revolution_current = yes NOD = { exists = yes is_subject = no } NOT = { has_war_with = NOD } }
			puppet = {
				target = NOD
				end_wars = no
				end_civil_wars = no
			}
			set_autonomy = { target = NOD autonomy_state = autonomy_puppet freedom_level = 0.10 end_wars = no end_civil_wars = no }
			NOD = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
			STP_ch_open_congress = yes
			NOD = { STP_pw_party_sync_revolution_subject = yes }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pc.42 hours = 1 }
		}
	}
}

STP_pw_party_win_nod_campaign = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_pw_party_revolution_current = yes has_completed_focus = STP_pw_party_nod_congress has_war_with = NOD NOD = { is_subject = no } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_ch_liberation_war } STP_pw_party_settle_nod_invasion_defeat = yes }
		else = { STP_pw_party_begin_nod_subject = yes }
	}
}

STP_pw_party_launch_northern_campaign = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_pw_party_can_launch_northern_campaign = yes }
		set_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_campaign_active
		clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_congress_pending
		clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_congress_refused
		clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_threat_active
		clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_compact_pending
		remove_decision = STP_pw_party_nod_invasion_countdown
		declare_war_on = { target = NOD type = puppet_wargoal_focus }
	}
}

# Called once after a peace callback, outside the settlement that raised it.
STP_pw_party_reconcile_northern_campaign = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_campaign_active
			OR = { NOT = { has_war_with = NOD } STP_pw_party_revolution_current = no NOD = { OR = { exists = no is_subject = yes } } }
		}
		STP_pw_party_close_northern_operations = yes
	}
}

# --- occidia_reintegration ---
STP_occidia_start_campaign = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = STP_occidia_preparing STP_occidia_invasion_available = yes }
		set_country_flag = STP_occidia_campaign_active
		clr_country_flag = STP_occidia_preparing
		SRP = { remove_mission = STP_occidia_invasion_warning }
		declare_war_on = { target = SRP type = annex_everything }
	}
	else = { STP_occidia_cancel_preparation = yes }
}

STP_occidia_cancel_preparation = {
	clr_country_flag = STP_occidia_preparing
	SRP = { remove_mission = STP_occidia_invasion_warning }
}

STP_occidia_settle_victory = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_occidia_victory_ready = yes }
		clr_country_flag = STP_occidia_campaign_active
		SRP = { STP_cw_finish_mobilization = yes remove_mission = STP_occidia_invasion_warning }
		annex_country = { target = SRP transfer_troops = no }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

# A peace imposed elsewhere or the attacker's defeat closes this campaign too.
STP_occidia_reconcile_peace = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = STP_occidia_campaign_active NOT = { has_war_with = SRP } }
		clr_country_flag = STP_occidia_campaign_active
	}
}


# COUNTRY STP: snapshot institutional preparation and faction cooperation at payment.

			limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_reorg_1_specialization_days }
			add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_ps_reorg_1 days = STP_ps_reorg_1_specialization_days }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = STP_pf_initialized check_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_support value = 35 compare = less_than } }
			add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_ps_reorg_1 days = 7 }
		}
	}
}

# A country event establishes STP as ROOT before entering the shared settlement.

STP_cw_resolve_fada_capture = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_cw_fada_captured = yes }
		STP = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.205 } }
	}
}

# STS: the council's second defeat ends independence, not merely the expedition.
STP_pc_annex_defeated_council = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = STS VAL = { exists = yes has_country_flag = VAL_council_revanche_active } }
		VAL = { clr_country_flag = VAL_council_revanche_active }
		if = { limit = { has_war_with = VAL } white_peace = VAL }
		annex_country = { target = VAL transfer_troops = no }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

# Idempotent staff delivery. The receipt survives retirement and annexation.
STP_qol_initialize_staff = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_qol_staff_available = yes NOT = { has_global_flag = STP_qol_regular_officers_delivered } }
		set_global_flag = STP_qol_regular_officers_delivered
		if = {
			limit = { tag = STP has_character = STP_Pavel_Gromin }
			add_corps_commander_role = {
				character = STP_Pavel_Gromin
				traits = { infantry_leader }
				skill = 2
				attack_skill = 2
				defense_skill = 2
				planning_skill = 2
				logistics_skill = 2
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { tag = STS has_character = STS_Pavel_Gromin }
			add_corps_commander_role = {
				character = STS_Pavel_Gromin
				traits = { infantry_leader }
				skill = 2
				attack_skill = 2
				defense_skill = 2
				planning_skill = 2
				logistics_skill = 2
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { tag = STP has_character = STP_Elena_Vetrova }
			add_corps_commander_role = {
				character = STP_Elena_Vetrova
				traits = { organizer }
				skill = 2
				attack_skill = 2
				defense_skill = 2
				planning_skill = 2
				logistics_skill = 2
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { tag = STS has_character = STS_Elena_Vetrova }
			add_corps_commander_role = {
				character = STS_Elena_Vetrova
				traits = { organizer }
				skill = 2
				attack_skill = 2
				defense_skill = 2
				planning_skill = 2
				logistics_skill = 2
			}
		}
	}
	if = { limit = { STP_qol_elite_army_available = yes } STP_qol_grant_elite_officer = yes }
}

# Called directly by completion rewards; never tests its own finishing focus.
STP_qol_grant_elite_officer = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_qol_staff_available = yes NOT = { has_global_flag = STP_qol_elite_officer_delivered } }
		set_global_flag = STP_qol_elite_officer_delivered
		if = {
			limit = { tag = STP has_character = STP_Oleg_Vershin }
			add_corps_commander_role = {
				character = STP_Oleg_Vershin
				traits = { logistics_wizard }
				skill = 4
				attack_skill = 4
				defense_skill = 4
				planning_skill = 4
				logistics_skill = 4
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { tag = STS has_character = STS_Oleg_Vershin }
			add_corps_commander_role = {
				character = STS_Oleg_Vershin
				traits = { logistics_wizard }
				skill = 4
				attack_skill = 4
				defense_skill = 4
				planning_skill = 4
				logistics_skill = 4
			}
		}
	}
}

# Sotnikov leads the legal humanists before the split; his military-directory
# role remains owned by the existing civil-war/postwar story.
STP_qol_initialize_party_leaders = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = STP
			NOT = { has_global_flag = STP_cw_started }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_participant }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_postwar }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_qol_prewar_leaders_initialized }
			NOT = { has_government = humanism }
			NOT = { has_government = etatism }
		}
		set_country_flag = STP_qol_prewar_leaders_initialized
		if = {
			limit = { has_character = STP_grigory_sotnikov }
			STP_grigory_sotnikov = { remove_country_leader_role = { ideology = steland_military_directory } }
			add_country_leader_role = { character = STP_grigory_sotnikov promote_leader = yes country_leader = { ideology = humanism_ideology expire = "2200.1.1.1" } }
			promote_character = { character = STP_grigory_sotnikov ideology = humanism_ideology }
		}
	}
}


# Shabrat hegemony endgame.
# The first campaign clears independent northern governments. Kefreyt is kept
# for a separate second campaign unless another alliance has already dragged it in.
STP_heg_start_northern_final_war = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = STS
			check_variable = { var = STP_pc_course value = 1 compare = equals }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_heg_northern_final_started }
		}
		set_country_flag = STP_heg_northern_final_started

		# Break outside dependency links before the northern declarations. This keeps
		# Kefreyt out of the first campaign even if it previously subordinated Yubor
		# or the Frontier through its own contract path.
		NOD = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes is_subject = yes NOT = { is_subject_of = STS } }
				overlord = { set_autonomy = { target = NOD autonomy_state = autonomy_free end_wars = no end_civil_wars = no } }
			}
			if = { limit = { exists = yes is_in_faction = yes NOT = { is_in_faction_with = STS } } leave_faction = yes }
		}
		YPR = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes is_subject = yes NOT = { is_subject_of = STS } }
				overlord = { set_autonomy = { target = YPR autonomy_state = autonomy_free end_wars = no end_civil_wars = no } }
			}
			if = { limit = { exists = yes is_in_faction = yes NOT = { is_in_faction_with = STS } } leave_faction = yes }
		}
		TFF = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes is_subject = yes NOT = { is_subject_of = STS } }
				overlord = { set_autonomy = { target = TFF autonomy_state = autonomy_free end_wars = no end_civil_wars = no } }
			}
			if = { limit = { exists = yes is_in_faction = yes NOT = { is_in_faction_with = STS } } leave_faction = yes }
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				NOD = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no is_subject = no NOT = { is_in_faction_with = STS } }
				NOT = { has_war_with = NOD }
			}
			declare_war_on = { target = NOD type = annex_everything }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				YPR = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no is_subject = no NOT = { is_in_faction_with = STS } }
				NOT = { has_war_with = YPR }
			}
			declare_war_on = { target = YPR type = annex_everything }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				TFF = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no is_subject = no NOT = { is_in_faction_with = STS } }
				NOT = { has_war_with = TFF }
			}
			declare_war_on = { target = TFF type = annex_everything }
		}
	}
}

STP_heg_start_kefreyt_final_war = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = STS
			check_variable = { var = STP_pc_course value = 1 compare = equals }
			STP_heg_northern_final_resolved = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_heg_kefreyt_final_started }
		}
		set_country_flag = STP_heg_kefreyt_final_started
		VAL = { if = { limit = { exists = yes } set_country_flag = STP_heg_kefreyt_final_member } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				VAL = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no is_subject = no NOT = { is_in_faction_with = STS } }
				NOT = { has_war_with = VAL }
			}
			declare_war_on = { target = VAL type = annex_everything }
		}
	}
}

STP_heg_prepare_nod_administration = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOD = { exists = yes } }
		NOD = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_subject = yes NOT = { is_subject_of = STS } }
				overlord = { set_autonomy = { target = NOD autonomy_state = autonomy_free end_wars = no end_civil_wars = no } }
			}
			if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes NOT = { is_in_faction_with = STS } } leave_faction = yes }
		}
		set_autonomy = { target = NOD autonomy_state = autonomy_STP_provisional_administration freedom_level = 0.05 end_wars = no end_civil_wars = no }
		NOD = { set_country_flag = STP_heg_provisional_administration }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { NOD = { exists = yes } } any_owned_state = { is_core_of = NOD } }
		release_autonomy = { target = NOD autonomy_state = autonomy_STP_provisional_administration freedom_level = 0.05 }
		NOD = { set_country_flag = STP_heg_provisional_administration }
	}
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

STP_heg_prepare_ypr_administration = {
	if = {
		limit = { YPR = { exists = yes } }
		YPR = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_subject = yes NOT = { is_subject_of = STS } }
				overlord = { set_autonomy = { target = YPR autonomy_state = autonomy_free end_wars = no end_civil_wars = no } }
			}
			if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes NOT = { is_in_faction_with = STS } } leave_faction = yes }
		}
		set_autonomy = { target = YPR autonomy_state = autonomy_STP_provisional_administration freedom_level = 0.05 end_wars = no end_civil_wars = no }
		YPR = { set_country_flag = STP_heg_provisional_administration }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { YPR = { exists = yes } } any_owned_state = { is_core_of = YPR } }
		release_autonomy = { target = YPR autonomy_state = autonomy_STP_provisional_administration freedom_level = 0.05 }
		YPR = { set_country_flag = STP_heg_provisional_administration }
	}
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

STP_heg_prepare_tff_administration = {
	if = {

		TFF = {

				limit = { is_subject = yes NOT = { is_subject_of = STS } }

			}

		}
		set_autonomy = { target = TFF autonomy_state = autonomy_STP_provisional_administration freedom_level = 0.05 end_wars = no end_civil_wars = no }
		TFF = { set_country_flag = STP_heg_provisional_administration }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { TFF = { exists = yes } } any_owned_state = { is_core_of = TFF } }
		release_autonomy = { target = TFF autonomy_state = autonomy_STP_provisional_administration freedom_level = 0.05 }
		TFF = { set_country_flag = STP_heg_provisional_administration }
	}
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

STP_heg_prepare_val_administration = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAL = { exists = yes } }
		VAL = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_subject = yes NOT = { is_subject_of = STS } }
				overlord = { set_autonomy = { target = VAL autonomy_state = autonomy_free end_wars = no end_civil_wars = no } }
			}
			if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes NOT = { is_in_faction_with = STS } } leave_faction = yes }
		}

		VAL = { set_country_flag = STP_heg_provisional_administration }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { VAL = { exists = yes } } any_owned_state = { is_core_of = VAL } }
		release_autonomy = { target = VAL autonomy_state = autonomy_STP_provisional_administration freedom_level = 0.05 }
		VAL = { set_country_flag = STP_heg_provisional_administration }
	}
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}


# STS: the hegemony demand becomes a real subject relationship after Nodrul's
# old faction has had one day to leave the diplomatic cache.
STP_pc_finalize_nod_client_subject = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = STS
			has_country_flag = STP_pc_nod_terms_accepted
			NOD = { exists = yes has_country_flag = STP_pc_nod_client_pending NOT = { has_war_with = STS } }
		}
		puppet = NOD
		set_autonomy = {
			target = NOD
			autonomy_state = autonomy_puppet
			freedom_level = 0.10
			end_wars = no
			end_civil_wars = no
		}
		NOD = {
			clr_country_flag = STP_pc_nod_client_pending
			add_ideas = STP_pc_nod_client
		}
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_pc_hegemony_league } STP_pc_form_hegemony_league = yes }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

# STS: the final war imposes a provisional administration after the technical
# peace. Full integration remains a separate paid operation.
STP_heg_settle_kefreyt_final = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = STS
			has_country_flag = STP_heg_kefreyt_final_started
			has_war_with = VAL
		}
		STP_pc_recover_stelander_cores_from_val = yes
		VAL = {
			set_country_flag = STP_heg_defeated_by_sts
			clear_variable = STP_cw_capitulation_occupier
		}
		every_enemy_country = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = STP_heg_kefreyt_final_member }
			clr_country_flag = STP_heg_kefreyt_final_member
			white_peace = STS
		}
		if = { limit = { has_war_with = VAL } white_peace = VAL }
		STP_heg_prepare_val_administration = yes
		clr_country_flag = STP_heg_kefreyt_final_started
		set_country_flag = STP_heg_kefreyt_final_won
		ADISCORD_economy_update_postwar_demobilization = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}


# COUNTRY STP: terminal settlement for the Party's independent Kefreyt campaign.
STP_pw_party_settle_kefreyt_victory = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = STP has_country_flag = STP_pw_party_kefreyt_campaign_active VAL = { exists = yes } }
		clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_kefreyt_campaign_active
		set_country_flag = STP_pw_party_kefreyt_subject_pending
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_core_of = STP
					is_core_of = STS
					state = 42
					state = 52
					state = 55
				}
				OR = {
					is_owned_by = VAL
					owner = { is_subject_of = VAL }
				}
			}
			add_core_of = STP
			transfer_state_to = STP
			set_state_controller_to = STP
		}
		VAL = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_in_faction = yes }
				if = {
					limit = { is_faction_leader = yes }
					dismantle_faction = yes
				}
				else = { faction_leader = { remove_from_faction = VAL } }
			}
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		if = { limit = { has_war_with = VAL } white_peace = VAL }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		# Advance the native faction cache before creating the subject.
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pc.46 days = 1 }
	}
}

STP_pw_party_finalize_kefreyt_subject = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = STP_pw_party_kefreyt_subject_pending }
		clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_kefreyt_subject_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				tag = STP
				STP_pw_can_reconstruct = yes
				is_subject = no
				VAL = { exists = yes is_subject = no }
				NOT = { has_war_with = VAL }
			}
			puppet = {
				target = VAL
				end_wars = no
				end_civil_wars = no
			}
			set_autonomy = {
				target = VAL
				autonomy_state = autonomy_puppet
				freedom_level = 0.10
				end_wars = no
				end_civil_wars = no
			}
			set_country_flag = STP_pw_party_kefreyt_victory
			VAL = { STP_pw_party_sync_revolution_subject = yes }
			add_political_power = 75
			add_war_support = 0.03
			VAL = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY NOD: consent, transfer and threat closure form one guarded settlement.
STP_pw_party_settle_compact = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_pw_party_compact_current = yes }
		ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 1350 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 1350 }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		STP = {
			subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 1350 }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 1350 }
			clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_compact_pending
			clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_threat_active
			clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_invasion_active
			set_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_compact
			# Clear the cause before the mission's cancellation callback can run.
			remove_decision = STP_pw_party_nod_invasion_countdown
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pc.37 hours = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY STP/STS: specialist templates recruit through the ordinary queue.
STP_pw_open_mountain_school = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = STP_pw_mountain_template_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_template = "Горнострелковая бригада Стеландера" } }
			if = {
				limit = { tag = STP }
				division_template = {
					name = "Горнострелковая бригада Стеландера"
					template_counter = 110
					override_model = ADISCORD_STP_mountaineer_entity
					is_locked = no
					force_allow_recruiting = yes
					regiments = {
						mountaineers = { x = 0 y = 0 }
						mountaineers = { x = 0 y = 1 }
						mountaineers = { x = 0 y = 2 }
						mountaineers = { x = 1 y = 0 }
						mountaineers = { x = 1 y = 1 }
						mountaineers = { x = 1 y = 2 }
					}
					support = { engineer = { x = 0 y = 0 } }
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { tag = STS }
				division_template = {
					name = "Горнострелковая бригада Стеландера"
					template_counter = 110
					override_model = ADISCORD_STS_mountaineer_entity
					is_locked = no
					force_allow_recruiting = yes
					regiments = {
						mountaineers = { x = 0 y = 0 }
						mountaineers = { x = 0 y = 1 }
						mountaineers = { x = 0 y = 2 }
						mountaineers = { x = 1 y = 0 }
						mountaineers = { x = 1 y = 1 }
						mountaineers = { x = 1 y = 2 }
					}
					support = { engineer = { x = 0 y = 0 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# STP: sovereignty changes the state identity independently of constitutional BOP.
STP_pw_party_sync_revolution_identity = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_ch_current = yes
			is_subject = no
		}
		set_cosmetic_tag = STP_revolution_capital
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_cosmetic_tag = STP_revolution_capital }
		drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
	}
	every_subject_country = { STP_pw_party_sync_revolution_subject = yes }
}

# Subject scope: apply only to the independent revolutionary centre's dependants.
# The autonomy guard prevents a repeated on_puppet callback from reapplying it.
STP_pw_party_sync_revolution_subject = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_subject_of = STP
			STP = {
				exists = yes
				is_subject = no
				has_cosmetic_tag = STP_revolution_capital
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_autonomy_state = autonomy_shadow_state } }
			STP = {
				set_autonomy = {
					target = PREV
					autonomy_state = autonomy_shadow_state
					freedom_level = 0.35
					end_wars = no
					end_civil_wars = no
				}
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			}
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { tag = NOD }
			set_cosmetic_tag = NOD_STP_revolution
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_leader = { character = NOD_Edgar_Renner } } }
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_character = NOD_Edgar_Renner } }
					recruit_character = NOD_Edgar_Renner
				}
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = hedonism
					elections_allowed = no
				}
				add_country_leader_role = {
					character = NOD_Edgar_Renner
					country_leader = {
						ideology = hedonism_ideology
						expire = "2300.1.1"
					}
				}
				promote_character = {
					character = NOD_Edgar_Renner
					ideology = hedonism_ideology
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { tag = VAL }
			set_cosmetic_tag = VAL_STP_revolution
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { tag = BJK }
			set_cosmetic_tag = BJK_STP_revolution
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_cosmetic_tag = NOD_STP_revolution
				has_cosmetic_tag = VAL_STP_revolution
				has_cosmetic_tag = BJK_STP_revolution
			}
		}
		drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
	}
}

STP_pw_party_initialize_constitution = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_pw_party_constitution_initialized } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_power_balance = { id = STP_shabrat_election_legitimacy } }
			remove_power_balance = { id = STP_shabrat_election_legitimacy }
		}
		set_power_balance = { id = STP_party_constitution set_value = 0 }
		set_country_flag = STP_pw_party_constitution_initialized
		STP_pw_party_refresh_constitution = yes
		set_cosmetic_tag = STP_revolution_capital
		every_subject_country = { STP_pw_party_sync_revolution_subject = yes }
		news_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pc.47 hours = 1 }
	}
}

STP_pw_party_refresh_constitution = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_pw_party_constitution_open = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pw_party_constitution_target value = STP_pf_radicals_influence }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_pw_party_constitution_target value = STP_pf_conservatives_influence }
		divide_temp_variable = { var = STP_pw_party_constitution_target value = 100 }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = STP_pw_party_constitution_target min = -0.60 max = 0.60 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_pw_party_constitution_target value = power_balance_value }
		# Этот эффект принимает число, поэтому подставляем рассчитанный сдвиг через meta_effect.
		meta_effect = {
			text = {
				add_power_balance_value = { id = STP_party_constitution value = [DELTA] }
			}
			DELTA = "[?STP_pw_party_constitution_target|.3]"
		}
	}
}

# Law swaps must finish before reading the new idea group; coalesce both callbacks.
STP_pw_party_queue_constitution_refresh = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_pw_party_constitution_open = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_pw_party_constitution_refresh_pending }
		}
		set_country_flag = STP_pw_party_constitution_refresh_pending
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pc.45 hours = 1 }
	}
}

STP_pw_party_clear_constitution = {
	clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_constitution_initialized
	if = {
		limit = { has_cosmetic_tag = STP_revolution_capital }
		drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
	}
	every_subject_country = { STP_pw_party_sync_revolution_subject = yes }
	clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_constitution_refresh_pending
	if = {
		limit = { has_power_balance = { id = STP_party_constitution } }
		remove_power_balance = { id = STP_party_constitution }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = STP_law_slavery }
		swap_ideas = { remove_idea = STP_law_slavery add_idea = ADISCORD_labor_policy_loose_contracts }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { has_idea = STP_law_public_orgies has_idea = STP_law_private_societies } }
		remove_ideas = { STP_law_public_orgies STP_law_private_societies }
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_cultural_policy_public_entertainment
	}
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { has_idea = STP_law_light_drugs has_idea = STP_law_hard_drugs } }
		add_ideas = STP_law_drug_prohibition
	}
	remove_ideas = {
		STP_pw_party_high_houses_government
		STP_pw_party_unbound_government
	}
}

# Political chapters: external settlement, congress, programme and inherited obligations.

STP_ch_initialize = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_pf_active = yes
			STP_pw_can_reconstruct = yes
			NOT = {
				has_variable = STP_ch_phase
			}
		}
		STP_pf_initialize = yes
		set_variable = { var = STP_ch_pressure_target value = 2 }
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_phase
			value = 1
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_nominee
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_cooperation
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_endorse_conservatives
			value = 1
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_endorse_borons
			value = 1
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_endorse_security
			value = 5
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_endorse_army
			value = 3
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_endorse_advisers
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_endorse_merchants
			value = 4
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_endorse_radicals
			value = 2
		}
		set_country_flag = STP_ch_army_access
		set_country_flag = STP_ch_economy_access
		set_country_flag = STP_ch_party_access
		STP_ch_refresh = yes
		STP_ch_select_ai_nominee = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_subject_of = NOD
			}
			STP_ch_enter_protectorate = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				NOD = {
					exists = yes
					is_subject = no
					has_capitulated = no
				}
			}
			STP_pw_party_start_nod_invasion_threat = yes
		}
		else = {
			STP_ch_open_congress = yes
		}
	}
}

			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pv_outcome value = 1 compare = equals } }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_ch_votes_1 value = 10 }
		}

			add_to_variable = { var = STP_ch_votes_5 value = 10 }
		}
	}
}

STP_ch_refresh = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_ch_current = yes
		}
		STP_ch_recount = yes
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_unpaid_promises
			value = 0
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = STP_ch_government
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_pledge_conservatives
					value = STP_ch_government
					compare = equals
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = STP_ch_fulfilled_conservatives
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_unpaid_promises
				value = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = STP_ch_government
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_pledge_borons
					value = STP_ch_government
					compare = equals
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = STP_ch_fulfilled_borons
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_unpaid_promises
				value = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = STP_ch_government
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_pledge_security
					value = STP_ch_government
					compare = equals
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = STP_ch_fulfilled_security
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_unpaid_promises
				value = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = STP_ch_government
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_pledge_army
					value = STP_ch_government
					compare = equals
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = STP_ch_fulfilled_army
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_unpaid_promises
				value = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = STP_ch_government
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_pledge_advisers
					value = STP_ch_government
					compare = equals
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = STP_ch_fulfilled_advisers
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_unpaid_promises
				value = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = STP_ch_government
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_pledge_merchants
					value = STP_ch_government
					compare = equals
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = STP_ch_fulfilled_merchants
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_unpaid_promises
				value = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = STP_ch_government
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_pledge_radicals
					value = STP_ch_government
					compare = equals
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = STP_ch_fulfilled_radicals
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_unpaid_promises
				value = 1
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_army_capacity
			value = 0
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				STP_ch_army_ready = yes
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_army_capacity
				value = 1
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_economy_capacity
			value = 0
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				STP_ch_economy_ready = yes
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_economy_capacity
				value = 1
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_party_capacity
			value = 0
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				STP_ch_party_ready = yes
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_party_capacity
				value = 1
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_organization
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_output
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_trade
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_administration
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_stability
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_political_power
			value = 0
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = STP_ch_army_access
				}
				STP_ch_army_ready = no
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_organization
				value = -0.15
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = STP_ch_economy_access
				}
				STP_ch_economy_ready = no
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_output
				value = -0.2
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = STP_ch_party_access
				}
				STP_ch_party_ready = no
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_administration
				value = 0.15
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				STP_ch_cold = yes
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_escalation
					value = 2
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
				has_country_flag = STP_ch_economy_access
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_trade
				value = -0.15
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = STP_ch_fulfilled_merchants
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_trade
				value = 0.05
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = STP_ch_fulfilled_borons
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_administration
				value = -0.04
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = STP_ch_fulfilled_conservatives
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_stability
				value = 0.02
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = STP_ch_fulfilled_radicals
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_stability
				value = 0.02
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_administration
				value = 0.03
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_security_ministry
					value = 1
					compare = equals
				}
				STP_ch_army_ready = yes
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_organization
				value = 0.03
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = STP_ch_military_veto
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_political_power
				value = -0.1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_dynamic_modifier = {
						modifier = STP_ch_institutions
					}
				}
			}
			add_dynamic_modifier = {
				modifier = STP_ch_institutions
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_completed_focus = STP_ch_production_agreements
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_output
				value = 0.12
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_completed_focus = STP_ch_market_charter
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_trade
				value = 0.12
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_completed_focus = STP_ch_security_directorate
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_stability
				value = 0.08
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_administration
				value = 0.05
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_completed_focus = STP_ch_register_of_powers
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_political_power
				value = 0.12
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = STP_ch_security_veto
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_political_power
				value = -0.1
			}
		}
		force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

STP_ch_close_pressure = {
	# Invalidate the threat before remove_mission can dispatch cancel_effect.
	clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_threat_active
	clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_compact_pending
	clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_offer_pending
	clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_offer_accepted
	remove_mission = STP_ch_pressure_1
	remove_mission = STP_ch_pressure_2
	remove_mission = STP_ch_pressure_3
	remove_mission = STP_ch_pressure_4
	remove_mission = STP_pw_party_nod_invasion_countdown
}

STP_ch_start_pressure = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_ch_pressure_current = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_variable = STP_ch_escalation
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_escalation
				value = 1
			}
			activate_mission = STP_ch_pressure_1
			country_event = {
				id = ADISCORD_STP_ch.2
				hours = 1
			}
		}
	}
}

STP_ch_escalate_1 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_ch_pressure_current = yes
			check_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_escalation
				value = 1
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_escalation
			value = 2
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = STP_ch_economy_access
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_pressure_target
				value = 2
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = STP_ch_party_access
				}
				set_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_pressure_target
					value = 3
				}
				set_temp_variable = {
					var = STP_pf_selected
					value = 5
				}
				STP_pf_shift = yes
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_pressure_target
					value = 1
				}
			}
		}
		activate_mission = STP_ch_pressure_2
		STP_ch_refresh = yes
		country_event = {
			id = ADISCORD_STP_ch.2
			hours = 1
		}
	}
}
