STP_ch_issue_invasion = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_ch_pressure_current = yes
			check_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_escalation
				value = 4
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		STP_pw_party_launch_nod_invasion = yes
	}
}

STP_ch_open_congress = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_ch_current = yes
			is_subject = no
			NOT = {
				has_war_with = NOD
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = STP_ch_resume_phase
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_phase
				value = STP_ch_resume_phase
			}
			clear_variable = STP_ch_resume_phase
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = STP_ch_phase
						value = 3
						compare = equals
					}
					has_country_flag = STP_ch_ballot_closed
				}
				country_event = {
					id = ADISCORD_STP_ch.6
					hours = 1
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_phase
				value = 3
			}
			country_event = {
				id = ADISCORD_STP_ch.3
				hours = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = STP_ch_protectorate_leader
			}
			clr_country_flag = STP_ch_protectorate_leader
			STP_ch_restore_independent_leader = yes
		}
		STP_pw_party_sync_revolution_identity = yes
		STP_ch_close_pressure = yes
		clr_country_flag = STP_ch_release_pending
		clr_country_flag = STP_ch_liberation_war
		STP_ch_resume_crisis = yes
		country_event = {
			id = ADISCORD_STP_ch.1
			hours = 1
		}
		STP_ch_refresh = yes
	}
}

STP_ch_enter_protectorate = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_ch_current = yes
			NOT = {
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_phase
					value = 2
					compare = equals
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_phase
					value = 2
					compare = greater_than
				}
				NOT = {
					has_variable = STP_ch_resume_phase
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_resume_phase
				value = STP_ch_phase
			}
		}
		STP_ch_pause_crisis = yes
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_phase
			value = 2
		}
		remove_mission = STP_ch_crisis_deadline
		STP_ch_close_pressure = yes
		clr_country_flag = STP_ch_liberation_war
		clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_invasion_active
		clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_campaign_active
		clr_country_flag = STP_ch_release_pending
		country_event = {
			id = ADISCORD_STP_ch.1
			hours = 1
		}
		STP_ch_install_protectorate_leader = yes
		STP_ch_refresh = yes
		country_event = {
			id = ADISCORD_STP_ch.4
			hours = 1
		}
	}
}

STP_ch_prepare_protectorate = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_ch_current = yes
			NOD = {
				exists = yes
				is_subject = no
			}
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = STP_ch_subject_pending
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = STP_ch_subject_pending
		STP_ch_close_pressure = yes
		clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_invasion_active
		STP_pw_party_close_northern_operations = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_war_with = NOD
			}
			white_peace = NOD
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_faction = yes
				NOT = {
					is_in_faction_with = NOD
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_faction_leader = yes
				}
				dismantle_faction = yes
			}
			else = {
				faction_leader = {
					remove_from_faction = STP
				}
			}
		}
		country_event = {
			id = ADISCORD_STP_ch.5
			days = 1
		}
	}
}

STP_ch_finalize_protectorate = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = STP_ch_subject_pending
		}
		clr_country_flag = STP_ch_subject_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				STP_ch_current = yes
				is_subject = no
				NOD = {
					exists = yes
					is_subject = no
				}
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = NOD
				}
			}
			NOD = {
				set_autonomy = {
					target = STP
					autonomy_state = autonomy_puppet
					end_wars = no
					end_civil_wars = no
				}
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			}
			STP_ch_enter_protectorate = yes
		}
	}
}

STP_ch_reconcile = {
	STP_ch_initialize = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_ch_current = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_subject_of = NOD
				NOT = {
					check_variable = {
						var = STP_ch_phase
						value = 2
						compare = equals
					}
				}
			}
			STP_ch_enter_protectorate = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_phase
					value = 2
					compare = equals
				}
				is_subject = no
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = NOD
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = STP_ch_subject_pending
				}
				NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ch_liberation_war }
			}
			STP_ch_open_congress = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				STP_ch_cold = yes
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = STP_ch_subject_pending
				}
				NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ch_liberation_war }
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_subject_pending
				}
				OR = {
					NOD = {
						exists = no
					}
					NOD = {
						is_subject = yes
					}
					has_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_compact
					has_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_invasion_defeated
				}
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = NOD
				}
			}
			STP_ch_open_congress = yes
		}
	}
}

STP_ch_close_ballot = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_ch_current = yes
			check_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_phase
				value = 3
				compare = equals
			}
			is_subject = no
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = STP_ch_ballot_closed
			}
		}
		STP_ch_recount = yes
		set_country_flag = STP_ch_ballot_closed
		country_event = {
			id = ADISCORD_STP_ch.6
			hours = 1
		}
	}
}

STP_ch_elect_1 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_ch_candidate_1_wins = yes
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_government
			value = 1
		}
		remove_ideas = { STP_pw_party_high_houses_government STP_pw_party_unbound_government }
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = hedonism
			elections_allowed = no
		}
		STP_ch_leader_1 = yes
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_phase
			value = 4
		}
		country_event = {
			id = ADISCORD_STP_ch.1
			hours = 1
		}
		STP_ch_refresh = yes
	}
}

STP_ch_begin_crisis = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_ch_governing = yes
			check_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_phase
				value = 5
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = STP_ch_army_crisis
		clr_country_flag = STP_ch_north_crisis
		clr_country_flag = STP_ch_trade_crisis
		clr_country_flag = STP_ch_security_crisis
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = STP_ch_security_powers
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = STP_ch_security_reviewed
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = STP_ch_security_crisis
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = STP_ch_emergency_powers
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = STP_ch_civilian_control
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = STP_ch_army_crisis
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = STP_ch_north_breach
				NOD = {
					is_subject_of = STP
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = STP_ch_north_crisis
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = STP_ch_supply_strain
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = STP_ch_supply_buffer
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = STP_ch_fulfilled_merchants
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = STP_ch_trade_crisis
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_phase
			value = 6
		}
		country_event = {
			id = ADISCORD_STP_ch.1
			hours = 1
		}
		STP_ch_refresh = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				STP_ch_no_crisis = yes
			}
			STP_ch_finish_crisis = yes
		}
		else = {
			activate_mission = STP_ch_crisis_deadline
			country_event = {
				id = ADISCORD_STP_ch.9
				hours = 1
			}
		}
	}
}

STP_ch_finish_crisis = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_ch_current = yes
			check_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_phase
				value = 6
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_phase
			value = 7
		}
		remove_mission = STP_ch_crisis_deadline
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = STP_ch_army_crisis
			}
			set_country_flag = STP_ch_military_veto
			add_stability = -0.12
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = STP_ch_north_crisis
				NOD = {
					is_subject_of = STP
				}
			}
			set_autonomy = {
				target = NOD
				autonomy_state = autonomy_free
				end_wars = no
				end_civil_wars = no
			}
			NOD = {
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			}
			add_stability = -0.08
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = STP_ch_trade_crisis
			}
			add_timed_idea = {
				idea = STP_ch_supply_crisis
				days = 180
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_pledge_conservatives
					value = STP_ch_government
					compare = equals
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = STP_ch_fulfilled_conservatives
				}
			}
			subtract_from_variable = {
				var = STP_pf_conservatives_support
				value = 15
			}
			set_country_flag = STP_ch_broken_conservatives
			add_stability = -0.03
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_pledge_borons
					value = STP_ch_government
					compare = equals
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = STP_ch_fulfilled_borons
				}
			}
			subtract_from_variable = {
				var = STP_pf_borons_support
				value = 15
			}
			set_country_flag = STP_ch_broken_borons
			add_stability = -0.03
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_pledge_security
					value = STP_ch_government
					compare = equals
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = STP_ch_fulfilled_security
				}
			}
			subtract_from_variable = {
				var = STP_pf_security_support
				value = 15
			}
			set_country_flag = STP_ch_broken_security
			add_stability = -0.03
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_pledge_army
					value = STP_ch_government
					compare = equals
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = STP_ch_fulfilled_army
				}
			}
			subtract_from_variable = {
				var = STP_pf_army_support
				value = 15
			}
			set_country_flag = STP_ch_broken_army
			add_stability = -0.03
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_pledge_advisers
					value = STP_ch_government
					compare = equals
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = STP_ch_fulfilled_advisers
				}
			}
			subtract_from_variable = {
				var = STP_pf_advisers_support
				value = 15
			}
			set_country_flag = STP_ch_broken_advisers
			add_stability = -0.03
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_pledge_merchants
					value = STP_ch_government
					compare = equals
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = STP_ch_fulfilled_merchants
				}
			}
			subtract_from_variable = {
				var = STP_pf_merchants_support
				value = 15
			}
			set_country_flag = STP_ch_broken_merchants
			add_stability = -0.03
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_pledge_radicals
					value = STP_ch_government
					compare = equals
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = STP_ch_fulfilled_radicals
				}
			}
			subtract_from_variable = {
				var = STP_pf_radicals_support
				value = 15
			}
			set_country_flag = STP_ch_broken_radicals
			add_stability = -0.03
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = STP_ch_security_crisis
			}
			set_country_flag = STP_ch_security_veto
			add_stability = -0.08
		}
		STP_refresh_apparatus_loyalty = yes
		STP_ch_refresh = yes
		country_event = {
			id = ADISCORD_STP_ch.1
			hours = 1
		}
		country_event = {
			id = ADISCORD_STP_ch.10
			hours = 1
		}
	}
}

STP_ch_accept_release = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_ch_release_offer_current = yes
		}
		set_autonomy = {
			target = STP
			autonomy_state = autonomy_free
			end_wars = no
			end_civil_wars = no
		}
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		STP = {
			clr_country_flag = STP_ch_release_pending
			set_country_flag = STP_ch_liberated
			STP_ch_open_congress = yes
		}
	}
}

STP_ch_refuse_release = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = STP_ch_release_pending
		}
		clr_country_flag = STP_ch_release_pending
		country_event = {
			id = ADISCORD_STP_ch.8
			hours = 1
		}
	}
}

STP_ch_begin_liberation_war = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_ch_protectorate = yes
			STP_ch_liberation_window = yes
		}
		NOD = {
			set_autonomy = {
				target = STP
				autonomy_state = autonomy_free
				end_wars = no
				end_civil_wars = no
			}
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		clr_country_flag = STP_ch_release_pending
		set_country_flag = STP_ch_liberation_war
		set_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_invasion_active
		STP_pw_party_break_nod_alliance = yes
		country_event = {
			id = ADISCORD_STP_ch.12
			days = 1
		}
	}
}

STP_ch_launch_liberation_war = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_ch_current = yes
			has_country_flag = STP_ch_liberation_war
			is_subject = no
			NOD = {
				exists = yes
				is_subject = no
				has_capitulated = no
			}
			NOT = {
				has_war_with = NOD
			}
		}
		declare_war_on = { target = NOD type = puppet_wargoal_focus }
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				STP_ch_current = yes
				has_country_flag = STP_ch_liberation_war
				is_subject = no
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = NOD
				}
			}
			clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_invasion_active
			clr_country_flag = STP_ch_liberation_war
			STP_ch_open_congress = yes
		}
	}
}

STP_ch_train_army_finish = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = STP_ch_train_army_deposit
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				STP_ch_current = yes
				has_capitulated = no
			}
			clear_variable = STP_ch_train_army_deposit
			set_country_flag = STP_ch_army_institution
			set_temp_variable = {
				var = STP_pf_selected
				value = 4
			}
			STP_pf_shift = yes
			STP_ch_refresh = yes
		}
		else = {
			add_to_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury
				value = STP_ch_train_army_deposit
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income
				value = STP_ch_train_army_deposit
			}
			clear_variable = STP_ch_train_army_deposit
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
	}
}

STP_ch_train_army_refund = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = STP_ch_train_army_deposit
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury
			value = STP_ch_train_army_deposit
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income
			value = STP_ch_train_army_deposit
		}
		clear_variable = STP_ch_train_army_deposit
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

STP_ch_clear = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = STP_ch_phase
		}
		# Cancellation callbacks must no longer see an active chapter.
		clear_variable = STP_ch_phase
		clear_variable = STP_ch_resume_phase
		clr_country_flag = STP_ch_refresh_pending
		STP_ch_train_army_refund = yes
		STP_ch_train_economy_refund = yes
		STP_ch_train_party_refund = yes
		clear_variable = STP_ch_crisis_paused_days
		clr_country_flag = STP_ch_protectorate_leader
		STP_ch_close_pressure = yes
		remove_mission = STP_ch_crisis_deadline
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dynamic_modifier = {
					modifier = STP_ch_institutions
				}
			}
			remove_dynamic_modifier = {
				modifier = STP_ch_institutions
			}
		}
		remove_ideas = STP_ch_supply_crisis
		clr_country_flag = STP_ch_release_pending
		clr_country_flag = STP_ch_aid_pending
		clr_country_flag = STP_ch_subject_pending
		clr_country_flag = STP_ch_liberation_war
	}
}

STP_ch_accept_aid = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_ch_aid_offer_current = yes
			STP_ch_aid_affordable = yes
		}
		STP = {
			clr_country_flag = STP_ch_aid_pending
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = -6000 }
		subtract_from_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury
			value = 450
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs
			value = 450
		}
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		STP = {
			clr_country_flag = STP_ch_aid_pending
			set_country_flag = STP_ch_aid_received
			set_country_flag = STP_ch_army_access
			set_country_flag = STP_ch_economy_access
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 6000 producer = NOD }
			add_to_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury
				value = 450
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income
				value = 450
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				var = STP_pf_selected
				value = 5
			}
			STP_pf_shift = yes
			STP_ch_refresh = yes
		}
	}
}

STP_ch_pause_crisis = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_ch_current = yes
			check_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_phase
				value = 6
				compare = equals
			}
			NOT = {
				has_variable = STP_ch_crisis_paused_days
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_crisis_paused_days
			value = days_mission_timeout@STP_ch_crisis_deadline
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_crisis_paused_days
			min = 1
			max = 90
		}
	}
}
STP_ch_resume_crisis = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_ch_governing = yes
			check_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_phase
				value = 6
				compare = equals
			}
			has_variable = STP_ch_crisis_paused_days
		}
		activate_mission = STP_ch_crisis_deadline
		set_temp_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_crisis_days_delta
			value = STP_ch_crisis_paused_days
		}
		subtract_from_temp_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_crisis_days_delta
			value = 90
		}
		add_days_mission_timeout = {
			mission = STP_ch_crisis_deadline
			days = STP_ch_crisis_days_delta
		}
		clear_variable = STP_ch_crisis_paused_days
	}
}


# Read completed-focus markers after the reward transaction, once per mutation batch.
STP_ch_schedule_refresh = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_ch_current = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ch_refresh_pending }
		}
		set_country_flag = STP_ch_refresh_pending
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_ch.1 hours = 1 }
	}
}

# Choose once from the inherited delegates; later coalition agreements keep this nominee.
STP_ch_select_ai_nominee = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = yes
			check_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_nominee
				value = 0
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_nominee
			value = 1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_votes_2
					value = STP_ch_votes_1
					compare = greater_than
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_votes_2
					value = STP_ch_votes_3
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_votes_2
					value = STP_ch_votes_4
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_votes_2
					value = STP_ch_votes_5
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_nominee
				value = 2
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_votes_3
					value = STP_ch_votes_1
					compare = greater_than
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_votes_3
					value = STP_ch_votes_2
					compare = greater_than
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_votes_3
					value = STP_ch_votes_4
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_votes_3
					value = STP_ch_votes_5
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_nominee
				value = 3
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_votes_4
					value = STP_ch_votes_1
					compare = greater_than
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_votes_4
					value = STP_ch_votes_2
					compare = greater_than
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_votes_4
					value = STP_ch_votes_3
					compare = greater_than
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_votes_4
					value = STP_ch_votes_5
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_nominee
				value = 4
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_votes_5
					value = STP_ch_votes_1
					compare = greater_than
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_votes_5
					value = STP_ch_votes_2
					compare = greater_than
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_votes_5
					value = STP_ch_votes_3
					compare = greater_than
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = STP_ch_votes_5
					value = STP_ch_votes_4
					compare = greater_than
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_nominee
				value = 5
			}
		}
	}
}


STP_ch_leader_1 = {
	# The High Houses' civil-war victory gives their presidium a named chairman.
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pv_outcome value = 1 compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_leader = { character = STP_Cyan_Crowe } } }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = STP_Cyan_Crowe
				country_leader = {
					ideology = aristocratic_hedonism
					expire = "2300.1.1"
				}
			}
		}
		promote_character = {
			character = STP_Cyan_Crowe
			ideology = aristocratic_hedonism
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_country_leader = {
			name = STP_ch_leader_1
			desc = STP_ch_leader_1_desc
			ideology = aristocratic_hedonism
			expire = "2300.1.1"
		}
		set_country_leader_portrait = {
			ideology = hedonism
			portrait = GFX_portrait_STP_Conservative_Presidium
		}
	}
}

STP_ch_leader_2 = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_character = STP_rufus_hedersett }
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = STP_rufus_hedersett
			promote_leader = yes
			country_leader = {
				ideology = hedonism_ideology
				expire = "2300.1.1"
			}
		}
		promote_character = {
			character = STP_rufus_hedersett
			ideology = hedonism_ideology
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_country_leader = {
			name = STP_ch_leader_2
			desc = STP_ch_leader_2_desc
			ideology = hedonism_ideology
			expire = "2300.1.1"
		}
	}
	# Hedersett's civil-war victory keeps the image he chose during the war.
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = STP_pv_outcome value = 2 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain value = 60 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_country_leader_portrait = {
			ideology = hedonism
			portrait = GFX_portrait_STP_Rufus_Hedersett_marshal
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = STP_pv_outcome value = 2 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_wins value = 2 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_country_leader_portrait = {
			ideology = hedonism
			portrait = GFX_portrait_STP_Rufus_Hedersett_uniform
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_country_leader_portrait = {
			ideology = hedonism
			portrait = GFX_portrait_STP_Rufus_Hedersett
		}
	}
}

STP_ch_elect_4 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_ch_candidate_4_wins = yes
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_government
			value = 4
		}
		remove_ideas = {
			STP_pw_party_high_houses_government
			STP_pw_party_unbound_government
		}
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = hedonism
			elections_allowed = no
		}
		STP_ch_leader_4 = yes
		set_variable = {
			var = STP_ch_phase
			value = 4
		}
		country_event = {
			id = ADISCORD_STP_ch.1
			hours = 1
		}
		STP_ch_refresh = yes
	}
}

STP_ch_leader_5 = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_leader = { character = STP_Vera_Korvina } } }
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = STP_Vera_Korvina
			country_leader = {
				ideology = hedonism_ideology
				expire = "2300.1.1"
			}
		}
	}
	promote_character = {
		character = STP_Vera_Korvina
		ideology = hedonism_ideology
	}
}

STP_ch_elect_5 = {

		limit = {

			var = STP_ch_government

			elections_allowed = no

			hours = 1
		}

}

STP_ch_restore_independent_leader = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_government
				value = 1
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		STP_ch_leader_1 = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_government
				value = 2
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character = STP_rufus_hedersett
			}
			promote_character = {
				character = STP_rufus_hedersett
				ideology = hedonism_ideology
			}
		}
		else = {
			STP_ch_leader_2 = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_government
				value = 3
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character = STP_August_Veil
			}
			promote_character = {
				character = STP_August_Veil
				ideology = hedonism_ideology
			}
		}
		else = {
			STP_ch_leader_3 = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_government
				value = 4
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		STP_ch_leader_4 = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = STP_ch_government
				value = 5
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		STP_ch_leader_5 = yes
	}
	else = {
		STP_ch_leader_1 = yes
	}
}

STP_ch_install_protectorate_leader = {
	set_politics = {
		ruling_party = hedonism
		elections_allowed = no
	}
	STP_ch_leader_6 = yes
	set_country_flag = STP_ch_protectorate_leader
}

# COUNTRY STP: one announced plot at a time; the opening delay belongs to this war.
STP_ps_tick_sabotage = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_ps_sabotage_war = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_ps_plots_started } }
			set_variable = { var = STP_ps_plots_started value = 0 }
			# The quiet service's prewar lists delay the first announced plot.
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ph_heir value = 5 compare = equals } }
				set_country_flag = { flag = STP_ps_plot_cooldown value = 1 days = 49 }
			}
			else = {
				set_country_flag = { flag = STP_ps_plot_cooldown value = 1 days = 21 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_variable = STP_ps_plot_kind }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_plot_cooldown }
				check_variable = { var = STP_ps_plots_started value = 3 compare = less_than }
			}
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_ps_plots_started value = 1 }
			set_variable = { var = STP_ps_plot_kind value = STP_ps_plots_started }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_plot_kind value = 1 compare = equals } } activate_mission = STP_ps_plot_1 }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_plot_kind value = 2 compare = equals } } activate_mission = STP_ps_plot_2 }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_plot_kind value = 3 compare = equals } } activate_mission = STP_ps_plot_3 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.207 hours = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { OR = { has_variable = STP_ps_plot_kind has_variable = STP_ps_plot_receipt } }
		STP_ps_plot_close = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { STP_ps_war_active = no }
		remove_ideas = STP_ps_supply_disrupted
		remove_ideas = STP_ps_false_orders
		remove_ideas = STP_ps_factory_strike
	}
}

STP_ps_plot_refund = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_plot_receipt }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_plot_refund_kind value = STP_ps_plot_receipt }
		clear_variable = STP_ps_plot_receipt
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_plot_refund_kind value = 1 compare = equals } }
			add_political_power = 10
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 300 }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 300 }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_plot_refund_kind value = 2 compare = equals } }
			add_political_power = 15
			add_command_power = 10
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_plot_refund_kind value = 3 compare = equals } }
			add_political_power = 10
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 450 }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 450 }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
	}
}

STP_ps_plot_close = {
	STP_ps_plot_refund = yes
	clear_variable = STP_ps_plot_kind
	set_country_flag = { flag = STP_ps_plot_cooldown value = 1 days = 35 }
	remove_mission = STP_ps_plot_1
	remove_mission = STP_ps_plot_2
	remove_mission = STP_ps_plot_3
}

STP_ps_plot_counter_finish = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_ps_plot_current = yes
			has_variable = STP_ps_plot_receipt
			check_variable = { var = STP_ps_plot_receipt value = STP_ps_plot_kind compare = equals }
		}
		clear_variable = STP_ps_plot_receipt
		STP_ps_plot_close = yes
	}
	else = { STP_ps_plot_refund = yes }
}

STP_ps_plot_timeout = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_ps_plot_current = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_plot_kind value = 1 compare = equals } }
			add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_ps_supply_disrupted days = 21 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_plot_kind value = 2 compare = equals } }
			add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_ps_false_orders days = 21 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_plot_kind value = 3 compare = equals } }
			add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_ps_factory_strike days = 21 }
			add_stability = -0.02
		}
	}
	STP_ps_plot_close = yes
}

# ============================================================
# PARTY WAR: INHERITANCE OF THE FESTIVAL
# ============================================================
# COUNTRY STP. The party's own civil war carries a strain gauge, bloc crises,
# three timed front crises and six claims on the victory. The claims decide
# who takes power when Shabrat is defeated; the congress later reads the
# same outcome as the winner's mandate.

# One-time start at the first defence tick of the party war.
STP_pv_begin_war = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = STP
			STP_ps_war_active = yes
			NOT = { has_variable = STP_pv_strain }
		}
		set_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain value = 15 }
		# The civil-war tree replaces the preparation tree without its completions;
		# prewar succession choices are read from their persistent markers.
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ph_heir value = 1 compare = equals } }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain value = 10 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ph_heir_settlement value = 1 compare = equals } }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain value = 5 }
		}
		set_variable = { var = STP_pv_war_months value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_wins value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_stage value = 0 }
		STP_pv_refresh_strain = yes
		STP_pv_refresh_claims = yes
	}
}

# Fixed-point variables keep three decimals: scale before dividing.
STP_pv_refresh_strain = {
	clamp_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain min = 0 max = 100 }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain_political_power value = STP_pv_strain }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain_political_power value = -3 }
	divide_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain_political_power value = 1000 }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain_stability value = STP_pv_strain }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain_stability value = -12 }
	divide_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain_stability value = 10000 }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain_income value = STP_pv_strain }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain_income value = -3 }
	divide_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain_income value = 1000 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_ps_war_active = yes
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_pv_strain_dynamic } }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_pv_strain_dynamic }
	}
	force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

# Input: temporary STP_pv_strain_change.
STP_pv_change_strain = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_pv_war_current = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain value = STP_pv_strain_change }
		STP_pv_refresh_strain = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain_change value = 0 }
}

STP_pv_monthly = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_pv_war_current = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_pv_war_months value = 1 }
		# The AI party receives a lighter baseline so its campaign stays viable.
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain_change value = 4 }
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain_change value = 3 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_node_lost_145 }
			add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain_change value = 4 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_node_lost_16366 }
			add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain_change value = 3 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_stage value = 4 compare = equals } }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain_change value = 3 }
		}
		STP_pv_change_strain = yes
		STP_pv_check_strain_thresholds = yes
		STP_pv_check_bloc_crisis = yes
		STP_pv_schedule_front = yes
		if = {
			limit = { STP_pv_kefreyt_offer_possible = yes }
			set_country_flag = STP_pv_kefreyt_offer_seen
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pv.40 days = 3 }
		}
		STP_pv_refresh_claims = yes
	}
}

STP_pv_check_strain_thresholds = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain value = 100 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pv.12 hours = 6 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain value = 70 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_pv_capital_flight_seen }
		}
		set_country_flag = STP_pv_capital_flight_seen
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pv.11 hours = 6 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain value = 40 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_pv_empty_resorts_seen }
		}
		set_country_flag = STP_pv_empty_resorts_seen
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pv.10 hours = 6 }
	}
}

# At most one bloc crisis per 45 days; each bloc confronts the party once per war.
STP_pv_check_bloc_crisis = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = STP_pf_initialized
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_pv_bloc_crisis_cooldown }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_support value = 30 compare = less_than }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_pv_crisis_army_seen }
			}
			set_country_flag = STP_pv_crisis_army_seen
			set_country_flag = { flag = STP_pv_bloc_crisis_cooldown value = 1 days = 45 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pv.20 hours = 12 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = STP_pf_security_support value = 30 compare = less_than }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_pv_crisis_security_seen }
			}
			set_country_flag = STP_pv_crisis_security_seen
			set_country_flag = { flag = STP_pv_bloc_crisis_cooldown value = 1 days = 45 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pv.21 hours = 12 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = STP_pf_merchants_support value = 30 compare = less_than }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_pv_crisis_merchants_seen }
			}
			set_country_flag = STP_pv_crisis_merchants_seen
			set_country_flag = { flag = STP_pv_bloc_crisis_cooldown value = 1 days = 45 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pv.22 hours = 12 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = STP_pf_radicals_support value = 30 compare = less_than }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_pv_crisis_radicals_seen }
			}
			set_country_flag = STP_pv_crisis_radicals_seen
			set_country_flag = { flag = STP_pv_bloc_crisis_cooldown value = 1 days = 45 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pv.23 hours = 12 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_support value = 30 compare = less_than }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_pv_crisis_conservatives_seen }
			}
			set_country_flag = STP_pv_crisis_conservatives_seen
			set_country_flag = { flag = STP_pv_bloc_crisis_cooldown value = 1 days = 45 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pv.24 hours = 12 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = STP_pf_borons_support value = 30 compare = less_than }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_pv_crisis_borons_seen }
			}
			set_country_flag = STP_pv_crisis_borons_seen
			set_country_flag = { flag = STP_pv_bloc_crisis_cooldown value = 1 days = 45 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pv.25 hours = 12 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				STP_pf_nod_contacts = yes
				check_variable = { var = STP_pf_advisers_support value = 30 compare = less_than }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_pv_crisis_advisers_seen }
			}
			set_country_flag = STP_pv_crisis_advisers_seen
			set_country_flag = { flag = STP_pv_bloc_crisis_cooldown value = 1 days = 45 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pv.26 hours = 12 }
		}
	}
}

# Eight heartland districts contested by the party and the resistance.
# Explicit state IDs keep the count country-scoped instead of scanning the map.
STP_pv_count_heartland = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_heartland value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { 1 = { is_controlled_by = STP } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_heartland value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { 2 = { is_controlled_by = STP } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_heartland value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { 3 = { is_controlled_by = STP } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_heartland value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { 28 = { is_controlled_by = STP } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_heartland value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { 29 = { is_controlled_by = STP } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_heartland value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { 45 = { is_controlled_by = STP } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_heartland value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { 46 = { is_controlled_by = STP } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_heartland value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { 53 = { is_controlled_by = STP } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_heartland value = 1 }
	}
}

# Crises start in order after one, three and five months of war.
STP_pv_schedule_front = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_pv_front_active = no }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_stage value = 0 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = STP_pv_war_months value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			STP_pv_start_front = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_stage value = 1 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = STP_pv_war_months value = 3 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			STP_pv_start_front = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_stage value = 2 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = STP_pv_war_months value = 5 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			STP_pv_start_front = yes
		}
	}
}

# The target is fixed at the crisis start: hold the line, then gain one and two districts.
STP_pv_start_front = {
	STP_pv_count_heartland = yes
	add_to_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_stage value = 1 }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_target value = STP_pv_heartland }
	clear_variable = STP_pv_front_result
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_stage value = 1 compare = equals } }
		activate_mission = STP_pv_front_1
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_stage value = 2 compare = equals } }
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_target value = 1 }
		activate_mission = STP_pv_front_2
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_target value = 2 }
		activate_mission = STP_pv_front_3
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_target min = 1 max = 8 }
	country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pv.30 hours = 2 }
}

# Mission timeout. The result report only reads the settled variables.
STP_pv_resolve_front = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_pv_war_current = yes }
		STP_pv_count_heartland = yes
		set_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_result value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_stage value = 1 compare = equals }
				controls_province = 145
				controls_province = 16366
				check_variable = { var = STP_pv_heartland value = STP_pv_front_target compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_result value = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_stage value = 2 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = STP_pv_heartland value = STP_pv_front_target compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_result value = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_stage value = 3 compare = equals }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = STP_pv_heartland value = STP_pv_front_target compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					1 = { is_controlled_by = STP }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_result value = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_result value = 1 compare = equals } }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_wins value = 1 }
			STP_pv_front_success = yes
		}
		else = {
			STP_pv_front_failure = yes
		}
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pv.31 hours = 1 }
		STP_pv_refresh_claims = yes
	}
}

STP_pv_front_success = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_stage value = 1 compare = equals } }
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_support value = 10 }
		add_war_support = 0.03
		add_command_power = 20
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain_change value = -8 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_stage value = 2 compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pf_selected value = 4 }
		STP_pf_shift = yes
		add_political_power = 40
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain_change value = -8 }
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_support value = 10 }
		army_experience = 20
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain_change value = -12 }
	}
	STP_refresh_apparatus_loyalty = yes
	STP_pv_change_strain = yes
}

STP_pv_front_failure = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_stage value = 1 compare = equals } }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pf_radicals_support value = 8 }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_support value = 8 }
		add_stability = -0.05
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain_change value = 12 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_stage value = 2 compare = equals } }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_support value = 12 }
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_security_support value = 8 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain_change value = 10 }
	}
	else = {
		subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_support value = 10 }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pf_merchants_support value = 8 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain_change value = 15 }
	}
	STP_refresh_apparatus_loyalty = yes
	STP_pv_change_strain = yes
}

# Claims are display caches during the war and the authoritative input at victory.
# 1 Crowe and the High Houses, 2 Hedersett, 3 Veil, 4 Lanskoy, 5 Korvina, 6 Renner.
STP_pv_refresh_claims = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = STP_pf_initialized }
		# District borons back the Chamber only in part; their weight is halved.
		set_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_1 value = STP_pf_conservatives_contribution }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_borons_credit value = STP_pf_borons_contribution }
		divide_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_borons_credit value = 2 }
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_1 value = STP_pv_borons_credit }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ph_heir value = 1 compare = equals } }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_1 value = 8 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = STP_ph_heir_signed }
				add_to_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_1 value = 6 }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_2 value = STP_pf_radicals_contribution }
		# Hedersett already leads the wartime government.
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_2 value = 10 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = STP_pv_glory_festival }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_2 value = 12 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ph_heir_settlement value = 2 compare = equals } }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_2 value = 10 }
		}
		set_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_3 value = STP_pf_army_contribution }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_credit value = STP_pv_front_wins }
		multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_credit value = 5 }
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_3 value = STP_pv_front_credit }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ph_heir value = 3 compare = equals } }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_3 value = 8 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = STP_ph_heir_signed }
				add_to_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_3 value = 6 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = STP_pv_glory_staff }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_3 value = 12 }
		}
		set_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_4 value = STP_pf_merchants_contribution }
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = STP_pv_glory_wallets }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_4 value = 12 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain value = 70 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_4 value = 10 }
		}
		set_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_5 value = STP_pf_security_contribution }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ph_heir value = 5 compare = equals } }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_5 value = 8 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = STP_ph_heir_signed }
				add_to_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_5 value = 6 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = STP_pv_glory_silence }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_5 value = 12 }
		}
		set_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_6 value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { STP_pv_advisers_have_voice = yes }
			set_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_6 value = STP_pf_advisers_contribution }
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = STP_pv_northern_loan_taken }
				add_to_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_6 value = 6 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOD = { has_country_flag = NOD_cw_victorious } }
				add_to_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_6 value = 20 }
			}
		}
		clamp_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_1 min = 0 max = 100 }
		clamp_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_2 min = 0 max = 100 }
		clamp_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_3 min = 0 max = 100 }
		clamp_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_4 min = 0 max = 100 }
		clamp_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_5 min = 0 max = 100 }
		clamp_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_6 min = 0 max = 100 }
		# Ties keep the incumbent, then the generals, the service, the Houses, the money and the patron.
		set_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_lead value = 2 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_best_claim value = STP_pv_claim_2 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_3 value = STP_pv_best_claim compare = greater_than } }
			set_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_lead value = 3 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_best_claim value = STP_pv_claim_3 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_5 value = STP_pv_best_claim compare = greater_than } }
			set_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_lead value = 5 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_best_claim value = STP_pv_claim_5 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_1 value = STP_pv_best_claim compare = greater_than } }
			set_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_lead value = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_best_claim value = STP_pv_claim_1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_4 value = STP_pv_best_claim compare = greater_than } }
			set_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_lead value = 4 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pv_best_claim value = STP_pv_claim_4 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_6 value = STP_pv_best_claim compare = greater_than } }
			set_variable = { var = STP_pv_claim_lead value = 6 }
		}
	}
}

# Winner scope STP. Runs once, before ADISCORD_STP_cw.71 queues the super-event.
STP_pv_resolve_victory = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = STP
			has_variable = STP_pv_strain
			NOT = { has_variable = STP_pv_outcome }
		}
		# A crisis still open at the capitulation counts as won by the victory itself.
		if = {
			limit = { STP_pv_front_active = yes }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_pv_front_wins value = 1 }
		}
		remove_mission = STP_pv_front_1
		remove_mission = STP_pv_front_2
		remove_mission = STP_pv_front_3
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = STP_pf_initialized }
			STP_refresh_apparatus_loyalty = yes
		}
		STP_pv_refresh_claims = yes
		set_variable = { var = STP_pv_outcome value = STP_pv_claim_lead }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_pf_initialized } }
			set_variable = { var = STP_pv_outcome value = 2 }
		}
		STP_pv_install_victor = yes
		STP_pv_apply_victory_terms = yes
		STP_pv_close_war = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pv.50 hours = 2 }
	}
}

STP_pv_install_victor = {
	set_politics = {
		ruling_party = hedonism
		elections_allowed = no
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pv_outcome value = 1 compare = equals } }
		STP_ch_leader_1 = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pv_outcome value = 3 compare = equals } }
		STP_ch_leader_3 = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pv_outcome value = 4 compare = equals } }
		STP_ch_leader_4 = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pv_outcome value = 5 compare = equals } }
		STP_ch_leader_5 = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pv_outcome value = 6 compare = equals } }
		STP_ch_leader_6 = yes
	}
	else = {
		STP_ch_leader_2 = yes
	}
}

STP_pv_apply_victory_terms = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pv_outcome value = 1 compare = equals } }
		add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_pv_outcome_houses days = 540 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pv_outcome value = 3 compare = equals } }
		add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_pv_outcome_staff days = 540 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pv_outcome value = 4 compare = equals } }
		add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_pv_outcome_wallets days = 540 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pv_outcome value = 5 compare = equals } }
		add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_pv_outcome_silence days = 540 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pv_outcome value = 6 compare = equals } }
		add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_pv_outcome_regent days = 540 }
	}
	else = {
		add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_pv_outcome_festival days = 540 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain value = 80 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_pv_pyrrhic_victory days = 365 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = STP_pv_strain value = 40 compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { var = STP_apparatus_loyalty value = 60 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_pv_clean_victory days = 365 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = STP_pv_kefreyt_debt }
		add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_pv_kefreyt_bill days = 365 }
	}
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

# The final strain, claims and outcome stay as the war record; wartime transients close.
STP_pv_close_war = {
	remove_mission = STP_pv_front_1
	remove_mission = STP_pv_front_2
	remove_mission = STP_pv_front_3
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_pv_strain_dynamic } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_pv_strain_dynamic }
	}
	clr_country_flag = STP_pv_bloc_crisis_cooldown
	clr_country_flag = STP_pv_crisis_army_seen
	clr_country_flag = STP_pv_crisis_security_seen
	clr_country_flag = STP_pv_crisis_merchants_seen
	clr_country_flag = STP_pv_crisis_radicals_seen
	clr_country_flag = STP_pv_crisis_conservatives_seen
	clr_country_flag = STP_pv_crisis_borons_seen
	clr_country_flag = STP_pv_crisis_advisers_seen
	clr_country_flag = STP_pv_empty_resorts_seen
	clr_country_flag = STP_pv_capital_flight_seen
	clr_country_flag = STP_pv_kefreyt_offer_seen
	clr_country_flag = STP_pv_kefreyt_debt
	clr_country_flag = STP_pv_northern_loan_taken
	clr_country_flag = STP_ph_heir_signed
	clear_variable = STP_ph_heir
	clear_variable = STP_ph_heir_settlement
	clear_variable = STP_pv_front_target
	clear_variable = STP_pv_front_result
	clear_variable = STP_pv_front_stage
	clear_variable = STP_pv_war_months
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

# ============================================================
# RESISTANCE WAR: MANDATE OF THE MOUNTAINS
# ============================================================
# COUNTRY STS. Shabrat's war runs on a popular mandate (0-100) and a
# three-pillar coalition: officers, mountain councils and the underground
# press. The mandate scales stability, war support and conscription; neglected
# pillars raise crises; the chosen coalition course shapes the victory.

# One-time start at the first weekly tick of the war with the party.
STP_hw_begin_war = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = STS
			has_war_with = STP
			NOT = { has_variable = STP_hw_mandate }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_postwar }
		}
		set_variable = { var = STP_hw_mandate value = 50 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_hw_officers value = 50 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_hw_councils value = 50 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_hw_underground value = 50 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_hw_war_months value = 0 }
		# Prewar preparation carries into the coalition through the party's own flags.
		if = {
			limit = { STP = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_archives_used } }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_hw_mandate value = 5 }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_hw_underground value = 10 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { STP = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_officers_ready } }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_hw_officers value = 10 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { STP = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_young_officers_ready } }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_hw_officers value = 5 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { STP = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_shabrat_election_victory } }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_hw_mandate value = 10 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { STP = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_limited_party_revolt } }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_hw_mandate value = 5 }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_hw_councils value = 5 }
		}
		STP_hw_refresh = yes
		if = { limit = { is_ai = no } country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_hw.1 days = 2 } }
	}
}

# Fixed-point variables keep three decimals: scale before dividing.
STP_hw_refresh = {
	clamp_variable = { var = STP_hw_mandate min = 0 max = 100 }
	clamp_variable = { var = STP_hw_officers min = 0 max = 100 }
	clamp_variable = { var = STP_hw_councils min = 0 max = 100 }
	clamp_variable = { var = STP_hw_underground min = 0 max = 100 }
	# Mandate 50 is neutral; 0 and 100 give -10%/+10% stability and war support
	# and -15%/+15% conscription.
	set_variable = { var = STP_hw_mandate_stability value = STP_hw_mandate }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_hw_mandate_stability value = 50 }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_hw_mandate_stability value = 2 }
	divide_variable = { var = STP_hw_mandate_stability value = 1000 }
	set_variable = { var = STP_hw_mandate_war_support value = STP_hw_mandate_stability }
	set_variable = { var = STP_hw_mandate_conscription value = STP_hw_mandate }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_hw_mandate_conscription value = 50 }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_hw_mandate_conscription value = 3 }
	divide_variable = { var = STP_hw_mandate_conscription value = 1000 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			STP_hw_war_current = yes
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_hw_mandate_dynamic } }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_hw_mandate_dynamic }
	}
	force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
}

# Input: temporary STP_hw_mandate_change.
STP_hw_change_mandate = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_hw_war_current = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_hw_mandate value = STP_hw_mandate_change }
		STP_hw_refresh = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_hw_mandate_change value = 0 }
}

STP_hw_monthly = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_hw_war_current = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_hw_war_months value = 1 }
		# A long war wears the mandate down; the AI resistance erodes more slowly.
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_hw_mandate_change value = -2 }
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_hw_mandate_change value = -1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_hw_war_months value = 8 compare = greater_than } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_hw_mandate_change value = -1 }
		}
		# Holding the capital district shows the mountains that the war can be won.
		if = {
			limit = { 28 = { is_controlled_by = STS } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { var = STP_hw_mandate_change value = 2 }
		}
		STP_hw_change_mandate = yes
		STP_hw_check_mandate_thresholds = yes
		STP_hw_check_pillar_crisis = yes
	}
}

STP_hw_check_mandate_thresholds = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = STP_hw_mandate value = 10 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_hw_collapse_cooldown }
		}
		set_country_flag = { flag = STP_hw_collapse_cooldown value = 1 days = 60 }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_hw.11 hours = 6 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = STP_hw_mandate value = 30 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_hw_tired_mountains_seen }
		}
		set_country_flag = STP_hw_tired_mountains_seen
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_hw.10 hours = 6 }
	}
}

# At most one coalition crisis per 45 days; each pillar confronts Shabrat once per war.
STP_hw_check_pillar_crisis = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_hw_crisis_cooldown } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = STP_hw_officers value = 25 compare = less_than }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_hw_crisis_officers_seen }
			}
			set_country_flag = STP_hw_crisis_officers_seen
			set_country_flag = { flag = STP_hw_crisis_cooldown value = 1 days = 45 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_hw.20 hours = 12 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = STP_hw_councils value = 25 compare = less_than }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_hw_crisis_councils_seen }
			}
			set_country_flag = STP_hw_crisis_councils_seen
			set_country_flag = { flag = STP_hw_crisis_cooldown value = 1 days = 45 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_hw.21 hours = 12 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = STP_hw_underground value = 25 compare = less_than }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_hw_crisis_underground_seen }
			}
			set_country_flag = STP_hw_crisis_underground_seen
			set_country_flag = { flag = STP_hw_crisis_cooldown value = 1 days = 45 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_hw.22 hours = 12 }
		}
	}
}

# Front operations feed the mandate: officers claim the wins, the mountains count the losses.
STP_hw_operation_won = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_hw_war_current = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_hw_officers value = 5 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_hw_mandate_change value = 5 }
		STP_hw_change_mandate = yes
	}
}

STP_hw_operation_failed = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_hw_war_current = yes }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_hw_officers value = 5 }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_hw_councils value = 3 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_hw_mandate_change value = -6 }
		STP_hw_change_mandate = yes
	}
}

# Course: 1 officers' directory, 2 council of passes, 3 truth commission.
# Without a chosen course the strongest pillar takes it at the victory.
STP_hw_resolve_victory = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = STS has_variable = STP_hw_mandate }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_hw_course } }
			set_variable = { var = STP_hw_course value = 1 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = STP_hw_councils value = STP_hw_officers compare = greater_than }
					check_variable = { var = STP_hw_councils value = STP_hw_underground compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
				set_variable = { var = STP_hw_course value = 2 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = STP_hw_underground value = STP_hw_officers compare = greater_than }
					check_variable = { var = STP_hw_underground value = STP_hw_councils compare = greater_than }
				}
				set_variable = { var = STP_hw_course value = 3 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_hw_course value = 1 compare = equals } }
			add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_hw_course_staff_idea days = 365 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_hw_course value = 2 compare = equals } }
			add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_hw_course_councils_idea days = 365 }
		}
		else = {
			add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_hw_course_truth_idea days = 365 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_hw_mandate value = 60 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_hw_republic_of_mountains_idea days = 365 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_hw_mandate value = 30 compare = less_than_or_equals } }
			add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_hw_victory_on_credit_idea days = 365 }
		}
		STP_hw_close_war = yes
	}
}

# STP_hw_course stays for the victory report; everything else is wartime state.
STP_hw_close_war = {
	STP_hw_stall_clear = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_hw_mandate_dynamic } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_hw_mandate_dynamic }
	}
	remove_ideas = {
		STP_hw_kefreyt_quartermasters_idea
		STP_hw_mountain_levies_idea
		STP_hw_officers_sulk_idea
	}
	clr_country_flag = STP_hw_crisis_cooldown
	clr_country_flag = STP_hw_collapse_cooldown
	clear_variable = STP_hw_mandate
	clear_variable = STP_hw_officers
	clear_variable = STP_hw_councils
	clear_variable = STP_hw_underground
	clear_variable = STP_hw_war_months
	clear_variable = STP_hw_mandate_stability
	clear_variable = STP_hw_mandate_war_support
	clear_variable = STP_hw_mandate_conscription
}

# COUNTRY STS: begin after the split's owner/controller reconciliation.
# Only districts still held by the party can relieve exhaustion, once each.
STP_hw_stall_begin = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = STS
			has_war_with = STP
			NOT = { has_variable = STP_hw_stall_days }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_postwar }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = STP_cw_union_wars_finished }
		}
		set_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_days value = 0 }
		2 = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_controlled_by = STP }
				set_state_flag = STP_hw_stall_objective
			}
		}
		3 = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_controlled_by = STP }
				set_state_flag = STP_hw_stall_objective
			}
		}
		28 = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_controlled_by = STP }
				set_state_flag = STP_hw_stall_objective
			}
		}
		29 = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_controlled_by = STP }
				set_state_flag = STP_hw_stall_objective
			}
		}
		45 = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_controlled_by = STP }
				set_state_flag = STP_hw_stall_objective
			}
		}
		46 = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_controlled_by = STP }
				set_state_flag = STP_hw_stall_objective
			}
		}
		53 = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_controlled_by = STP }
				set_state_flag = STP_hw_stall_objective
			}
		}
		STP_hw_stall_refresh = yes
	}
}

# One bounded country pulse; modifier refreshes only at a tier boundary.
STP_hw_stall_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_hw_stall_active = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_days value = 210 compare = less_than } }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_days value = 1 }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_remaining value = 1 }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_remaining value = 0 compare = equals } }
				STP_hw_stall_refresh = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = STP_hw_stall_days }
		STP_hw_stall_clear = yes
	}
}

STP_hw_stall_refresh = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_hw_stall_active = yes }
		set_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_stage value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_support value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_recovery value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_mobilization value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_remaining value = 90 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_days value = 210 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_stage value = 3 }
			set_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_support value = -0.15 }
			set_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_recovery value = -0.15 }
			set_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_mobilization value = -0.30 }
			set_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_remaining value = 210 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_days value = 150 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_stage value = 2 }
			set_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_support value = -0.10 }
			set_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_recovery value = -0.10 }
			set_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_mobilization value = -0.15 }
			set_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_remaining value = 210 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_days value = 90 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_stage value = 1 }
			set_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_support value = -0.05 }
			set_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_recovery value = -0.05 }
			set_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_remaining value = 150 }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_remaining value = STP_hw_stall_days }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_stage value = 0 compare = greater_than }
				NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_hw_stall_dynamic } }
			}
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_hw_stall_dynamic }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_stage value = 0 compare = equals }
				has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_hw_stall_dynamic }
			}
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_hw_stall_dynamic }
		}
		force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
	}
}

# STATE scope from on_state_control_changed. Consuming the objective prevents
# deliberate surrender/recapture from buying repeated relief.
STP_hw_stall_capture = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = STP_hw_stall_objective
			is_controlled_by = STS
			STS = { STP_hw_stall_active = yes }
		}
		clr_state_flag = STP_hw_stall_objective
		STS = {
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_days value = 210 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				set_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_days value = 150 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_days value = 150 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				set_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_days value = 90 }
			}
			else = { set_variable = { var = STP_hw_stall_days value = 0 } }
			STP_hw_stall_refresh = yes
		}
	}
}

STP_hw_stall_clear = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_hw_stall_dynamic } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_hw_stall_dynamic }
	}
	clear_variable = STP_hw_stall_days
	clear_variable = STP_hw_stall_stage
	clear_variable = STP_hw_stall_remaining
	clear_variable = STP_hw_stall_support
	clear_variable = STP_hw_stall_recovery
	clear_variable = STP_hw_stall_mobilization
	2 = { clr_state_flag = STP_hw_stall_objective }
	3 = { clr_state_flag = STP_hw_stall_objective }
	28 = { clr_state_flag = STP_hw_stall_objective }
	29 = { clr_state_flag = STP_hw_stall_objective }
	45 = { clr_state_flag = STP_hw_stall_objective }
	46 = { clr_state_flag = STP_hw_stall_objective }
	53 = { clr_state_flag = STP_hw_stall_objective }
}

STP_party_refresh_policies = {
	STP_refresh_apparatus_loyalty = yes
	STP_pw_refresh_modifier = yes
	STP_ch_schedule_refresh = yes
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}


STP_pe_houses_cancel = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = STP_pe_houses_deposit
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury
			value = STP_pe_houses_deposit
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income
			value = STP_pe_houses_deposit
		}
		clear_variable = STP_pe_houses_deposit
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}
STP_pe_houses_settle = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = STP_pe_houses_deposit
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				STP_pe_current = yes
				has_capitulated = no
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = STP_pe_houses_prepared
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = STP_pe_houses_prepared
			remove_ideas = STP_pe_houses_crisis
			add_political_power = 50
		}
		else = {
			add_to_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury
				value = STP_pe_houses_deposit
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income
				value = STP_pe_houses_deposit
			}
		}
		clear_variable = STP_pe_houses_deposit
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}
